Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.10

Chapter 3305
As the Jinwu fought with the humans, the Tai Yiwei and Heavenly Generals in the 12th Pass were watching the show.
They could not participate in such a battle. Furthermore, the Jinwu Clan had taken away so many Jinwu …
After Gui Ling took out these ink balls, the expressions of the people in the 12th Pass turned extremely ugly.
"Activate the array!"
"Everyone, retreat to the Pass!"
"We can't stop that thing!"
Countless Tai Yiwei and Nirvana Realm cultivators ran madly towards the Pass.
Every time a world descended, a rule would be changed …
Fortunately, other than the Taiming Mountain Master and Gui Ling, no other worlds descended.
Swoosh swoosh swoosh …
Dozens of ink balls suddenly appeared above Tian Xun Pass. These ink balls floating in the air suddenly charged downwards. Before the five-star Tai Yiwei could enter the array of Tian Xun Pass, these ink balls had already attached themselves to their bodies.
The Tai Yiwei's skin turned pitch-black. Their skin cracked and their eye sockets sunk. They had turned into another type of monster. They quickly pounced on their former comrades.
"Wu!"
One of the black monsters howled and charged towards Evil Deities.
When this black monster was about two to three feet away from Evil Deities, a light flashed in Evil Deities' hand. A diamond-shaped token formed by Dao-Fusion energy was gently thrown out by Evil Deities.
"Bang!"
The diamond-shaped token exploded on the black monster's head.
"You're courting death," said Evil Deities calmly.
The black monster's head was blown to smithereens. It did not look like it would survive.
However, the black monster that had lost its head struggled on the ground for a while. Then, it got up again. Its head had also recovered.
"Because this world produced monsters, it is immortal in this world?" muttered Evil Deities.
The huge commotion caused by the explosion of the diamond-shaped token attracted the attention of even more black monsters. After staring at the Evil God for a while, the black monsters began to chase after him.
Evil Deities glanced at Tian Xun Pass not far away. He had enough time to return to Tian Xun Pass.
However, just as he was about to move, his eyes flashed.
He was only slightly slower. His path back to Tian Xun Pass was blocked by a large number of black monsters. These black monsters swarmed over. Even Evil Deities had no choice but to retreat!
This scene was naturally witnessed by many Heavenly Generals in Tian Xun Pass.
"Heavenly General called Evil Deities seems like he won't be able to come back?"
"Should we help him?"
"Let him fend for himself …"
The Evil God had also displayed great power in Tian Xun Pass, but his personality was destined to be unlikeable. Those Divine Generals who had fought on the battlefield for many years had an instinctive respect for the Evil God, so they were naturally unwilling to lend a helping hand.
After becoming a Dao Refinement Avatar, the Evil God became extremely cautious. At the very least, he couldn't have any thoughts of betraying Luo Zheng.
However, in this chaotic battle, Evil Deities made a very instinctive reaction. He used these black monsters to distance himself from Luo Zheng!
Within a certain distance, Luo Zheng could control Evil Deities at will. With a thought, Evil Deities could only stay in place, unable to move. However, the distance between Heavenly Generals and Heavenly Generals was not limitless. There had to be a limit.
Being chased by these black monsters was an opportunity for Evil Deities to test this distance!
"Wu wu wu …"
The black monsters spread their legs and chased after Evil Deities. Evil Deities was as light as a swallow, flying close to the ground.
Every time Evil Deities flew a certain distance, he would extract a certain amount of Shen Jun's power from Luo Zheng's inner world. As Heavenly Generals and Heavenly Generals, Evil Deities could obtain Shen Jun's power from Luo Zheng. Moreover, this Shen Jun's power was extracted from the Instrument of Civilization of the Tianzhi Civilization.
Within the range of Luo Zheng's control, Evil Deities could naturally extract Shen Jun's power continuously.
Once Evil Deities could not extract Shen Jun's power, it meant that they were out of Luo Zheng's control.
…
He crawled on the back of a Golden Crow Zombie Spirit for a short rest. It was not easy to control so many Golden Crows Zombie Spirits.
After fighting with Phoenix twice, Luo Zheng simply returned the control to Di Jun.
In Di Jun's hands, these Golden Crows Zombie Spirits seemed to be more agile.
"Pop!"
Phoenix suddenly burst out a ball of blazing flame.
Even Qiu Yinhe and the others might not be able to withstand this kind of flame. However, under the layers of Golden Crows Zombie Spirits, not a single wisp of flame escaped.
Perhaps due to anger, she forgot that Luo Zheng was not afraid of fire at all.
"Hmm?"
When Luo Zheng was resting, he found Evil Deities rushing east.
With a flash of thought, he arrived in the mind of Evil Deities.
Through the vision of Evil Deities, he instantly saw dozens of distorted black monsters!
Were these black monsters born after the arrival of the world?
Luo Zheng read the memory of Evil Deities. At the same time, he found that Evil Deities would extract Shen Jun's power from time to time when they were being chased by the black monsters. This behavior was somewhat strange.
"You can't go east anymore. Kill these black monsters and come back to me."
Luo Zheng's order echoed in the mind of Evil Deities. Then he left Evil Deities' mind.
Evil Deities clenched his teeth and his body stiffened.
He told himself in the depths of his heart that this was probably his only chance to escape!
However, he could not disobey Luo Zheng's order at all!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Evil Deities stretched out his hand and threw diamond-shaped tokens at the monsters.
"Bang bang bang bang …"
Each diamond-shaped token exploded on the heads of the black monsters.
However, these black monsters were simply immortal. Even if they were blown to pieces, they could still recover.
After being reborn, the black monsters seemed to have grown in size and the power contained in their bodies seemed to have increased.
"These black monsters can grow?"
Evil Deities raised his eyebrows and had another thought in the depths of his heart.
He did not hesitate anymore. His body flipped in the air and the diamond-shaped tokens exploded again at the black monsters.
"Bang bang bang …"
With Luo Zheng's continuous bombardment, these black monsters kept dying and coming back to life. Every time they came back to life, they became even stronger!
At this time, even Gui Ling, who was far away in the core area, showed a trace of confusion.
The world that Gui Ling descended into was called the Illusory World. Once the Illusory Monsters appeared, they were immortal. After being killed, they would be reborn. The more times they were reborn, the stronger they would become!
This kind of growth was almost limitless.
Almost all opponents who discovered this characteristic of the Illusory Monsters would stop attacking the Illusory Monsters. After all, killing the Illusory Monsters like this would only increase their strength.
But hundreds of miles away, there was a guy who was killing his Illusory Court Demons over and over again, and this batch of Illusory Court Demons was growing rapidly!

Chapter 3306
"Ceng!"
A sword light slashed down on Phoenix's back.
Phoenix was already prepared for this attack. She flipped her hand and actually broke Luo Zheng's sword into two. Following that, she stabbed her spear towards Luo Zheng's head.
Facing the increasingly furious Phoenix, Luo Zheng remained surprisingly calm.
He tilted his head back slightly and dodged the spear before returning to the back of the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit.
Even if the Phoenix Maiden wanted to chase after him, the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits would swarm her again, blocking her path.
These Golden Crow Corpse Spirits were like a moving forest. Luo Zheng was the hunter hiding in this forest, and Phoenix was the beast trapped in this forest.
As long as there was an opportunity, Luo Zheng would stab Phoenix with his sword from time to time.
This stab might not be able to injure Phoenix, but it could slowly deplete the Chaotic Origin Spirit in Phoenix's body.
As long as he stabbed her enough times, Luo Zheng could still kill her!
Phoenix had never thought that she would fall into such a strange situation. These Golden Crow Corpse Spirits were simply too nimble. No matter which direction she fled in, they would always be able to block her.
At the start, she still cared about her reputation and did not call out to her clansmen.
But after being stabbed three or four times by Luo Zheng, Phoenix felt a sense of danger.
If she did not escape from this place, she would most likely die!
Being forced into such a state by a junior, Phoenix felt bitter in her heart.
But when she transmitted her voice out, it was like a stone sinking into the sea. The other Jinwu were all in the midst of an intense battle. Nobody could spare any time to save her!
"Sou …"
After dodging another wing attack from a Golden Crow Corpse Spirit, Phoenix gritted her teeth. Her wings flickered with a mystical luster.
"Hundred Phoenix Spirit Dance!"
Phoenix's bloodline power was awakened once again. Her entire body was emitting a faint blue light. She became incomparably nimble.
With a light flap of her wings, hundreds of illusions appeared.
As long as there was a gap in front of her, those illusions would be able to drill out.
From afar, it seemed that Phoenix's body could be big or small, more or less.
Right now, all she wanted to do was to get rid of Luo Zheng. She had no choice but to use her bloodline power to perform a life-saving movement art.
Such a strange movement technique was so strange that even a Mountain Master would not be able to distinguish it in a short period of time.
Luo Zheng was dazzled by all the Phoenix figures.
If it were still Luo Zheng controlling these Golden Crow Corpse Spirits, he would not be able to stop Phoenix.
However, Phoenix's opponent was Di Jun.
A trace of a smile appeared on Di Jun's handsome face.
The Hundred Phoenix Spirit Dance was a unique movement divine ability of the Golden Crow Race, and it was created by him.
Even though Phoenix was extremely agile and created all kinds of visual obstacles, Di Jun could see through it at a glance.
"Huff huff huff …"
The outermost Golden Crow Corpse Spirits moved in a circle, followed by the middle layer, and then the innermost layer.
As these Golden Crow Corpse Spirits moved around, those big and small 'Phoenix' avatars were completely destroyed. The real Phoenix was rushing towards a narrow passage!
Through this passage, she could see the sky outside.
Just when the Phoenix Girl thought that she could escape, the two Golden Crow Corpse Spirits grabbed at her.
"Ka, ka!"
Four huge claws of the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits pinned her in the middle, preventing her from moving.
Phoenix widened her eyes as she saw Luo Zheng coming out from under a Golden Crow Corpse Spirit. She had an expression as though she had seen a ghost.
Luo Zheng came over with a sword. Phoenix had just broken Luo Zheng's sword. Currently, she was wielding Tengshe's sword.
More than half of her Spirit of Chaotic Origin had been consumed. She might not be able to withstand Tengshe's sword.
At this moment, Phoenix finally understood that she could not defeat this young man in front of her.
And just two years ago, Phoenix could easily crush it with her fingers …
"How are you able to break my movement technique?" Phoenix Maiden asked.
She had calmed down at the last moment, and the resentment in her heart had disappeared.
But Luo Zheng could see through Hundred Phoenix Spirit Dance with a glance, which still left Phoenix in disbelief.
Luo Zheng did not answer Phoenix's question, nor did he attack to kill her. Hearing Phoenix's question, Luo Zheng indifferently replied, "I did not see through your movement technique."
"Then how …" Phoenix was even more puzzled.
"Someone else saw through your movement technique," Luo Zheng said.
"Who!"
In Phoenix's eyes, only Emperor Jun could see through her movement technique. It was impossible for Emperor Jun to be here, and it was even more impossible for him to help this brat.
Luo Zheng did not say a word. An illusory figure appeared above his head. It was Emperor Jun himself.
Phoenix's spirited eyes widened. Thousands of thoughts and possibilities flashed through Phoenix's mind. "You are …"
She thought it was Emperor Jun's remnant soul, but there was a slight difference in aura. But Phoenix's instinct told her that it was Emperor Jun himself.
"I am Emperor Jun of Haotian," Emperor Jun replied.
When facing Phoenix, Emperor Jun released a unique majesty. This majesty was enough to make any Golden Crow Clan submit to him.
"But you are not …"
Phoenix had doubts in her heart, but she did not dare to.
"I am not the Emperor Jun you know," Emperor Jun said. "I am the real Emperor Jun of Haotian, the first Golden Crow born between heaven and earth. The Emperor Jun you know is just a seed I spread in the mother world. He is my avatar."
The Emperor Jun in the mother world knew his origin, but he had never made the 77th civilization known to the public.
When Phoenix Maiden heard such an explanation, her heart was filled with shock.
The Golden Crow Clan was a superpower with a high degree of compliance. Everyone in the Golden Crow Clan obeyed Emperor Jun unconditionally. However, according to Emperor Jun, the Emperor Jun in the mother world was just a doppelganger of this Emperor Jun in front of him.
"I, I don't believe it!" Phoenix looked like she was about to collapse.
Some people were full of energy and fighting spirit all year round because they had incomparably firm beliefs. Phoenix's belief was Emperor Jun himself. Once this belief collapsed, it would be more painful than death for her.
"Luo Zheng," Emperor Jun said.
Luo Zheng stabbed with his sharp Sword Tengshe. With a 'pu' sound, the sword pierced Emperor Jun's shoulder.
Phoenix's fleshy body was so strong that it exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations.
Luo Zheng stabbed out three times in a row!
Phoenix's Spirit of Chaotic Origin was finally exhausted. Luo Zheng's sword also pierced through Phoenix's shoulder.
When her blood spurted out, golden light flashed in Emperor Jun's eyes. The blood turned into a thin red line that floated in front of him.
"My avatar gave you the Dream Blood Transformation. It can stimulate your potential, but it can also control you indirectly," Emperor Jun said. "The Dream Blood Transformation was created by me when I was young. There is a huge flaw in this ability. But I have solved this flaw later on."
For an existence like Emperor Jun, it was equivalent to living for more than 1,000 chaos epochs in the Nirvana Realm.

Chapter 3307
Under Di Jun's control, the blood thread began to rotate. At the same time, light spots were extracted.
When the light spots appeared, they folded into a strange pattern in the air.
Luo Zheng stared at these light spots and asked curiously, "What are these light spots?"
"The power of blood," Di Jun answered.
Upon hearing Di Jun's words, Luo Zheng and Phoenix Maiden's eyes were filled with surprise.
The power of blood had always been hidden in the blood essence, but no one had ever extracted it.
It was the first time for Di Jun to display the power of blood alone.
After extracting the power of blood, Di Jun began to adjust the light spots. He pushed some light spots farther away and pulled some closer. After adjusting them, he merged them into the blood.
When he was adjusting the light spots, Luo Zheng found them familiar. These light spots were very similar to those in the Xuanliang World.
However, the light spots in the Xuanliang World were far apart and there were many of them, unlike the few in front of him.
After a while, Di Jun had adjusted these light spots.
"Hum..."
The light spots kept their positions and merged into the blood again.
"Hiss..."
The blood turned into a thin line and drilled into her wound.
When the blood completely entered her wound, Phoenix showed a painful expression.
The bloodline that had been adjusted by Di Jun was different from before. It would be rejected by the Phoenix Girl's bloodline. When the two bloodlines fought, it would cause an unbearable pain.
However, the Phoenix Maiden's willpower was astonishing. She endured the pain without making a sound.
The blood modified by Di Jun was more powerful. It kept assimilating the remaining blood in Phoenix's body. After a while, the blood in Phoenix's body was completely modified.
The Golden Crows that had been holding on to the Phoenix Girl also loosened their huge claws.
Luo Zheng took a step back reflexively, showing a vigilant look.
After all, this woman had tried to kill him many times. He had to be careful.
"Don't worry. She can't kill you," Di Jun said.
"Why?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Because after the modification, she can only submit to me," Di Jun answered.
Phoenix's expression also became very complicated.
Her heart was obviously filled with hatred for Luo Zheng, and she wanted to get rid of Luo Zheng as soon as possible, but her body was very resistant to taking action.
This was not enslavement of the mind, but enslavement of the blood!
In fact, this kind of enslavement had existed since the birth of Jinwu. From the ordinary Jinwu all over the sky to the many female demons in the Hive, they all trusted and submitted to Di Jun unconditionally. This kind of submission was engraved in their blood.
Therefore, there were very few traitors among the Golden Crows. Those who betrayed the Golden Crows often meant that their bloodline was not pure. They would also be hated and hated by the Golden Crows, just like Feng Ge and Concubine Yan. This was bloodline worship.
For example, the Ear Rats could control other rats at will for the same reason.
"Luo Zheng is the spokesperson of the 77th civilization. From now on, Luo Zheng is your master," Di Jun said again.
Hearing this, Phoenix's face was full of dissatisfaction. Her body and even the pair of wings on her back were trembling slightly, but instinctively she had to lower her head. She almost gritted her teeth and said, "Yes, Master."
Just a moment ago, she was crazily trying to kill him, but now she was respectful. Luo Zheng looked at this scene and clicked his tongue in wonder. He suddenly had an idea. "Her bloodline has been modified. If her blood is injected into the body of another Jinwu..."
"You are very smart," Di Jun smiled faintly.
This kind of bloodline worship was engraved in the instinct. Of course, it was only effective for the Jinwu people.
Under Di Jun's command, Phoenix reluctantly drew out her blood...
The Golden Crows, which were originally wrapped in a ball, quickly separated. Phoenix, who was in the innermost layer, rode a Golden Crow, locked on a target, and sped over there.
In mid-air not far away, Li Bianxue stepped on the Heavenly Chasm Sword with a serious expression.
In front of her stood a tall and sturdy Golden Crow Clan member.
This Jinwu man was called Duchai, and he was one of the best in the guards.
To Li Bianxue, Duchai was the strongest opponent she had ever faced!
In such a battle, the slightest carelessness could lead to death. Her standard oval face was full of caution and seriousness.
"Your bone age seems to be very young," Duchai spread his wings and raised his right hand slightly. Fire snakes slowly swam around his arm. "Such a young genius is rare, hehe..."
Li Bianxue did not wait for Duchai to finish speaking. She lightly tapped the Heavenly Chasm Sword with the tip of her foot. The Heavenly Chasm Sword suddenly flipped over and slashed at Duchai with supreme power.
As the Heavenly Chasm Sword slashed, Li Bianxue ran wildly along the blade of the Heavenly Chasm Sword.
She had two two-foot short swords in her hands. These two short swords were not ordinary weapons!
"Whoosh!"
Following the flashing of the two short swords, her speed suddenly increased, and she disappeared from Duchai's vision in an instant.
In the next moment, Li Bianxue appeared on the right side of Duchai.
The Heavenly Chasm Sword slashed from the left while Li Bianxue attacked from the right. Both sides led to a dead end!
"Not a bad idea..." Duchai revealed an interested look, "It's a pity that you are too weak."
Duchai spread his wings and chose to avoid the Heavenly Chasm Sword from the left. He directly pounced towards Li Bianxue.
Li Bianxue's beautiful eyes flashed with a strand of astonishment. Both her hands immediately started waving.
"Cha cha cha cha cha cha …"
In a short two breaths of time, Li Bianxue slashed out more than a hundred swords on Duchai's body.
Even if these hundred swords were not weak, they could not break through Duchai's Chaotic Origin Spirit. Naturally, they could not injure Duchai!
"Like I said, you are not strong enough." Duchai revealed a smile.
Li Bianxue frowned. She leaned back and tapped on the air. She was like a swallow flying backwards, attempting to escape back to the Heavenly Chasm Sword.
But Duchai had already taken the initiative to block in front of Li Bianxue. He spread out his wings, causing countless fire snakes to fly out from his wings. These fire snakes rapidly formed an enormous fire net that enveloped Li Bianxue within.
"Such a genius is killed just like that. What a pity. Tsk tsk …" Duchai licked his lips. He revealed a look of excitement. With a light pull, that fire net rapidly shrunk.
Li Bianxue did not have the capability like Luo Zheng who could endure all the burning flames.
If this fire net were to completely shrink, it was not hard to imagine what would happen to her.
Just as her beautiful face turned pale, a Golden Crow Corpse Spirit flew down from the side. It actually penetrated through the fire net and arrived by the side of Li Bianxue.
At this moment, Li Bianxue was in despair. When she saw Luo Zheng rushing in, she was startled, "Why did you come in?"

Chapter 3308
When Phoenix landed, there were more than ten Golden Crow Corpse Spirits following behind her.
When Duchai saw those Golden Crow Corpse Spirits, he said disdainfully, "It took you so long to get back more than ten … And that kid is still alive?"
In the eyes of Duchai and the other Jinwu people, Phoenix lost the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits because of her own carelessness, and it was her duty to get them back.
But after so long, not only did Phoenix fail to get back all the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits, but she also failed to get rid of Luo Zheng!
"I've tried my best," Phoenix said with a twinkle in her eyes.
Duchai shifted his attention away from Phoenix and stared at Luo Zheng and Li Bianxue opposite him with a smile. "I don't know why you walked right into the trap, but since you're here, die with that beautiful girl!"
The fire net that had been broken by Luo Zheng was restored to its original state, and at the same time, it was shrinking at an extremely fast speed.
Li Bianxue's heart was also full of confusion, but Luo Zheng did not answer her question.
Seeing the fire net quickly shrinking, Li Bianxue gritted her teeth and said, "There's no other way …"
"Buzz …"
Three ice crystals emerged from the top of Li Bianxue's head. Those were the Snow Elephant Ice Crystals of the 29th Layer of Heaven.
When the three Snow Elephant Ice Crystals appeared, the temperature around Li Bianxue plummeted.
A huge ice cocoon enveloped her and Luo Zheng!
This ice cocoon also had amazing defensive power. Even if the force of 20,000 Shen Jun hit it, it could still be broken.
However, as a Nirvana token, the Snow Elephant Ice Crystal only had a passive defensive effect and could not be used as the main Nirvana token. Li Bianxue had originally fused with this Nirvana token only as an emergency.
"Hiss …"
The fire net instantly enveloped the surface of the ice cocoon, and the blazing flames constantly melted the ice.
"The ice cocoon can only last for a dozen breaths at most. Once it breaks, we will still die," Li Bianxue said distractedly.
She glanced at Luo Zheng and found that Luo Zheng's face was calm, without the slightest trace of panic.
Where did this kid get his confidence from?
Just as Li Bianxue was guessing in her heart, Luo Zheng looked at Duchai and raised his hand. He was gesturing to Duchai.
Watch your left side …
Luo Zheng smiled.
Li Bianxue looked to Duchai's left with a puzzled expression.
At the same time, Duchai and Li Bianxue had similar thoughts.
The ice cocoon could protect them for a while, but it couldn't protect them forever.
Luo Zheng and Li Bianxue would die sooner or later.
However, Luo Zheng didn't realize that he was going to die. Instead, he was reminding him. Remind him of what …
On his left was Phoenix.
The moment Duchai turned his head, Phoenix Maiden had already closed in on Duchai and grabbed his neck with one hand.
"What are you doing …"
Just as Duchai's voice fell, Phoenix's flames exploded.
Boom!
This strike of Phoenix was almost at full strength. But her goal wasn't to kill Duchai. Instead, she wanted to destroy the Chaotic Origin Spirit in his body.
Even if Duchai cracked his brain, he could not understand why Phoenix would act against him.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit in his body rapidly became fatigued under the dual assault of the Divine Jun Energy and Golden Crow God Fire.
After Duchai's Chaotic Origin Spirit became fatigued, his fleshy body was no longer strong.
Phoenix lightly sliced her finger, causing a droplet of fresh blood to flow into the meridians on Duchai's neck.
"Are you crazy, Phoenix!"
Duchai held onto his neck and retreated. He thought that Phoenix had betrayed him when she attacked him. But she merely pierced his neck and did not kill him. This was different from what he had guessed.
Li Bianxue and Duchai were equally puzzled …
She looked at Luo Zheng and asked curiously: "What exactly is going on? What are the two of you doing? "
This should have been a battle of life and death. Yet now, it had turned bizarre.
"Just watch." Luo Zheng nonchalantly replied.
After Duchai distanced himself from Phoenix, he suddenly felt an intense pain coming from his neck. This pain quickly spread throughout his body. The blood in his body felt like it was boiling. It was being assimilated by the blood injected by Phoenix.
This pain lasted for a moment before disappearing without a trace. And other than the fatigued Chaotic Origin Spirit, Duchai's body did not seem to have any other abnormalities.
"Phoenix, if you can't give me an explanation, I'll kill you too!" Duchai gritted his teeth and said.
Phoenix Maiden let out a faint sigh.
Bloodline Worship was a belief rooted in the bloodline. The culture of the entire Jinwu had been formed over generations, making this belief stronger than steel. And Phoenix, who retained her rationality, felt that she had betrayed her Di Jun. But this betrayal was something she could not disobey.
Now that there was another person who betrayed her, she felt much more relaxed.
"Ask him." Phoenix pointed at Luo Zheng.
Duchai and Li Bianxue once again looked at Luo Zheng in confusion. Luo Zheng's illusory image of Di Jun appeared above his head. "Seeing Luo Zheng is the same as seeing me. You just have to obey Luo Zheng's orders unconditionally."
"What the hell are you …"
With Duchai's bad temper, he was about to scold the illusory image of Di Jun when he saw it. He originally thought that it was a clumsy illusion created by Luo Zheng. But just as he was about to speak, an irresistible force arose from within his body. "Yes, sir. This one will obey Luo Zheng's orders."
Li Bianxue watched this scene. Her cherry lips parted slightly. The astonishment in her eyes could not be concealed.
She could not help but want to scold someone …
No matter how she looked at it, this looked like a clumsy performance. But it was the truth!
"Very good, the second one." Luo Zheng smiled and looked at the sky.
In the sky above Taiyi Sect, the Heavenly Chasm Swords were flying around in the air, crazily fighting with the Jinwu people.
What Taiyi Heavenly Palace lacked the most was high-end combat power.
Di Jun's guards were no weaker than the Lords of the Seven Mountains and the experts.
If he could inject that kind of blood into these people, wouldn't they all be under his control?
Duchai was the second target!
"What the hell is going on? Explain it to me first!" Li Bianxue was a little annoyed. She felt that she was completely ignored.
"Please melt the ice cocoon," Luo Zheng said.
"If you don't explain it clearly, you will be locked here for the rest of your life!" Li Bianxue thought. The snow elephant ice crystals above her head constantly flashed with icy light. The ice cocoon thickened by several layers.
Luo Zheng looked at this woman strangely. He did not know why she was so angry. He had no choice but to explain, "I used some means to instigate them. You only need to know that they are now under my command …"
After Luo Zheng's simple explanation, Li Bianxue reluctantly opened the ice cocoon.
She did not understand Luo Zheng's words at all. But Phoenix and Duchai were indeed under Luo Zheng's command …
"Huhu …"

Chapter 3309
There were more than 80 people in Di Jun's personal guards.
They were divided into three levels according to their strength.
Duchai was one of the stronger ones in the second level. Phoenix was in the first level, but she was at the back of the first level.
Of course, Phoenix's position in Jinwu was extraordinary. Even the strongest people in the personal guards were not as powerful as Phoenix.
Ming Shi and Bai Hu were in the first level of the personal guards. They were the two strongest people in Jinwu.
These two Jinwu people were usually very low-key. They were always guarding the Jinwu and rarely showed themselves outside.
Their loyalty to Di Jun was unquestionable.
The battle that Jinwu came out in full force was a huge test for them. After all, the Great Yi Heavenly Palace was not to be trifled with.
At this moment, Ming Shi's wings were flapping as he chased upward.
The one who was retreating in the sky was Ning Xu Yuan of the Great Yi Mountain.
"Xiu —"
Ning Xu Yuan kept retreating in the air. Three Evil Eyes floated around him.
The three Evil Eyes were constantly blinking, and every time they blinked, a sharp light would shoot out. These were Flying Swords that shone brightly.
These flying swords looked ordinary, but they contained terrifying power.
Even an expert like Ming Shi would not be able to take it if he was stabbed by one of them!
Previously, in order to get closer to Ning Xu Yuan, Ming Shi took two swords. These two swords almost exhausted his Spirit of Chaotic Origin!
After that, Ming Shi was very careful and did not dare to use force.
Therefore, even though Ming Shi was faster than Ning Xu Yuan, he still did not dare to get close to him.
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
The flying swords in the three Evil Deities kept shooting out.
Ning Xu Yuan's old face was full of seriousness.
He could not remember how long it had been since he last fought. Now that he was in a life and death situation, he had no choice but to go all out.
Ming Shi flapped his wings and moved left and right in the air, sneaking in and out of the gaps between the flying swords. He used every tiny gap to shorten the distance between him and Ning Xuyuan.
"Tai Yi Heavenly Palace's power is gone. Elder Ning is also a famous elder in the Heavenly Palace, you must be able to see the situation clearly. If you can pledge allegiance to our Golden Crow Clan, our Emperor will not bear grudges," Ming Shi persuaded as he approached.
Back then, Emperor Donghuang and Emperor Jun were as close as brothers. Later on, they became enemies. Ning Xu Yuan once scolded Emperor Jun to his face. The conflict between him and Emperor Jun was not small.
The three slanted eyes floating beside Ning Xuyuan were constantly flying around, the angle of the flying sword was also rapidly adjusting. He gave a faint smile, and then said in a carefree manner, "We sword cultivators value integrity the most. If the Greater One Heavenly Palace collapses, we will lower our swords and end our lives. That Demon Emperor will remember and forget our former enmity.
I don't care! "
Ming Shi stared at Ning Xuyuan indifferently. This kind of existence must be inflexible. He did not mean that he could persuade Ning Xuyuan. The reason why he did this was just to distract him.
"Our Emperor's Dao of Adherence is the only true explanation of the End. Elder Ning, why do you insist on it?" Ming Shi asked again.
When it came to the conflict between the Dao of Order and the End, Ning Xuyuan's lips curled up. He said disdainfully, "When it comes to the fight of Dao, you bird demons have no power to think. You are just copying the words of the Demon Emperor, haha …"
Because of the bloodline worship in Jinwu, Emperor Jun was the only one who called the shots. Even though Phoenix and Ming Shi were quite intelligent, they did not question any of Emperor Jun's ideas. They could not question them.
But because there was no bloodline worship in the Greater One Heavenly Palace, there was naturally no such problem. No matter how wise Emperor Donghuang was, if he did something wrong, there would be people who would criticize him. Therefore, Ning Xuyuan's disdain was reasonable.
But before Ning Xuyuan's voice died away, Ming Shi's lips curled up slightly. A white light rushed from behind Ning Xuyuan at an extremely fast speed!
"Swoosh —"
When Ning Xuyuan reacted, it was already too late to dodge. His pupils suddenly widened, and a crescent-shaped bubble appeared on the surface of his body.
The rapidly approaching white light was Bai Hu of Jinwu.
Bai Hu was originally fighting with Gan Gaohan, but after Bai Hu activated his bloodline power, his speed increased sharply, and he chose to sneak attack Ning Xuyuan.
Under such a rush, Bai Hu was confident that he could take Ning Xuyuan's head with one blow!
But the bubble that appeared on the surface of Ning Xuyuan's body seemed fragile, but its toughness was far beyond Bai Hu's expectations. Under Bai Hu's charge, the bubble was constantly squeezed and deformed, but it did not burst in the end!
Ning Xuyuan bent his body, and tenaciously pulled away from Bai Hu. The three slanted eyes that surrounded Ning Xuyuan shone with a fiercer light.
"Swoosh, swoosh, swoosh, swoosh …"
More flying swords continued to shoot towards Ming Shi like a dense rain of swords.
When Bai Hu wanted to attack again, a sharp sword light fell from the sky and blocked in front of Bai Hu.
Gan Gaohan said with a grim face, "Bai Hu, your opponent is me!"
Bai Hu frowned and stared at Gan Gaohan.
Although their strength was about the same, Gan Gaohan was extremely difficult to deal with. Bai Hu was very dissatisfied with the opponent assigned to him!
He planned to kill Ning Xuyuan first, and then work together with Ming Shi to deal with Gan Gaohan!
The four of them confronted each other in the sky.
But this confrontation did not last for too long …
Below them, a large number of Golden Crow Corpse Spirits spread their wings and flew over, and above these Golden Crow Corpse Spirits stood Phoenix and Duchai!
When Bai Hu saw the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits, his face immediately lit up with joy, "We finally got it back!"
Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan's expressions darkened.
Before this, they were still counting on these Golden Crow Corpse Spirits to be of help to Great Yi Heavenly Palace …
But the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits were taken back by them so quickly. As the controller of the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits, Luo Zheng was most likely dead.
The two of them looked at each other, their eyes full of regret. The speed of the Heavenly Palace's collapse was faster than they had imagined.
Hundreds of Golden Crow Corpse Spirits stood side by side, almost forming a floating island in the sky. Phoenix and Duchai stood side by side on it, while Ming Shi and Bai Hu slowly landed on the backs of the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits.
Using the Nirvana Realm Token to control people's thoughts, or using other means to control the soul, or even refining the body, Ming Shi and Bai Hu could sense it.
Never in their wildest dreams would they have imagined that the Phoenix Maiden and Duchai would submit to another 'Dijun'.
"Elder Ning, Elder Gan, I am willing to give you one last chance," Ming Shi continued to persuade them. He was actually sincerely trying to recruit them.
But the two of them did not care about their words. They were already prepared to sacrifice themselves, and now was the time to fight with their lives on the line.
"Don't talk nonsense!" Gan Gaohan shouted coldly.
The sword edge hummed, and the aura soared!
Just as the two of them were about to fight with their lives on the line, the Golden Crow Corpse Spirits that were lying flat in the sky suddenly rolled up.

Chapter 3310
The huge Golden Crow Zombie Spirit was controlled by Di Jun as usual.
These Golden Crow Corpse Spirits intertwined with each other to form a unique maze, trapping Phoenix Maiden, Duchai, Ming Shi, and Bai Hu within.
Luo Zheng leaned on the Golden Crow Zombie Spirit and smiled at Ning Xuyuan and Gan Gaohan.
"Whoosh …"
A huge Heavenly Chasm Sword flew up and Li Bianxue sat on it. She pursed her lips and looked at the pile of Golden Crow Corpse Spirits with a hint of speculation and caution in her eyes.
Gan Gaohan looked at Li Bianxue and then at Luo Zheng, his eyes full of confusion.

No matter how they looked at it, it was strange. No matter how they guessed, they couldn't figure out what Luo Zheng was up to.

At this moment, the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits suddenly burst into flames. The flames were of two types, blue and green. Obviously, Phoenix and Duchai were the first to attack.
When the flame flickered, Ming Shi and Bai Hu's angry roars came from within. They probably could not understand why the loyal Phoenix Maiden would betray the Golden Crow Tribe …
"Whoosh!"
After the two roared, some of the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits also spat out blue flames.
After all, Ming Shi and Bai Hu were the strongest in Jinwu, second only to Di Jun. Their strength was comparable to the Hill Master. Even if Phoenix and Duchai launched a sneak attack, it would be difficult to take them down.
However, with Di Jun controlling the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits personally, the problem became much easier.
The battle inside the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits only lasted for seven or eight breaths. After a while, all the Golden Crow Zombie Spirits started to fight again.
Phoenix and Duchai had already stepped aside.
Duchai was unscathed, but half of Phoenix Girl's beautiful face was burned away.
"Are you okay?" Luo Zheng asked.
Phoenix reached out and touched her face gently. Her burned face quickly recovered. She shook her head and said, "I'm fine. I was just accidentally hurt by the flames."
It was Di Jun himself who had accidentally injured her, so she naturally wouldn't feel any dissatisfaction. Not to mention that half of her face was burned, she wouldn't resist even if he killed her.
As for Ming Shi and Bai Hu, they were covered in dust and grime, and their faces were filled with pain.
There was a wound on each of their chests, where the modified Golden Crow blood flowed in. After the pain passed, the two of them floated in the air, staring at Luo Zheng. An extremely contradictory feeling rose in their hearts.
"Buzz …"
Emperor Jun's virtual shadow floated up and gave the order to surrender to Luo Zheng again. The two experts lowered their heads in conflict.
Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan stared at this scene with their mouths agape …
His heart felt as if it had experienced a magnitude 18 earthquake.
What was that phantom of Di Jun just now?
It couldn't be a part of Di Jun's soul, could it?
If it was Di Jun's Divided Soul, he would be able to command Phoenix and Ming Shi.
But would Emperor Jun help Luo Zheng and the Greater One Heavenly Palace?
If he really helped them, there wouldn't be this war!
Whether it was reality or logic, it didn't make sense. The confusion in the hearts of the two old men could be imagined.
"Ha …"
Li Bianxue sat on the Heavenly Chasm Sword and inexplicably let out a chuckle.
Previously, she thought that Luo Zheng had attracted the Golden Crow corpse spirits by luck, but now he had directly attracted the experts of Jinwu.
Looking at the situation, was Luo Zheng planning to bring all the people of Jinwu over?
The people of Jinwu in front of her were strong enough to fight against the entire Heavenly Palace. If they really submitted to Luo Zheng, wouldn't Luo Zheng's status far surpass that of an ordinary Mountain Lord, and be comparable to Donghuang?
A genius always had a strong sense of vigilance towards the rise of another genius.
Therefore, Luo Zheng didn't provoke Li Bianxue, but in exchange, Li Bianxue was hostile and provocative.
But only after a few days, Li Bianxue realized that she and Luo Zheng weren't on the same level at all. This guy's ambition wasn't in the Land of Sword Grief, nor was it in the Greater One Heavenly Palace.
"Luo Zheng, can you tell us now?" Ning Xuyuan asked cautiously.
In the entire mother world, no one except Di Jun could make the experts of Jinwu submit absolutely in such a short time. Could it be that it was the real Di Jun?
Facing Ning Xuyuan's question, Luo Zheng had a headache.
In the fierce battle between the two sides, there were experts dying every second.
Whether it was the people of the Heavenly Palace or the people of Jinwu dying, Luo Zheng didn't want to see it happen.
But looking at the attitude of Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan, if Luo Zheng didn't explain clearly, they wouldn't let him go.
Therefore, Luo Zheng made a long story short, only telling them that he used the bloodline of the Jinwu, and that the "Di Jun" above his head wasn't the real Di Jun …
This explanation couldn't clear the doubts of the two elders at all, but the two elders also knew that the battle was in full swing. Since Luo Zheng had such a heaven-defying method, he naturally had to race against time.
The two elders had planned to sacrifice themselves generously. Now that their lives were saved and they saw the hope of a comeback, they would ask questions later. For now, they would do their best to cooperate with Luo Zheng.
"Okay, no matter what method you use, I, Gan Gaohan, and Elder Ning are at your disposal." Gan Gaohan smiled.
In the face of Gan Gaohan's goodwill, Luo Zheng didn't have much of a reaction. He lowered his head and glanced around, attracted by another scene.
Seven huge sword shadows crazily bombarded the body of a Golden Crow Clan member, erupting with a thunderous explosion.
The one who sent out these sword shadows was Qiu Yinhe, and his opponent was the Golden Crow Clan's Wen Qiu!
Wen Qiu's strength belonged to the second tier within the Personal Guards. He originally thought that Qiu Yinhe was much weaker than Gan Gaohan and Ning Xu Yuan, and that he could easily take him down.
Who would have thought that Qiu Yinhe's strength was enough to be ranked first in the Heaven Combat Index!
Many Heaven Chasm Swords came crashing down, forcing Wen Qiu to complain incessantly. He could only retreat continuously …
"Hong, hong, hong …"
Under the bombardment of those sword shadows, Wen Qiu's Chaotic Origin Spirit had already entered a state of fatigue. His face was filled with fear, and he was only thinking of how to escape.
And Qiu Yinhe's expression was solemn. He had no intention of letting Wen Qiu go.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly descended, saying, "Senior Qiu, please wait a moment!"
Qiu Yinhe glanced at Luo Zheng and frowned. He couldn't think of a reason why Luo Zheng would stop him.
Wen Qiu saw Qiu Yinhe hesitating, and he felt joy in his heart. Just as he was about to flap his wings and escape, Phoenix descended and landed beside Wen Qiu.
'Phoenix! You came at the right time. Hurry up and help me … 'Wen Qiu felt even happier when he saw Phoenix. Not only did he not have to escape, he could even work together with Phoenix to kill Qiu Yinhe instead.
But Phoenix didn't care about Wen Qiu's words at all. With a light flap of her wings, she approached Wen Qiu.

Chapter 3311
Under Qiu Yinhe's gaze, Wen Qiu's bloodline had been changed.

Luo Zheng relied on Phoenix and the Golden Crow zombie spirit to convert all the Jinwu people who had moved to the Taiyi Sect.

Among the Seven Mountains, the Tai Qing Mountain was best at defense. The Tai Qing Array was actually the 36 Sword Shields Array.
After the Jinwu people teleported in, they did not destroy the 36 Sword Shields Array. Instead, they were chased out by the people of the Tai Qing Mountain.
Now, the leader of the Tai Qing Mountain led the powerful people of the mountain to retreat and defend the array …
However, defense was always passive. If they kept defending the array, the array would eventually be broken.
Under the relentless attacks of the Golden Crow Clan, the Thirty-Six Sword Shield Formation was faintly unable to hold on. The cultivators of Mount Tai Qing responded. Although their faces were full of anticipation, they did not think so in their hearts.
In this battle, the Jinwu had the upper hand. Although the Greater One Mountain had Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan, the Jinwu would definitely send more powerful people to break the formation. The Greater One Mountain would most likely be unable to take care of themselves, how could they come and save them?

"They're from Jinwu!"

" The Jinwu person who spoke was called Feng Ting. Together with Ming Shi and Bai Hu, they were in the first tier. This time, he was the one who led the team to attack Tai Qing.
The formation.

In this battle, the Taiyi Heavenly Palace lost completely …

"What, what are they doing …"
"The Jinwu people are killing each other? That's impossible! "
"That's impossible. What are they up to?"
After Ming Shi and the others subdued Feng Ting and the others, Luo Zheng led Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and the others to slowly descend.
Luo Zheng looked around. Feng Ting and the others did not have the chance to resist or escape before they were injected with the modified Golden Crow bloodline.
"Well done." Luo Zheng nodded.
There were about 20 Jinwu people on both sides of the Supreme One Formation and the Tai Qing Formation. They were not a small force.
Moreover, these Jinwu people followed Luo Zheng and quickly dragged down the strength of the Jinwu people.
"Elder Gan! Elder Ning … what happened! "The Mountain Master of the Great Green Mountain was still in a daze.
Although the situation had been completely reversed and they had even waited for Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan to arrive, the Mountain Master of the Great Green Mountain was still somewhat guarded and did not remove the Tai Qing Formation.
He was afraid that this was just an illusion …
There was no need for Luo Zheng to explain. Gan Gaohan smiled and sent a message to the Mountain Master of the Great Green Mountain, explaining what he knew.
The Mountain Master of the Great Green Mountain stared at Luo Zheng in a daze. The shock on his face was indescribable.
Luo Zheng had been famous for a long time in the Greater One Heavenly Palace.
At that time, he stood up for the Heavenly Palace in the Fourth Heavenly Palace. After the You Xiong Clan could not do anything to Luo Zheng, they came to threaten the Greater One Heavenly Palace.
The Mountain Masters also thought that Luo Zheng had a bright future.
But for such a Fourth Heavenly Layer genius to grow, it would take hundreds or thousands of years.
In just one or two years, Luo Zheng was already able to reverse the battle between two super forces by himself?
The Mountain Master of the Great Green Mountain sighed in his heart. As for what magical method Luo Zheng used to make these Jinwu experts submit, it was no longer important.
Just as the Mountain Master of the Great Green Mountain opened the Tai Qing Formation, several people quickly approached from not far away!
As those people flew, they were supported by rays of purple light.
After seeing the purple light, Gan Gaohan's face darkened. He immediately said, "That bastard Xu Shou is here!"
The Mountain Master of the Great Green Mountain's face was also covered with a layer of frost. He sneered, "He probably thought that our Great Green Mountain was the weakest and was the first to attack us?"
Xu Shou attacked the other six mountains at a critical time, which naturally made the other Mountain Masters disappointed.
The entire Great Green Mountain had already burned their boats. Since he attacked, he naturally had to eliminate the other six mountains!
When the Jinwu attacked the other six mountains, Xu Shou also made some preparations and led his troops to attack.

Chapter 3312
When Xu Shou, Lady Xuan, and the others arrived at the Tai Qing Array, their momentum was overwhelming.
With the strength of the Tai Hao Mountain and the Jinwu people, it should be easy to take down the Tai Qing Mountain.
But when he saw Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan, he was stunned. He didn't expect the people of the Greater One Mountain to be here!
With these two people in charge, the Greater One Mountain was not a pushover!
Xu Shou frowned and turned his head. Only then did he see many members of the Golden Crow Tribe gathered outside the Supreme Green Formation. There were many members of the Golden Crow Tribe, and when he saw Ming Shi and Bai Hu, Xu Shou's face was filled with confidence.
In a situation where both sides were about the same strength, the addition of the Tai Hao Mountain was enough to break the balance.
"Xu Shou! Donghuang treated you well, but you betrayed the Heavenly Palace at a time of crisis! I ask you, where is your conscience! "Gan Gaohan saw Xu Shou and couldn't suppress the anger in his heart.
The Seven Mountains of the Heavenly Palace had always been united, and they were the foundation of the Greater One Heavenly Palace. Without Donghuang, how could the Tai Hao Mountain and his Xu family be where they were today?
"Where is my conscience?" Xu Shou smiled coldly. "Donghuang was stubborn and led the people of the world to the wrong path. My Tai Hao Mountain just chose the right path!"
His words were for Phoenix, Ming Shi, and the others to hear. It was also to show his sincerity.
According to Xu Shou's expectations, Ming Shi, Phoenix Maiden, and the others would definitely agree with him after he finished speaking. However, when he looked at them, Phoenix Maiden, Ming Shi, and the others all had cold expressions on their faces. The atmosphere seemed a little strange …
But Xu Shou couldn't know what was going on.
For example, Ming Shi and Bai Hu agreed with Xu Shou's choice, but their bodies didn't.
Ning Xuyuan looked at Xu Shou with a fake smile and said in an old voice, "This may be the stupidest choice you've made in your life."
"The stupidest choice? How can you be so sure? "Consort Xuan asked in an overbearing manner.
Luo Zheng looked at the red-clothed Lady Xuan and smiled faintly. "You'll know soon."
Lady Xuan's gaze fell on Luo Zheng, and a strange expression appeared on her face. "Luo Zheng, you're really lucky. You're actually still alive and kicking after such a long time!"
Several months ago, Mount Taihao had received an order to capture Luo Zheng. However, at that time, Mount Taihao had never had the opportunity to act.
In the end, during the battle between Jinwu and the Heavenly Palace, Luo Zheng had been safe and sound until now, which really surprised Consort Xuan.
"Consort Xuan, you seem very surprised?" Luo Zheng asked indifferently.
Mistress Xuan raised her eyebrows and said condescendingly, "It is indeed unexpected, but it doesn't matter. Today, you are destined not to escape this calamity!"
In her and Xu Shou's opinion, Jinwu was bound to win. Naturally, Taihao Mountain was one of the winners.
Luo Zheng revealed a bright smile, "I'd like to see who can't escape from whose palm …"
As he spoke, he pointed to the sky and said, "Do it."
Xu Shou, Lady Xuan, Huang Long, the leader of the Dark Tribe, and the other experts of Mount Taihao were all puzzled when they saw Luo Zheng's attitude.
Since when was it Luo Zheng's turn to give orders here?
Weren't Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan here?
However, in the next moment, they understood what was going on …
The people Luo Zheng ordered to act were not from Mount Taihao, but those from Jinwu!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Ming Shi, Bai Hu, Phoenix, and many other Jinwu people spread their wings and flew up. In the blink of an eye, they had surrounded Xu Shou and the others!
Xu Shou stared at Ming Shi with wide eyes, his eyes filled with disbelief.
As for Lady Xuan, her mouth was agape, and her body became extremely stiff.
"Brother Ming Shi, why are you doing this?" Xu Shou asked.

There was no turning back for Luo Zheng's orders. Many Jinwu people could not disobey.
Seeing that Ming Shi, Bai Hu, and the others were really going to act, Xu Shou roared. He led the experts of Mount Taihao and took out their Nirvana Realm tokens …
For a moment, the air was filled with energy and flames flew everywhere.
Luo Zheng looked at the sky indifferently for a while, then turned to Gan Gaohan and said, "Elder Gan, can you help the Jinwu people?"
These Jinwu people had completely submitted to Luo Zheng. They were also valuable forces.
Although they were enough to deal with Mount Taihao, there might be casualties.
If Mount Taiyi and Mount Taihao were to help, things would be simpler.
How could Gan Gaohan not understand Luo Zheng's thoughts?
He immediately said, "My fellow comrades, we should naturally eliminate the traitors of Taihao Mountain. Those members of the Golden Crow Clan have already submitted to Luo Zheng. Everyone, there is no need to fear them!"
After he finished speaking, Gan Gaohan took the lead and rushed forward!
He had followed Emperor Donghuang his whole life. Naturally, he hated traitors like him.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Then, Ning Xuyuan, Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, and the experts of Mount Taiqing also rushed into the sky.
It had to be said that Xu Shou's strength had improved tremendously over the years!
He held a purple broadsword in his hand. Purple Dragon Qi coiled around his body. Every time he waved the sword, purple light would appear. Everything close to him would be crushed into powder.
Even Ming Shi and Bai Hu, the two Jinwu experts, could not get close to him. Naturally, they could not take down Xu Shou in a short time.
However, after Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan joined the battle, the situation of Mount Taihao took a turn for the worse.
Xu Shou's strength might be powerful, but he was not as strong as Elder Gan. Emperor Donghuang, Gan Gaohan, and Ning Xuyuan might be a little weaker than Xu Shou, but the gap was not big. How could Xu Shou withstand the joint attack of the two of them, Ming Shi, and Bai Hu?
He was still in high spirits when he came, but suddenly he was in such a situation. Xu Shou was so angry that he almost vomited blood!
He even suspected that he was having a nightmare …
Just as Xu Shou was in a difficult situation, Lady Xuan's scream came from not far away.
Lady Xuan's strength was not outstanding among the many experts. Moreover, she was surrounded by four or five people. She could not last more than three to five breaths.
Seeing Lady Xuan's tragic death, Xu Shou roared angrily. Purple light surged, and he was about to fight with his life.
However, just as his aura skyrocketed, numerous flying swords shot towards Xu Shou's head!
The flying swords shot out by the three Evil Eyes beside Ning Xuyuan were extremely powerful. No one was willing to face them head-on.
Xu Shougang wanted to retreat but Bai Hu who was not far away had already taken out a Nirvana Realm Token. The Nirvana Realm Token was in the shape of a triangle. The three sides of the Nirvana Realm Token were as smooth as a mirror. It was very small and it was a Nirvana Realm Token that no one had ever seen before.
"Freeze!"
Only one word was heard from Bai Hu.
The triangular Paramita Token flashed, and Xu Shou was imprisoned in a triangular space. He could not escape.
"Boom! Boom! Boom! Boom … "
While Xu Shou was imprisoned, flying swords that contained tens of thousands of kilograms of power slammed into Xu Shou's body.

Chapter 3313
After that protective Nirvana Treasure shattered, Xu Shou could only use his fleshy body to resist.
But even if his Chaotic Origin Spirit was dense, there was a limit to how much Godly Jun Energy it could withstand.
At the final moment, Xu Shou decided to burn his Nirvana Token. But looking at the situation, he ultimately chose to give up.
Burning the Nirvana Token could allow him to fight to the death, but he could not escape death. He still had to bear the indescribable pain.
He would never have thought that he would actually die under the joint attack of the Jinwu and the Human Race …
Xu Shou did not burn his Nirvana Token, but one of his subordinates chose this path.
The one who burned his Nirvana Token was Huang Long, the old man who brought Luo Zheng away from the Greater One Mountain. His Nirvana Token was a golden monkey covered in pure gold.
Burning the Nirvana Token would result in the punishment of the Truth. This punishment was extremely painful.
And it was not just him who suffered, the Nirvana Token was the same.
It was fine if the Nirvana Token was an inanimate object, but Huang Long's golden monkey was a living object!
When the golden monkey started burning, everyone heard a loud and clear scream. That was the sound of the golden monkey instantly becoming as huge as a mountain, swallowing Huang Long whole!
The Nirvana Token had killed its owner!
This enraged golden monkey continued burning as it tried to tear everything around it apart.
If it was placed in front of ordinary experts, the golden monkey would have already started a massacre.
But those surrounding the golden monkey were not ordinary people …
Whether it was Gan Gaohan or Ming Shi, they were existences second only to those supreme experts.
Even though this golden monkey was incomparably strong, it was still tightly controlled by these people. In the end, this huge golden monkey was burned to ashes, leaving behind a trace of burnt smell. It disappeared unwillingly into the horizon.
"Hu …" After eliminating this golden monkey, Gan Gaohan said, "The thing descending from the Nirvana Realm is truly terrifying!"
Luo Zheng heard this from not far away. He asked, "Old Gan, this is just burning the Nirvana Token. Why do you say it descends?"
Gan Gaohan glanced at Luo Zheng and answered, "Actually, the essence of burning the Nirvana Token is to descend. Their continuous burning is just a kind of appearance …"
"Did it burn because it triggered the Law of Truth?" Luo Zheng asked.
"That's right." Gan Gaohan sized up Luo Zheng. He realized that Luo Zheng's knowledge was no less than his own.
Luo Zheng nodded.
From a certain point of view, the Nirvana World was indeed one level higher than the Chaos. At least the flowers and plants there contained terrifying energy. Even the sand of the first layer of heaven contained the power of one jun. This was something that neither the Mother World nor the Chaos could compare to.
In the time it took for an incense stick to burn, Xu Shou of Taihao Mountain was defeated.
The two leaders of the Dark Group were defeated.
The Desolate Dragon was dead …
In the end, only Lady Xuan and the other three experts of Taihao Mountain were left.
An expert of Mount Tai Qing took out a Dragon Imprisoning Chain, using the Paramita Token to bind Lady Xuan and the others. Their fates would naturally be decided by the Celestial Palace.
When Lady Xuan passed by Luo Zheng, she glanced at Luo Zheng. Her gaze was a little sluggish. She still hadn't recovered from the huge change.
Perhaps because they saw Luo Zheng, regret appeared on their faces. Although Empress Xuan didn't know what was going on, she had clearly heard that these Golden Crow clansmen were under Luo Zheng's orders!
Mount Tai Qing had fallen to this state because of this brat!
"I really should have killed you back then …" Lady Xuan suddenly said through gritted teeth.
At that time, Lady Xuan was determined to get the True Comprehension Chapter from Luo Zheng. Unfortunately, she didn't get it and instead attracted Concubine Yan and Elder Ying.
If she had directly killed Luo Zheng back then, there wouldn't be so much trouble now.
Facing Lady Xuan, whose face was full of hatred, Luo Zheng only showed a faint smile. Then, Lady Xuan was taken away.
"Xu Shou is really wishful thinking! If it weren't for Luo Zheng, he would've easily broken my formation with the help of the Jinwu. I'm afraid the other six mountains would've been destroyed by him one by one … "the master of Mount Tai Qing said.
"Of course." Qiu Yinhe looked at Luo Zheng with satisfaction and smiled.
Even Qiu Yinhe found it hard to believe that Luo Zheng could single-handedly turn the situation around.
"What should we do next?" The master of Mount Tai Qing asked as he looked at Phoenix and the other Jinwu people. A thought suddenly appeared in his mind. Could Luo Zheng control all the Jinwu people?
"We need to hurry to the next formation," Luo Zheng replied.
His purpose didn't need to be stated. The others already knew.
Phoenix pursed her lips. There was unwillingness in the depths of her eyes. She was inexplicably controlled by this brat. One could imagine how she felt.
Next was the Tai Ming Formation, Tai Jin Formation, Tai Xiu Formation …
As the Jinwu team behind Luo Zheng grew stronger and stronger, it became easier to deal with the Jinwu people. After all, Luo Zheng's team was like a rolling snowball. The number of experts in Luo Zheng's team was almost overwhelming.
While Luo Zheng was subduing the Jinwu people, Gan Gaohan, Qiu Yinhe, and even Li Bianxue were secretly observing.
After a few times, they found some patterns.
It seemed that to control the Jinwu people, one needed to integrate the bloodline. And the Jinwu people controlled by Luo Zheng all had this bloodline.
As for the phantom of Di Jun that suddenly appeared above Luo Zheng's head, everyone was still puzzled.
Was that the real Di Jun?
If so, why did he help Luo Zheng?
If not, why could he command the Jinwu people …
Under the great confusion, there was also great joy.
For the Taiyi Heavenly Palace, it was originally an extremely difficult battle, but in the end, they won so easily.
When they reached the Tai Xiu Formation, Luo Zheng was leading more than 80 Jinwu people behind him. These people were almost the main battle force of the Jinwu people!
Next to the Tai Xiu Formation, Gui Ling was leading seven or eight Jinwu people to fight with their backs to the wall!
When he saw Phoenix and the other Jinwu people standing opposite him, he could hardly believe his eyes.
Phoenix directly went forward to persuade him to surrender.
In the Jinwu, Gui Ling had always been unconvinced by Phoenix.
In the face of Phoenix's persuasion, he did not obey at first. With the power of the world that descended, no matter how many opponents there were, he could hold on for a while.
Therefore, Luo Zheng stepped forward and asked Di Jun to persuade him to surrender.
Although Gui Ling and the other Jinwu people had not yet injected their bloodline, when they saw the other Di Jun, they instantly began to doubt their position. After all, Phoenix, Bai Hu, Ming Shi, and nearly 80% of the Jinwu people had surrendered to this "Di Jun".
After hesitating for a while, Gui Ling sighed helplessly and took the remaining Jinwu people to surrender and let Phoenix inject their bloodline.
When Gui Ling was about to retrieve the world that he had descended, he looked to the southeast with a strange expression on his face.
"Hey, these Illusory Court Demons …"
The world that Gui Ling had descended covered an area of three million miles. Within this area, there were scattered Illusory Court Demons.

Chapter 3314
When Luo Zheng heard Ghost Spirit mention the east, his heart skipped a beat, and he realized that he had neglected an important thing.
Evil Deities!
With a slight change of thought, he sensed the location of Evil Deities.
Evil Deities were now his Taoist doppelgangers, just like Luo Zheng's hands and feet. As long as he could sense them, he could control them. And Evil Deities could not disobey his orders.
Luo Zheng did not find the existence of Evil Deities. His face suddenly changed.
Before this, Luo Zheng had ordered Evil Deities to return immediately after killing those Illusory Court Monsters. Logically speaking, he could not disobey this order. Evil Deities should not be able to escape!
Luo Zheng thought for a while, then suddenly stared at Ghost Spirit and asked, "Can these Illusory Court Monsters be killed?"
Ghost Spirit did not know why Luo Zheng asked this question, but since Luo Zheng had been identified as his master by Emperor Jun, Ghost Spirit could not refuse his question. He could only answer honestly, "Illusory Court Monsters can't be killed. The more they are killed, the bigger they will become.
Only when the whole Illusory Court World is destroyed will the Illusory Court Monsters disappear. I … will take back the Illusory Court World now! "
There were still many Illusory Court Monsters within three million miles. Most of the Tai Yiwei had hidden in the 12th Level, but those Illusory Court Monsters were still crazily attacking the Magic Array of the 12th Level.

"Of course." Ghost Spirit's expression became more and more strange. He did not know what Luo Zheng wanted to do.
"I want to find someone. That direction!" Luo Zheng pointed to the east.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Ghost Spirit immediately thought of those huge Illusory Court Monsters. With a slight change of thought, he had already escaped into the minds of those Illusory Court Monsters.
Very soon, Gui Ling was dumbfounded by what he saw.
He saw a human being crazily attacking those Illusory Court Monsters.
Not only would the Illusory Court Demon not die after being attacked, but it would also become stronger. That human should have noticed this long ago.
However, his tireless attacks seemed to be aimed at making these Illusory Court Demons stronger!
As the Illusion Court Demon's warning continued to expand, the human was finally no match for it. In order to save his life, he could only run all the way. While running, he didn't forget to turn around and attack the Illusion Court Demon. Just like that, he flew toward the east.
"Have you found it?" Luo Zheng asked.
Ghost Spirit stretched out her hand, and a translucent black gem appeared in her palm. This black gem was the weapon of civilization of the Illusory Court World and the most important thing to Ghost Spirit. Normally, she wouldn't show it to others.
"Buzz …"
A wisp of thick ink-like smoke floated out of the black gem, drifting towards Luo Zheng's eyes.
In fact, the smoke from the Weapon of Civilization can turn people into Illusory Monsters. If the Ghost Spirit chooses to sneak attack, Luo Zheng may also be affected.
In fact, the ghost spirits really did want to do that …
However, as soon as he had this thought, his instinct immediately erased it. He couldn't hurt his master.
Ghost Spirit sighed in her heart and chose to compromise. The entire Jinwu had betrayed her, and she wasn't the only one at fault.
After Luo Zheng's eyes came into contact with the two wisps of smoke, he saw the Evil Deities being chased by the Illusory Court Demons.
"They only used the energy of the Dao Fusion. Why do they keep borrowing the power of the Weapon of Civilization?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
Just now, Luo Zheng could sense that the Evil Deities were borrowing the power of the Weapon of Civilization. It was normal for them to use this power to deal with the Illusory Court Demons. Luo Zheng didn't think too much about it, but just thinking about it made him feel that something was wrong.
The Evil Deities didn't borrow much power of the Weapon of Civilization, only one or two Senior Gods.
Previously, Luo Zheng felt that it was strange, but he didn't think too much about it. Now, it seemed that the Evil Deities were testing the limits of his control …
Ghost Spirit looked at Luo Zheng's serious and gloomy expression and said, "This person just left not long ago. He must still be in Qi Bei Province. We can help Master find him!"
Luo Zheng hesitated for a moment and said to the people of Jinwu in a low voice, "Dig three feet into the ground and find him!"
He didn't want to drive the people of Jinwu away at this juncture. After all, he wasn't the real commander of the people of Jinwu. However, the potential of the Evil Deities and the energy of the Dao Fusion he comprehended were no small matter. He couldn't let him escape.
"Yes!"
Ghost Spirit bowed to Luo Zheng and looked at Bai Hu.
Looking for one person in the huge Qi Bei Province was like looking for a needle in a haystack. However, Jinwu didn't have much, but they had the most people!
With a wave of Bai Hu's hand, a space was opened, and countless Golden Crows rushed out. They covered the sky and covered the earth, and flew towards the east …
…
The truth was exactly what Luo Zheng thought.
The Evil Deities kept drawing the power of Shen Jun. He didn't use it to fight the enemy, but to test the range of Luo Zheng's control.
He couldn't disobey Luo Zheng's orders, but the Illusory Court Demons couldn't be killed. Therefore, before killing the Illusory Court Demons, he didn't need to return to Luo Zheng's side.
Evil Deities attacked and retreated. After retreating 1.5 million miles, the Evil Deities found that he couldn't draw the power of Shen Jun anymore.
There was a trace of joy in his heart, and he no longer entangled with the Illusory Court Demons. After shaking them off, he ran all the way.
The feeling of confinement that always lingered on him was finally shaken off!
In fact, the Wu Kong Clan had already thought of using the "Life Ending Bone Land" to help the Evil Deities solve the problem of Dao Refinement. After all, entering the Life Ending Bone Land was equivalent to being reborn.
Unfortunately, in order to deceive Luo Zheng, Evil Deities had asked Fairy Qiong Xiao to erase his memories. The current Evil Deities didn't know anything at all, so he took the risk and seized this opportunity to escape.
After continuing east for another million miles, the Illusory Court World that covered this area disappeared …
The Evil Deities were once the owners of the Weapon of Civilization, so they knew the rules of the world that descended.
When a world disappeared, it would either be taken back by the owner or the owner would die.
No matter what the situation was, it was not something the Evil Deities were happy to see. This meant that the war might end.
Qi Bei Province was not safe for the Evil Deities. He should leave as soon as possible.
However, the area of a province was extremely huge. If he wanted to escape without the help of a teleportation formation, the time would probably be calculated in months.
After thinking for a while, the Evil Deities found a river and jumped down with a splash. After sinking to the bottom of the river, he dug out a cave and continued to drill downward.
Hiding deep underground should be the safest method.
Along the way, he dug out a complicated cave and killed a few snakes that were hiding underground. Finally, he opened up a small underground secret room.
In the secret chamber, the Evil Deities sat cross-legged and silently chanted the true meaning of Dao. He had already fallen into the Nirvana Realm.

Chapter 3315
Just as Evil Deities' Sun Soul condensed in the Nirvana Realm, a red ball popped out from a diamond-shaped array not far away.
Fairy Qiong Xiao emerged from the red ball and glared at Evil Deities impatiently.
Evil God looked at this uninvited guest and his face was filled with wariness.

She was originally unwilling to stay in the lower level of the Nirvana Realm. The pressure of the Fourteenth Heaven made her feel very uncomfortable.
However, she could not disobey the orders of the higher-ups. She could only wait patiently. How could she be in a good mood after waiting for so long?
"What are you waiting for?" Evil Deities sized up the woman in front of him.

Evil Deities stood on the spot and digested the memories. After a while, he chuckled and said, "Interesting. It seems that my escape was unnecessary!"

Evil Deities told her about him leaving Luo Zheng's side and the battle with Jinwu.

Although Fairy Qiong Xiao could not return to her mother world, she was still very familiar with the situation in her mother world.
Jinwu had launched a decisive battle against Tai Yi Heavenly Palace and sent a very important signal. Genryū was probably going to make a big move. She had to report this matter to her master …
…
The Heavenly Chasm Swords were stabbed into the ground, emitting a different kind of power.
The disciples of the Nirvana Realm, Tai Yiwei, the various sword sects, and the Divine Generals gathered in front of Tai Yi Heavenly Palace.
Previously, all the people of the Heavenly Palace were very pessimistic. They thought that they would definitely lose this battle.
No one expected that this battle would end in such a way …
Regardless of whether it was the Nirvana Realm experts or Tai Yiwei, all of them felt like they had just survived a disaster.
However, everyone was still wary of the Jinwu people on the right side of the Taiyi Sect.
"Those Jinwu people are just going to surrender to our Greater One Heavenly Palace?"

"Not to seek refuge with our Greater One Heavenly Palace, but to serve Luo Zheng!"
