This explanation still sounded hard to accept, but the truth was right in front of them.
Bai Hu sat next to Luo Zheng. He seemed to be meditating with his eyes closed.
Millions upon millions of Golden Crows were under Bai Hu's control. Those Golden Crows had already spread out at an extremely fast speed. They would not let go of any corners along the way …
Unfortunately, Bai Hu maintained this posture for a long time without any reaction. This meant that he did not gain anything at all.
Phoenix secretly observed Luo Zheng's expression from the side. This guy also had a worried expression?
She roughly knew the whole story of this matter.
That Evil Deities who were once unparalleled in the Chaotic Origin Great World had been refined into a Dao Refinement Avatar by this brat. However, he did not erase the memory of the Evil Deities, allowing the Evil Deities to escape.
In Phoenix's heart, she could not help but admit that Luo Zheng was a freak. The lifeform in his inner world was actually so powerful. That Evil Deities only needed to be nurtured slightly, and they could become the peak characters in the black ship.
Gan Gao Han and Ning Xu Yuan were also very concerned about Luo Zheng.
Even though they could not understand how important that "Dao Refinement Avatar" was to Luo Zheng, they still sent people to search for it. Li Bianxue was one of the people searching for it.
Ning Xu Yuan brought the Divine General, Tai Yiwei, and many Nirvana Realm experts back to North Xuan City.
This search lasted for three days and three nights. They covered a considerable area of Qi Bei Prefecture, but they still did not find anything.
"Forget it, Elder Gan. Let's return to North Xuan City first before making a decision," Luo Zheng said to Gan Gao Han with cupped fists.
After that, he asked Bai Hu to recall all the Golden Crows and brought many Jinwu people back to North Xuan City with the experts from various mountains.
But just as everyone returned to North Xuan City, a strange scene suddenly appeared in the sky. A huge sun appeared in the sky, and the entire Qi Bei Prefecture could see it clearly.
"Weng …"
That huge sun trembled, and it turned from one to two, three, four, five … Finally, there were nine suns floating in the sky.
When Phoenix, Ming Shi, and the others saw these nine suns, their expressions changed drastically.
"Nine Suns Apocalypse!"
"Di Jun is here!"
"Oh no, he wants to destroy Qi Bei Prefecture?"
Most of the Jinwu people who followed behind Luo Zheng were Di Jun's personal guards. Naturally, they could recognize the origin of those huge suns in the sky.
In fact, there was no need for these Jinwu people to remind them. Others could also see it.
When the nine suns appeared, the temperature of the entire Qi Bei Prefecture soared. The water in all the rivers and lakes boiled rapidly. Those mortals who lived in remote mountainous areas died instantly.
Ordinary mortals in North Xuan City had already evacuated. The weakest ones who stayed here were at the Nirvana Realm. Naturally, they could withstand the temperature.
But in the face of the nine rapidly expanding suns, they still fell into panic.
When Gan Gao Han and Ning Xuyuan saw this scene, their faces were also filled with shock. The most primitive worry in their hearts was suddenly aroused.
In the war with Jinwu, the biggest crisis of Great Heavenly Palace was not the advancing Golden Crows, but Donghuang Taiyi and Elder Ying being trapped!
If Di Jun personally took action, who could stop him?
From this point of view, Great Heavenly Palace had already lost without a fight!
As the war began, Di Jun did not appear. Gan Gao Han naturally had a trace of luck in his heart. Di Jun might not come.
No matter what, Luo Zheng had helped the Heavenly Palace get through this stage and won some time. He hoped that Donghuang could leave the Nirvana Realm as soon as possible.
But just as they returned to North Xuan City, the scene in the sky shattered Gan Gao Han's fantasy.
"Everyone retreat to the teleportation array!"
"Retreat to the Center God Province!"
"…"
The entire North Xuan City was in an uproar. Everyone was rushing towards the teleportation array.
During this process, the nine suns in the sky became bigger as if they were going to crash down on the ground.
"Feng! Feng … "
All the flowers and trees in Qi Bei Prefecture were being roasted. The temperature was rapidly rising.
It was still fine for Chaotic Origin Realm experts. This bit of scorching heat could be withstood by the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
But to ordinary Nirvana Realm experts, it was difficult!
Some people's clothes were ignited, turning them into flaming humans. Under the blazing flames, those people were roaring in pain.
The teleportation array in North Xuan City was not big. As they fought to be first, they were squeezed into a bundle. The flames even spread on these people.
"Hua!"
At this moment, a blue umbrella opened above North Xuan City. The one who acted was an expert from the Great Brightness Mountain.

Chapter 3316
The nine huge suns expanded rapidly, and the temperature rose rapidly.
When the flowers and trees turned into ashes, the mountain rocks exploded one by one, making an earth-shattering sound.
"Let's go!"
"If we don't teleport out, we'll be dead!"
"You guys go first …"
Some of the Nirvana Realm cultivators were crying, and some were roaring.
However, the teleportation portal was only so big. Even if three of them squeezed into it, it might take some time for them to teleport out.
The people at the back of the line were filled with anxiety!
"Get out of my way!"
A five-star Tai Yiwei roared.
In their desperation to escape, many of them did not care about their dignity and modesty.
This five-star Tai Yiwei immediately took out his Nirvana Realm Token, which was a huge white skull. This skull belonged to the Southern Sky Rhinoceros in the twelfth layer of heaven. The Southern Sky Rhinoceros was the most powerful Nirvana Realm creature in the twelfth layer of heaven. It could not be tamed and kept as a Nirvana Realm Token.
After killing it, the Nirvana Realm power in its skull was shocking, and it was very tough.
He held the skull of the Southern Sky Rhinoceros and rammed forward.
"Bang, bang, bang …"
A large number of people in front of him fell, and dozens of people were sent flying. Some of them were seriously injured.
Just as this five-star Tai Yiwei was about to crash into the teleportation portal, a needle-like sword ray descended from the sky and landed gently on the skull of the Southern Sky Rhinoceros.
"Cha …"
A thin line of damage appeared in the center of the Southern Sky Rhino's skull. Behind the skull, the Five-Star Taiyi Guard's forehead, nose, neck, and chest had a bloody line on his entire body.
"Plop …"
His entire body was split into two by this needle-like sword ray.
"If you want to enter the teleportation portal, you have to line up obediently. This is the consequence of anyone who violates the order!"
A crisp voice came from mid-air. It was Li Bianxue who spoke.
Hechi ordered her to maintain the order. Looking at the chaotic crowd, she did not know where to start. Who knew that someone would use the Nirvana Realm Token to crush her companions? Li Bianxue was furious.
Under such circumstances, if chaos really broke out, everyone would probably take out their Nirvana tokens one after another.
In fact, Li Bianxue had already sensed the aura of many Nirvana Realm tokens. If these people were to use the Nirvana Realm tokens to fight with their lives on the line, not to mention escaping, the teleportation portal would probably be destroyed immediately. As a result, no one would be able to escape!
Li Bianxue also decisively killed him.
On the one hand, this person deserved to die. On the other hand, he had greatly intimidated the others.
Those Tai Yiwei in the Nirvana Realm stared at the five-star Tai Yiwei that had been sliced into two with horrified expressions.
Many of them had indeed intended to use their Nirvana Realm tokens to resist. Now, they had also silently put away their Nirvana Realm tokens.
He took it back.
The originally crowded and chaotic group gradually regained order.
Just as Li Bianxue heaved a sigh of relief, she suddenly heard an explosion coming from above.
"Crackle …"
A crack had already appeared on the ice blue umbrella.
The heat emitted by the nine gigantic suns was getting stronger and stronger. The powerhouse of Mount Taiming was obviously unable to hold on any longer. No matter how he squeezed the Nirvana Realm token, or even desperately replenished the divine crystal, he could not maintain the umbrella!
"Crackle, crackle, crackle …"
The cracks on the surface of the umbrella became bigger and bigger. A crack appeared in the middle of the umbrella. Scorching rays of light shone down from the crack and shone on the bodies of the Tai Yiwei.
"Ah …"
Following a series of screams, these Tai Yiwei were reduced to ashes.
Seeing this scene, almost everyone's heart skipped a beat.
They thought that with their strength, they would be able to hold on for a while under the light of the nine suns. Seeing the fate of these Tai Yiwei, their hearts were filled with fear. Once the umbrella collapsed, it was easy to imagine what would happen to them.
Luo Zheng furrowed his brows and looked at the sky. It seemed like nothing could block these suns …
"Crackle …"
Just as he was looking at the sky, another crack opened. Rays of light shone down from the crack. It was as if a divine sword of light had descended from the sky and was swimming around Beixuan City. Someone below screamed.
"Dodge!"
"It's coming, it's coming!"
"The crack is spreading towards us …"
The person who was screaming was actually Lai Huabei!
Lai Huabei's talent was worlds apart from the disciples of Dao Sword Palace.
Even with the resources of Dragon City, he was unable to step into the Nirvana Realm.
Although Dao Sword Palace was in urgent need of manpower, True Gods below the Nirvana Realm would not be recruited. Lai Huabei was not even qualified to enter Qi Bei Region …
However, Lai Huabei was a very loyal person. He knew that the disciples of Dao Sword Palace might not be able to return, so he followed Yin Yuehuan to Qi Bei Region without hesitation.
At the same time, this kid's luck was exceptionally good. After going back and forth with Jinwu for a few times, he was not injured at all.
But as the crack spread in his direction, the light sword formed by the rays of the sun slashed towards him. With just a light sweep, he would be reduced to ashes.
Lai Huabei screamed and retreated, but no one could withstand the rays of light …
The scorching waves came and Lai Huabei almost fainted. Just as he was about to give up, a huge shadow blocked him. It was a Golden Crow Corpse Spirit.
This Golden Crow Corpse Spirit was personally created by Di Jun. It was impervious to water and fire. Even existences like Phoenix could not destroy it. It could withstand the rays of light.
"Rise!"
Under Luo Zheng's control, the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit spread its wings and rose up, blocking the crack of the umbrella.
Lai Huabei also heaved a sigh of relief and looked gratefully at "Brother Luo Zheng" in the air.
When he was in Heaven's Wall City, he got to know Brother Luo Zheng. At that time, he and Luo Zheng were both at the Great Perfection Realm. But now, Luo Zheng was an existence that he looked up to. According to Yin Yuehuan and Su Kuan, Luo Zheng even relied on his own strength to reverse the war!
What kind of concept was this …
Perhaps only existences like Donghuang could do something like this.
Being able to know Luo Zheng was probably his greatest fortune.
More and more cracks appeared on the umbrella. Luo Zheng also constantly commanded the Golden Crow Corpse Spirit to rise. The Golden Crow Corpse Spirit could spread its wings for hundreds of feet. With the advantage of its size, it was enough to block the cracks.
Not long after, over a hundred Golden Crow Corpse Spirits had all ascended into the air, and like a huge patch, they landed on the umbrella.
The expert of Taiming Mountain was still struggling to hold on. His face was getting paler and paler. Obviously, he had pushed himself to the limit.
"Buzz!"
As the rays of the nine suns grew stronger, the expert's face flashed with a touch of dark red. He roared, "I can't hold on anymore!"
A crackling sound came from the ice-blue umbrella. It seemed that it was going to break completely!
"Let me!"

Chapter 3317
The experts from the six mountains had just retreated back to the North Xuan City. Naturally, they did not have the time to construct the array.
Now, they could only rely on their own methods to resist the nine suns.
But as the nine suns expanded, the heat they released became more and more terrifying.
Jing Ping was stronger than that expert from the Great Brightness Mountain. But in just a few breaths of time after this barrier was constructed, it was already strenuous for her!
"Shua …"
The newly constructed barrier was constantly distorting and deforming. It would collapse at any moment.
"Let me do it!"
One of the experts from the Great Brightness Mountain acted. He turned into a huge piece of black silk cloth.
This silk cloth seemed to want to wrap up the sky, blocking out all the light.
But he lasted for a shorter time than Jing Ping. After five breaths of time, a layer of flame ignited on the surface of the black silk cloth. It was actually ignited!
Even though there were other experts who were eager to try and take over, they did not have much time left!
Luo Zheng looked at the teleportation array. It had been a while, yet not many people had left. The disciples of the Dao Sword Palace were located on the west side of the road. They had only moved about 30 meters or so …
At this speed, it would be good if one-third of the people in the North Xuan City could leave.
Previously, those god generals were still calm. Even though the nine suns were powerful, they could still resist against it.
But given the current situation, even if they reached the Chaotic Origin Realm, they would still be burned to ashes.
The god generals had thoughts of retreating. They wanted to escape through the teleportation array!
"Senior Di Jun, is there a way to break this Nine Sun Apocalyptic Divine Ability?" Luo Zheng asked.
He understood himself the best. Being the late Emperor of the Jinwu, Di Jun should be very familiar with the Nine Sun Apocalyptic Divine Ability.
Di Jun's illusory figure appeared. He stared at the nine suns in the sky.
Those slender eyes of his flashed with a strand of reminiscence. After a while, he replied, "There is naturally a way to break the Nine Sun Apocalyptic Divine Ability. But the cultivation technique I know … you can't use it."
"Then others can too!" Luo Zheng immediately said.
Luo Zheng might not be strong enough, but there were many Mountain Masters, Gan Gao Han, Ning Xu Yuan, and other experts present.
"They can't use it either." Di Jun said with certainty.
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng's brows immediately furrowed.
He was not afraid of being burned by the flames, but the Nirvana Realm cultivators below were in trouble …
Luo Zheng glanced around and happened to meet Yin Yuehuan's eyes. Yin Yuehuan forced a smile at Luo Zheng.
She knew that Luo Zheng was busy, so she didn't try to communicate with Luo Zheng.
At this point, Yin Yuehuan still had hope for Luo Zheng in her heart.
He could rely on his own strength to turn the tide of the battle. Perhaps he could also deal with this scorching sun?
These people who had once followed Luo Zheng had unconditional trust in Luo Zheng. It was also because of this blind trust that he, the Dao Sword Palace disciples, Su Kuan and the others were the calmest.
"Is there really no other way?" Luo Zheng asked.
Disgust flashed across Di Jun's face as she said, "There are other ways, but you shouldn't have asked me for it."
"Who are you looking for? Heavenly Lord Yuanshi? "Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
Di Jun had helped Luo Zheng a lot during the battle with Jinwu, but Heavenly Lord Yuanshi was definitely the strongest in the Instrument of Civilization.
"No, it's Hou Yi." Di Jun answered.
"Hou Yi?" Luo Zheng had seen Hou Yi in the temple before. He was the expert who had shot countless stars with his bow.
Di Jun nodded with an unconvinced look on her face. "He once used the Sun-Shooting Divine Bow to break my Nine Suns World Destroying Super Power …"
Di Jun's voice was filled with hatred when she mentioned this.
Luo Zheng could tell that there seemed to be a lot of hatred between Di Jun and Hou Yi.
However, no matter how great the hatred was, it was something that happened more than a thousand chaos eras ago.
Luo Zheng only wanted to deal with the nine suns in the sky. Without any delay, he sent his thoughts into the Inner World and communicated with Heavenly Lord Yuanshi. Heavenly Lord Yuanshi also admired Hou Yi very much, so he summoned Hou Yi out of the Instrument of Civilization …
"Buzz …"
The shadow of a long-haired man appeared above Luo Zheng's head again. Two heads appeared above Luo Zheng's head, which looked very strange from afar. However, due to the critical situation, not many people paid attention to it.
"Oh, nine suns!"
Hou Yi looked at the scorching suns in the sky and laughed cheerfully. Di Jun, who was next to him, glared at him fiercely, the corners of his eyes trembling.
Di Jun was invincible in the 77th Instrument of Civilization.
The Nine Suns World Destroying Super Power allowed him to dominate the Star Field and lead Jinwu to victory. However, he had encountered Hou Yi, who was his nemesis.
Di Jun had always regarded that battle as his humiliation.
"Senior Hou Yi, I wonder if you have a way to deal with these suns?" Luo Zheng asked respectfully.
It was not difficult to communicate with these seniors in the Instrument of Civilization. After all, from the moment they acknowledged Luo Zheng, their fates were bound to Luo Zheng's.
Hou Yi smiled and said, "It's easy. The Nine Suns World Destroying Super Power is much weaker than it was in the past!"
"It's not that far off," Di Jun muttered, "and you're in a useless state now. How can it be easy?"
The nine expanding suns in front of Hou Yi were indeed similar to the Theurgy that Emperor Jun had once used. But then again, Hou Yi was not here in person. Like Emperor Jun, he was just a wisp of spirit attached to the Instrument of Civilization.
However, Hou Yi was still full of confidence. He asked, "Luo Zheng, are you not afraid of fire?"
"I'm not afraid of fire. However, the Nine Suns World Destroying Super Power is too powerful. I may not be able to bear it," Luo Zheng replied.
Hou Yi waved his hand gently. As the Instrument of Civilization in Luo Zheng's body fluctuated, a golden longbow appeared beside Luo Zheng. Hou Yi shrugged and said, "Then it's a little troublesome. I can't come in person. Although I can give you Divine Sense, your strength is not enough.
At this distance, you have no choice but to go close to those suns. "
"Go close to them … …"
Luo Zheng looked up at the sky. Although he was very confident in his physical body, at least he had never been hurt by fire before.
However, these nine suns were the Divine Sense that Di Jun had used with all his strength. It was incomparable to the ordinary Golden Crow Divine Fire … …
Hesitation flashed across his face. Given the current situation, Luo Zheng had no other choice. He could only say, "I'll go!"
Whoosh!
Luo Zheng stepped on the back of a Golden Crow Corpse Spirit and flew into the sky.

"You're going?"
This time, not only Gan Gaohan, but also the experts on the mountains were puzzled.

Chapter 3318

"Whoosh!"

He was not sure if he could withstand it …

That god general was an outstanding expert amongst the many god generals. He had once come out from the Second Floor of the Land of Sword Grief. Li Bianxue flipped in midair and continued stabbing out with her thin sword.

That god general was very brave in the battle with the Jinwu. He guarded the Beichi Pass where he was at and did not retreat. Now, even escaping was an extravagant hope. Moreover, he was forced to retreat by Li Bianxue's sword light. He was filled with grief and indignation.

Li Bianxue was shocked. This god general's status and identity were not low. She did not kill him and only forced him to retreat. She did not expect him to be so extreme as to fight with his life!
One could imagine the power of a punch that burned the Paramita token. Even someone as strong as Li Bianxue could not take it head-on.

The experts on the various mountains were all paying attention to the sun in the sky. They were all racking their brains to open a layer of enchantment to buy time for the people from the Heavenly Palace to retreat. Even the people from the Jinwu joined in.

Chapter 3319
Of course, Luo Zheng did not know what had happened in Beixuan City. He kept climbing and was getting closer and closer to one of the suns.

The energy in the Chaos doesn't come from the Chaos …

From something as small as a mortal lighting a torch to something as big as a Nirvana Realm expert shattering the heavens and earth, the energy was all related to the outside of Chaos. However, the level of energy was still too small, so it was impossible for people to notice this point. However, when the level of energy reached a certain level, the things that appeared before people's eyes were different.
Some things could only be observed after a qualitative change occurred.

His physical body was not releasing energy, but devouring energy. How could this be explained?

This proved that the Heavenly Primogenitor was right!
This was also the reason why many powerful human experts were willing to follow the Heavenly Primogenitor.

"Weng!"
Just as this layer of imprint disappeared, Luo Zheng pulled the bowstring by half a chi. But after half a chi, the bowstring could not be pulled anymore …
Hou Yi chanted words once again. These words seemed to have been passed down from the 77th Primal Chaos. Every word was filled with energy.
"Weng!"
The golden imprint once again enveloped Luo Zheng. When the imprint disappeared, Luo Zheng pulled the golden bow by half a chi again.

This time, the Sun Chasing Divine Technique was pulled to its limit.

Some formidable bows did not require arrows. They could simply condense the void.

The corner of Di Jun's eyes twitched. He still felt that it was unforgivable for him to be defeated by such an idiot.

Di Jun's Divine Ability was completely restrained by this point.

Chapter 3320

Chapter 3321

"It should be! But why did he do that? "

Many of them were trying to figure out the reason behind it.
They did not know how Luo Zheng captured those Jinwu natives. But since those Jinwu natives were so obedient in front of Luo Zheng, they naturally thought of them as' captives'.
Quite a number of people believed that Emperor Jun was being lenient.
But there were still some who disagreed.
"Did Luo Zheng make a move and extinguish the sun?"
"I saw him leaving the North Xuan City and flying towards those suns …"
"How is that possible! Luo Zheng is indeed powerful. But he is just an ordinary Chaotic Origin Realm expert. Those suns … even the Mountain Lords could not do anything about it. How could he? "
Even though many had seen Luo Zheng flying towards those suns, the majority did not think that Luo Zheng had the capability to do so.
"I believe it's Sir Banner Master," Yue Bai Hao suddenly said.
"I, I believe it too!" Lai Huabei nodded with certainty.
Yin Yuehuan looked at the blazing white sky with a complicated expression. The glaring light caused her eyes to turn red. She said faintly, "If anyone could create this miracle, that person must be Luo Zheng!"
"Weng …"
At this moment, another sun disappeared from the sky. This was the fourth sun to be extinguished.
As the suns were extinguished one by one, the pressure on those experts maintaining the barrier was also decreasing.
Originally, a barrier could only last for a few breaths of time. But now, it had been extended to more than 20 breaths of time. The others were able to maintain the barrier with ease, giving them time to replenish their Deity Crystals.
This way, everyone could continue maintaining the barrier, allowing the people in the North Xuan City to survive.
The sixth …
The seventh …
The eighth …
The ninth …
The blazing white light dimmed layer by layer as the sun was extinguished. When the last sun was extinguished, Qi Bei Province instantly returned to normal.
At the same time, everyone saw a black dot flash in the sky. That was the black light emitted when the sun was extinguished. There had been black light before, but it was covered by the light of the other suns. Naturally, they could not see it.
At the same time, the people in Beixuan City also saw a figure the size of an ant floating in the sky.
"Someone's here!"
"Who's there?!"
"When the sun was extinguished, this person was right beside me. Could it be that he was the one who extinguished the sun?"
The people in Beixuan City became agitated once again. Those with strong eyesight stared fixedly at the black dot.
Ning Xuyuan, Gan Gaohan, the leaders of Taixiu Mountain, Taijin Mountain, and the other mountains all had extremely complicated expressions on their faces.
"It's Luo Zheng …"
"It's really him!"
"This man is the savior of our Heavenly Palace!"
Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, and the others were even more excited, especially Qiu Yinhe. His face was glowing, and his emotions were indescribable.
As Luo Zheng descended with his golden bow, more and more people saw Luo Zheng's true face.
"It's Luo Zheng!"
"Oh my god, he did it. He really extinguished the sun!"
"How is this possible …"
From Luo Zheng's point of view, it was easy for him to extinguish the nine suns. However, in the eyes of others, this kind of thing could not be measured by common sense. It could only be called a miracle.
After all, Luo Zheng's cultivation was too far away from Di Jun's …
Even the wisest people, Ning Xuyuan and Gan Gaohan, did not know that Di Jun had an insurmountable nemesis more than a thousand divine eras ago. And this nemesis defeated Di Jun at a very small price.
As for Phoenix, Ming Shi, and the others in Jinwu, they thought of another problem. Di Jun, who was living in Luo Zheng's body, was very familiar with the Nine Suns Destruction. He must have given him the means to extinguish the sun.
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng descended from the sky and landed in the center of Beixuan City.
"Wow …"
Applause swept back and forth in Beixuan City like a wave.
After going through a life and death situation, everyone's eyes were filled with gratitude. The applause could be said to be from the bottom of their hearts.
People like Lai Huabei, Su Kuan, and Yin Yuehuan rushed up in excitement and hugged Luo Zheng layer by layer. They were unwilling to let go for a long time.
Li Bianxue was rather puzzled when she saw this scene. After all, Luo Zheng was a disciple of the Land of Sword Grief. How could he know those in the Nirvana Realm? After asking around, the surprise in her eyes grew. She did not expect Luo Zheng to have advanced step by step from Dragon City …
…
Since there was the word "World Destruction" in the supernatural power, it was enough to show the power of the Nine Suns Destruction.
A layer of the inner surface of the entire Qi Bei Province was cut off …
The living beings on the surface of the Qi Bei Province, from the ants in the cracks of the rocks to the rare beasts, were all burned to ashes.
Only a small number of living beings who hid deep underground were spared.
Evil Deities was hiding deep underground and was still in a meditative state. He did not know that such a huge change had happened in the outside world.
This was also because Evil Deities was lucky to have chosen a place far away from Beixuan City.
In the southwest of the Qi Bei Province, even deep underground was not safe.
The nine suns were actually standing high in the sky in the southwest and descending from above. In other words, their target was Beixuan City.
When the suns were extinguished, it turned into a strange scene.
The ground around Beixuan City was burned and baked, turning into layers of flowing magma. Standing on the city wall of Beixuan City, one could see this vast sea of magma. Beixuan City had become a lonely boat. Standing on the city wall, one could feel the pressing heat from the magma.
After surviving this calamity, the mountain masters of the various mountains brought their men and retreated to the Center God Province.
The southwest of the Qi Bei Province was already a sea of magma. It would take many years for the magma to solidify.
The magma naturally could not trap everyone. Everyone rose into the air and left the ruined Beixuan City.
Gan Gaohan flew at the front, thinking about what Jinwu would do next …
Luo Zheng had almost taken away all the core people of Jinwu. It was not difficult to guess Di Jun's reaction after knowing this news. It would not be long before he aggressively came to kill his way over.
With Di Jun's strength, who in the Greater One Heavenly Palace could stop him …
Thinking of this, Gan Gaohan felt a headache.
While Gan Gaohan was having a headache, the golden sword at his waist flashed. A wisp of consciousness passed through the golden sword and entered his mind. It was a distress signal from the Greater One Heavenly Palace.

Chapter 3323
The Center God Province had always been the bottom line of the Greater One Heavenly Palace.
In the face of the invasion of the Jinwu, Gan Gaohan also chose to lead the soldiers of the Heavenly Palace to attack the Jinwu in the Qbei Province.
They did not want the Jinwu to wreak havoc in the Center God Province.
But they did not expect that when the Jinwu invaded, the You Xiong Clan also sent another group of people to the southeast.
The southeast of the Center God Province had always been deserted, and from time to time, there would be invasions by the Savage Tribes.
Later, the Greater One Heavenly Palace set up a checkpoint in the southeast of the Center God Province, and set up a Blood Mandate Sword Formation. This sword formation was set up on a cliff, and this cliff was called the Dragon Cliff.
For thousands of years, the expert guarding the Dragon Cliff was called Tian Jiedu.
In the last thousand years, the person guarding the Dragon Cliff was called Lin Ruyuan.
Like Luo Zheng, Lin Ruyuan also had a perfect compatibility with the Eternal Sword Fate. As a direct descendant of the Tai Yi Mountain Clan, the treatment he received in the clan was close to that of Ling Shuang. However, 200 years ago, Lin Ruyuan was embroiled in a dispute within the Heavenly Palace, and his partner, Qin Hua, died because of it.
Since then, Lin Ruyuan took the initiative to apply to go to the Dragon Cliff, and for the past 200 years, he had been guarding the Dragon Cliff.
Since he guarded the Dragon Cliff, the Savage Tribes in the southwest rarely dared to invade, and those who dared to invade were all suppressed by him with bloody means …
Since the Jinwu invaded from the northeast, the people of the Greater One Heavenly Palace were anxious, and even the soldiers on the Dragon Cliff were worried and discussed this matter all day long.
"This time, the Jinwu came with the momentum, I don't know if the Seven Mountains can be defended."
"We have to defend even if we can't, we have to retreat from the Guanshan Province, all the way to the Qbei Province, and then even the Center God Province, why should we!"
"I made an application to go to the Qbei Province to participate in the battle, but was rejected …"
"There's no other way, fighting against the Jinwu is important, but guarding the Dragon Cliff is equally important. If we leave, what if the Savage Tribes take advantage of the situation to stir up trouble?"
Just as the soldiers were discussing, a man in a green robe floated over from afar.
The soldiers immediately stood up and saluted the man with cupped fists.
"Jiedu Daren, is there any news from Qi Bei Region?"
"Does our Heavenly Palace have an advantage?"
"Should we ambush Jinwu in Qi Bei Province …"
The azure-clothed man was Tian Jiedushi Lin Ruyuan.
The soldiers were paying attention to the news from the north, and so was Lin Ruyuan.
However, as they were separated by a large province, there was still a delay in the transmission of information. Even Lin Ruyuan had to use the sound transmission token to transmit information one after another.
Lin Ruyuan shook his head. "The news I received is that they are still fighting. The specific situation is unknown."
When the soldiers heard Lin Ruyuan's words, they were all dejected.
"Lord Jiedu, it doesn't matter whether we defend the Dragon Cliff or not. In any case, those Savage Tribes won't dare to enter the Center God Province!"
"Yes, how about we go to the north?"
"…"
Lin Ruyuan's face darkened when he heard his subordinate's words. "Nonsense! Let's not talk about whether we can leave or not. Even if we do, how much help can you provide with your meager strength? "
Although Lin Ruyuan was a rare genius of Taiyi Mountain, his foundation was still shallow. He was slightly stronger than Li Bianxue, but he could not be compared to Qiu Yinhe.
Even if he led these people to help, it would only be a drop in the bucket. They would not be able to change the entire situation.
After being scolded by the Tian Jiedushi, these soldiers immediately became obedient. They all silently looked at the mountains and rivers in the distance.
At this moment, some of the patterns at the front of the Dragon Cliff suddenly released rays of red light.
When Lin Ruyuan and the soldiers saw the red light, they all frowned.
"Those Savage Tribes really know how to pick a time to attack our Dragon Cliff!" A soldier stood up fiercely.
Another soldier pulled out his sword. "Hmph, I have nowhere to vent my anger. They came at the right time!"
The soldiers present often slaughtered the Savage Tribes invading them. It was already a common occurrence for them.
When Lin Ruyuan saw the red light, he frowned and did not give any orders. Instead, he stood there thinking about something. After a while, he said, "The situation is not good …"
"Sir Jiedushi, what's wrong?" A tall and sturdy soldier carrying a huge sword asked.
Normally, at this time, Lin Ruyuan would be swift and decisive. He would lead many soldiers to fight the Savage Tribes on the Dragon Cliff. Why would he be afraid today? He could not imagine Lin Ruyuan's timid appearance.
Lin Ruyuan pointed at the red light and said, "The bone formation, dream formation, and spirit formation have all been forcefully destroyed. But what about the secret formation, picture formation, and nether formation?"
The others also sensed that something was wrong. They focused their gazes on the red light, and their expressions immediately became serious.
The bone formation, dream formation, and spirit formation were ranked behind the secret formation, picture formation, and nether formation. The first three formations had been forcefully destroyed. This meant that the secret formation, picture formation, and nether formation had also been destroyed. However, they could not see the condition of these three formations.
This meant that the secret formation, picture formation, and nether formation had been destroyed by someone …
Those Savage Tribes had all kinds of powerful talents, but their intelligence was generally inferior to humans. They could not break such a complicated formation.
These red lights reflected a problem — there was an extremely powerful formation breaker among the invaders.
In other words, the invaders were not Savage Tribes!
In this sensitive period, this small piece of news was enough for them to be vigilant!
"Blood Command Sword Formation, open," Lin Ruyuan immediately issued an order.
The Blood Command Sword Formation was the largest sword formation on the Dragon Cliff. Since the Dragon Cliff was built, it had only been activated three times, and all of them were not long after the Greater One Heavenly Palace was built. After the Eastern Sovereign personally eradicated the three Savage Tribes, the Blood Command Sword Formation had never been activated again.
"Weng …"
Streaks of blood-red light surrounded the entire Dragon Cliff and flickered.
This huge cliff was like a giant dragon stretching its neck to look at the sky. When the Blood Command Sword Formation was activated, under the red light, it looked even more like it.
A vast aura rose up along the Dragon Cliff. Lin Ruyuan stood alone on the spot, his face still solemn.
In just three to five breaths of time, three thousand feet away from the Dragon Cliff, a figure appeared.
After seeing those people clearly, Lin Ruyuan closed his eyes tightly and took a deep breath. A strong sense of relief arose spontaneously.
Almost without thinking, he guessed that those people were from You Xiong or Shen Nong. It was impossible for the soldiers of the Dragon Cliff to defend it.
"Generals of the Dragon Cliff, leave through the transmission formation and return to the Heavenly Palace," Lin Ruyuan ordered.
The soldiers gasped when they saw the enemy clearly, but they felt that there was still a chance to fight by relying on the Dragon Cliff.
After hearing Lin Ruyuan's order, everyone was stunned for a moment before they reacted.
"I'm not leaving!"
"If you want to leave, you can leave. In any case, I don't plan to leave!"

Chapter 3324
These soldiers already understood that the invaders were extremely powerful, far from what the dragon cliff could resist.
Lin Ruyuan knew this, so he let his subordinates leave first.
At least, after keeping them alive, he let them guard the seven mountains …
But Lin Ruyuan ignored the determination of the soldiers, none of them were willing to leave first.
"If Lord Jiedu is not willing to fight with us, then please kill us!" The tall man said with a determined expression.
Lin Ruyuan looked at them in a daze. After a while, he said, "Zhao Di, go and destroy the Transmission Array. Before you destroy it, you can all leave through the Transmission Array. I want you to go back to guard the Seven Mountains. I definitely don't want you to be deserters!"
How could they not understand this, but still, none of them chose to leave, until Zhao Di went to destroy the teleportation formation, they had no way out …
Nearby, a white-clothed old man carrying a black pole slowly approached. He called out with a hoarse voice, "I've long heard that the one guarding the Dragon Cliff is the Taiyi Mountain's genius, Lin Ruyuan. Are you the esteemed one?"
Lin Ruyuan unsheathed his three-foot long sword, the entire dragon cliff flashed with red light, the Blood Mandate Sword Formation's sharpness appeared, "Yes, so what?"
"We are all humans, why do we have to fight with our own race, You Xiong Clan is in need of people, submit to us, and you will have eternal life!" The old man said.
At Lin Ruyuan's level, he naturally understood You Xiong Clan's goal, this so-called eternal life was indeed a good hope, but how could Lin Ruyuan be persuaded to surrender?
He sneered, "What a joke! My Taiyi Heavenly Palace is the orthodox human race, how can we surrender to barbarians like you? "
The old man did not think he could persuade Lin Ruyuan, he indifferently looked at the Blood Mandate Sword Formation around the dragon cliff, he stretched out a finger, "Charge through from the center, breaking the formation is easy for me …"
…
About the time it took for three incense sticks to burn later …
The old man had already arrived in front of the broken teleportation formation behind the dragon cliff, he frowned and sized up the teleportation formation.
"It is very damaged, even if it is repaired, it should not be able to create an accurate spatial passage," the old man said.
There was a masked person beside the old man. His skin was pale green, and he said expressionlessly, "Once we enter the Center God Province, we can go straight in. Time is not important."
"Let's go." The other person passed by the teleportation portal and flew straight ahead.
The old man waited for the other dozens of people to fly away, then gently shook the fishing rod on his shoulder. Lin Ruyuan's head was hanging at the front of the fishing rod. Lin Ruyuan's head had been cut off, but his handsome face still had a fierce glint as he stared fiercely at the old man.
"My speed in breaking the formation was fast enough, but you were still able to rely on a sword and a broken formation to kill three of us." The old man sighed. "Youngsters these days really can't be underestimated."
"Da da da …"
After saying that, he took a step forward and leaped into the air. Lin Ruyuan's head hung on the fishing line and floated behind him.
After the old man caught up with the team at an extremely fast speed, a tall person asked, "Although there are seven mountains in the Greater One Heavenly Palace, our target should be the Greater One Mountain, right?"
The old man nodded. "The greatest hidden danger of the Greater One Heavenly Palace is the East Emperor himself. If he can break free from the restraints and awaken, all of us will die."
"Since he is trapped on the other side, it is best to kill him first," the tall person said.
"Of course, I will lead people to attack the Greater One Palace," the old man said.
The Greater One Mountain was densely covered with great formations. Even if it was an expert, it would be difficult to break the formations and enter.
But this old man was an extraordinary person of the You Xiong Clan. His talent was to break formations. There were thousands of strange formations in the world, and there was no formation that he could not break.
And Xu Shou had given most of the information on the Greater One Mountain to the old man. He knew the Greater One Mountain like the back of his hand, and naturally felt that it would not be difficult to break into the Greater One Mountain …
After the teleportation portal was broken, it did slow down their speed.
But not long after entering the Center Divine Province, they found the Jinglong City in the Southwest.
There were also people of the Greater One Heavenly Palace guarding the Jinglong City. But facing the experts of the You Xiong Clan, they did not have any room to resist. They were all killed in the blink of an eye.
Some people wanted to destroy the teleportation portal in the Jinglong City again, but in the end it was all in vain.
After the You Xiong Clan's group passed through the teleportation portal, they reached the mountain city under the Tai Yue Mountain. After another teleportation, they reached the Dragon City!
The teleportation portal of the Dragon City was outside the Hidden Dragon Pavilion, and was also guarded by several Tai Yiwei.
During wartime, the entire Center Divine Province was under martial law. All the large and small formations of the Greater One Mountain were all activated. Even so, those Tai Yiwei did not think that these people would enter the Dragon City through the teleportation portal!
When they saw the old man and the other You Xiong Clan members, they were all stunned.
"Who are you? Show me the sword token of the Heavenly Palace …" A Tai Yiwei shouted.
Before he finished his words, a thin fishing line was wrapped around his neck. The old man's arm trembled slightly, and this Tai Yiwei's head was separated from his body.
The other Tai Yiwei were alarmed. Some of them shouted and warned, some of them took out their Paramita Token and fought with their lives, and some of them chose to run away …
But facing these experts, no matter how they reacted, no matter how they chose to deal with them, it was meaningless.
They just casually attacked, and many Tai Yiwei were turned into pieces …
The death of these Tai Yiwei alarmed even more people.
For example, Wen Si, who was guarding the Sword Training Tower, Guchen, who was guarding the Coiling Dragon Ancient Road, and other experts who were stationed in the Dragon City all made their moves.
These people were much stronger than the Tai Yiwei, but these people from the You Xiong Clan were all on the same level as Qiu Yinhe. How could they put these people in their eyes?
In the time it took for a stick of incense to burn, the entire Dragon City was bathed in blood.
The old man's wrinkled face looked somewhat bored. He looked up at the Greater One Mountain that he couldn't see the top of. The Great Mountain Guarding Formation had been activated.
"Compared to slaughtering, I prefer breaking formations …"
As he spoke, he carried the fishing rod and walked toward the Coiling Dragon Ancient Road.
Blazing flames were ignited everywhere in the Dragon City.
Places like the True Intent Sword Pavilion and the Famed Sword Tower were all burned to the ground …
After the wanton slaughter and burning of the Dragon City, those people flew up and followed behind the old man.
The old man climbed up the Greater One Mountain and broke formations along the way.
No matter how powerful the formations on the Greater One Mountain were, he was able to solve them directly.
In less than two hours, he had climbed up the Coiling Dragon Ancient Road.
He didn't lead the group toward the three major sword sects. Whether it was the Heart Flow Sword Sect or the Murdering Sword Sect, they had no interest in them.

Chapter 3325
When the You Xiong Clan attacked the Dragon Cliff, the news had spread to the Seven Mountains.
The formations and boundaries in the Seven Mountains had been opened but the disciples of the major sword sects were still in a state of panic.
In the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
Xiao Yin, dressed in a white dress, was shuttling back and forth between the courtyards. After gently pushing open the courtyard door, Ning Yudie, who was standing in the middle of the courtyard, stepped forward and asked with a concerned expression, "How is it?"
Jinwu came too suddenly. Luo Zheng was afraid that Ning Yudie and the others would be worried so he did not go up the mountain when he went from Dragon City to Qi Bei Province.
Ning Yudie only found out later that Jinwu and the Heavenly Palace were fighting and Luo Zheng had joined Tai Yiwei to fight against Jinwu.
It would be a lie to say that she was not worried but after experiencing so much with Luo Zheng, she knew Luo Zheng's ability to resolve a crisis. She believed that Luo Zheng could return safely.
"The situation is not good," Xiaoyin said with a pout.
"What situation is not good?" Ning Yudie's heart clenched.
Xiaoyin hesitated for a moment before saying, "I heard that in Qi Bei Province, Di Jun released the Nine Suns Apocalypse Theurgy and burned everything in Qi Bei Province …"
This news was said by those who escaped from the teleportation formation. Later, the teleportation channel was destroyed and they thought that Beixuan City had been burned down and the strong cultivators of the Seven Mountains had died.
"Nine Suns Apocalypse Theurgy?" Ning Yudie's eyes narrowed.
Xiaoyin nodded and said, "That's Theurgy of Di Jun. Young master Luo Zheng will probably have a hard time, very hard time …"
Hearing the terrifying rumors, Xiaoyin felt that Luo Zheng had probably died in Qi Bei Province with the other strong cultivators.
Young master Luo Zheng was powerful but his growth was limited in a short time. Even strong cultivators like Qiu Yinhe and Gan Gaohan had died in Qi Bei Province. How could Luo Zheng be spared?
"Okay, I got it." Ning Yudie nodded.
Xiaoyin noticed that Ning Yudie's expression remained calm. She found it strange. Of course, she knew that Ning Yudie and Luo Zheng had a deep relationship. After hearing this news, Ning Yudie couldn't possibly remain so calm.
Although Ning Yudie was worried, she had never seen Luo Zheng afraid of any kind of flame in all these years.
If it was really a scorching sun, other people might not survive, but Luo Zheng had a very high chance of surviving …
At this moment, another woman in purple walked over and asked hurriedly, "Sister Yin, is there any news about my husband?"
The woman in purple was Xiuzhu. After Luo Zheng released her from his Inner World, he did not take her back and allowed her to live in the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
Although the Evil Deities had become Luo Zheng's body, Xiuzhu was still very concerned about the Evil Deities.
In Xiuzhu's eyes, as long as the Evil Deities did not betray the "Lord", they would eventually have a bright future.
The women in the courtyard had seen the Evil Deities and had an almost instinctive dislike for the Evil Deities. Therefore, even Xiuzhu was hated. Xiaoyin glanced coldly at Xiuzhu and said, "He's a nameless nobody. How would I know? He most likely died in Qi Bei.
In Qi Bei Province. "
Hearing Xiaoyin's answer, Xiuzhu's shoulders trembled slightly and her eyes turned red. She forced a smile and turned around.
Xi Youqin stood on the second floor and heard Xiaoyin's words clearly.
If it was in the past, Xi Youqin would inevitably burst into tears and worry.
But after being pregnant, her state of mind had calmed down a lot. She had followed Luo Zheng's instructions one by one, wholeheartedly for the sake of the fetus in her belly.
Her face paled slightly, pursed her lips, and her expression returned to normal. She believed that Luo Zheng would come back.
Not long after Xiaoyin came in, the ground suddenly shook.
"Rumble — —"
A dragon-like bolt of lightning exploded in the distance, and a layer of heaven and earth seemed to have been cut by this bolt of lightning.
Even a colossus like Greater One Mountain began to shake, like a giant beast trying to shake off the lice on its body.
Xi Youqin, who was on the second floor, was unusually nimble. She had already supported herself on the railing, staring at the back of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. The lightning came from behind the mountain, where the Taishi Palace was located.
Greater One Mountain had entered a state of alert, and the Taishi Palace, as the core of the Heavenly Palace, was even more so. After all, the East Sovereign was protected in the Taishi Palace.
If someone broke into the Taishi Palace and killed the East Sovereign, the greatest pillar of the Greater One Heavenly Palace would really be broken.
Above the white mist, there were more than sixty people floating, led by an old man carrying a fishing rod.
The one who had just released the lightning was a red-haired man. Bolts of lightning were still drilling around in the red-haired man's palm. He said with a smile, "Elder Wei has been unstoppable along the way, but to think that he would be stopped by this white mist. I really didn't expect!"
The red-haired man was called Zhu Hongkuan, and was the younger brother of the fire envoy of the Youxiong clan.
Elder Wei glared at Zhu Hongkuan and sneered, "Although the previous formations were powerful, their techniques were not brilliant, and breaking them was child's play. The Nightmare Domain of the Absolute Beginning was personally set up by the East Sovereign, how could it be so easy to break it?"
Elder Wei despised Zhu Hongkuan's action of releasing the lightning and randomly attacking the Nightmare Domain of the Absolute Beginning. A boorish person will always be a boorish person. If Zhu Hongkuan did not have a responsible elder brother, he would not be qualified to stand with him!
After Elder Wei's disdain, he lightly flicked the fishing rod, and the thin and long fishing line disappeared into the mist.
"This Absolute Beginning Mirage is set up using the eighteen pillars of the Taishi Palace. If you cannot find those eighteen pillars, you may be trapped inside for the rest of your life," Elder Wei said with a smile.
In the past, the Greater One Heavenly Palace and the Jinwu Palace were on good terms, and the East Sovereign and Di Jun were like brothers. Some secrets were not hidden from the two clans, and this gave Elder Wei the confidence to break the formation.
Everyone in the Taishi Palace was uneasy.
The lightning that had just descended from the sky was enough to prove the strength of the other party.
Fortunately, the Absolute Beginning Mirage was not so easy to break. The lightning had all landed outside the Taishi Palace, and the Taishi Palace itself was unharmed.
But could the Absolute Beginning Mirage really stop the experts outside?
Most of them were in despair.
The Taishi Palace was surrounded by three layers inside and three layers outside.
On the outermost layer were thousands of golden armoured guards.
Most of these golden armoured guards were only five star Tai Yiwei.
Concubine Yan, who was standing in front of the palace, knew very well that if the Taishi Palace was broken, these golden armoured guards would not even be considered cannon fodder.
She raised her head, her gaze easily piercing through the layers of white mist and landing on the experts of the You Xiong Clan.
"Divide the army into two, one to take the North State, the other to attack the Center God Province …" Concubine Yan sighed.
This was not a strategy at all …
It was just that the Greater One Heavenly Palace was lacking in soldiers and generals, and facing the alliance of several large clans, they had no power to fight back.
"Those experts are invading, what should we do? How about we move the East Sovereign away?"

Chapter 3326
Consort Qin was usually arrogant in the Taishi Palace. Other than the East Sovereign, she was not afraid of anything.
When the real crisis was imminent, Consort Qin was out of her wits.
"Mother!"
Even Chun Hao, Chun Xuan, and Chun Yuan, who were sitting on the steps, could not stand it anymore.
Although the three brothers were very selfish, they were still the sons of the East Sovereign.
Now that it was a matter of life and death, they were indifferent.
They could sit on such a high position because the East Sovereign was their father. Now that the East Sovereign was in danger of dying, they naturally had to protect him to the end.
"Am I not thinking of a way?" Consort Qin glared at her sons.
Chun Hao calmly advised, "Those strong people are already outside the Realm of the Absolute Beginning. We can't move even if we want to now. We have no way out!"
"If they attack, we should fight to the death!" Chun Xuan said seriously.
Those sitting on the steps were all princes and imperial grandchildren …
Feng Ge was also sitting by the side. His elbow was on his knee and supporting his face. His other hand was holding a thin piece of paper. The paper was full of words that were fluttering in the wind.
Hearing Chun Xuan's words, she only glanced at him indifferently.
In the face of disaster, these three brothers still had a backbone. They were indeed worthy of being his half-brothers. They were much better than their mother.
Feng Ge regretted listening to Luo Zheng's words. No matter how exquisite the Enlightenment piece was, it was a piece of waste paper at this time. Not to mention that Imperial Father could not see it. Even if he did, what was the use?
Could it allow these people to defeat those above?
In the end, the Enlightenment piece could not be sent to Imperial Father and he could not go to North Region.
The tragic situation of the battle in North Region was continuously transmitted to the Taishi Palace. Feng Ge naturally received the news first hand. As a "female demon", she was even more clear about the horror of the nine suns destroying the world.
The strong people of the seven mountains were probably all wiped out, right?
The only exception should be Luo Zheng. She did not know if he could survive …

Such an expert could easily split mountains and split the earth with a casual strike. This elbow strike of his contained a strength of more than 10,000 divine jun!

"Feng Ge!"
Concubine Yan let out a shrill scream. Her eyes were about to burst.

As he released the soul attack, two short blades flew out of his hands and shot toward Concubine Yan's face.
Concubine Yan was unable to resist the soul attack, so she could not dodge the two short blades.
Just as the short blades were about to pierce through her head, a flying sword came from afar.
The flying sword flashed, as if it was constantly shuttling through space.
"Ding! Ding!"
The flying sword actually accurately hit the two short blades, causing them to bounce away.
"Who is it!"
The black-robed man's face showed anger.
He thought that the You Xiong Clan had the situation under control. The Black Jade Clan only needed to deal with the East Emperor's family members. He never thought that there would be other variables.
But when he looked in the direction where the flying sword came from, he was stunned …
In the distance, Gan Gaohan led the experts of the other six mountains and approached quickly. Beside Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan had three Squinted Eyes. The flying sword just now was released by Ning Xuyuan.
He was not the only one who was stunned.
Zhu Hongkuan and the others were watching the show in the sky. Suddenly, they saw so many people from the Taiyi Heavenly Palace and were stunned.
"Wasn't the main force of the Heavenly Palace suppressed by the Jinwu?"
"Logically speaking, the Jinwu has the ability to destroy them all!"
"But how could they return …"
In terms of numbers, the You Xiong Clan only had 40 people, but the Taiyi Heavenly Palace had twice as many.
In terms of strength, although the You Xiong Clan was powerful, they were not the strongest of the You Xiong Clan!
Now, it was the You Xiong Clan's turn to despair …
Just as they were in despair, another group of people rushed over from the other side.
When Zhu Hongkuan and the others took a closer look, they were overjoyed. It was the Jinwu people!
And they saw Ming Shi and Bai Hu among the Jinwu people!
"It's the Jinwu!"

Chapter 3331

Powerful energy was constantly surging.

Feng Ge's physical body was able to withstand this attack, but his soul seemed to have been severely injured from the shock …

While Concubine Yan was helping Feng Ge nurture his soul, Xun, Han Liusu, and Mu Ning had the opportunity to look up and observe.

After the people of the Jinwu took the lead in attacking, Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan also led the powerhouses of the Heavenly Palace to attack.

These seven pieces of white porcelain seemed to be a whole. When combined, they could form a unique round piece of porcelain.
When these pieces of white porcelain were used, they could easily create layers of thick crystal walls. Under the protection of these crystal walls, they provided shelter for the You Xiong Clan.
After being chased for a full 30 minutes, the You Xiong Clan only lost five people.
However, these unique crystal walls could only delay, but could not really stop the people of the Jinwu and Gan Gaohan. As long as the Prison of Heaven and Earth was not released, these people of the You Xiong Clan would eventually lose their lives here.
Despair was spreading among Zhu Hongkuan's group …
At the same time, in the northwest of the Center God Province.
Due to the urgency of the situation, the Tai Yiwei and the Nirvana Realm cultivators chose to stay in Huyan City and wait for orders.
The rest of the battle did not have much to do with them. These Tai Yiwei and Nirvana Realm cultivators were very relaxed. After all, they had just escaped death not long ago.

Just as they were discussing with indignation, strange cubes suddenly appeared in the sky above Huyan City.
Those cubes emitted a dazzling blue light, which immediately attracted everyone's attention.
After a while, the cubes gradually turned into a human figure. A young man with wings appeared out of thin air. This young man had long eyebrows and thin eyes. He was as delicate as a girl. He was Di Jun himself.
After Di Jun appeared, he only glanced at the people below Huyan City …
The tens of thousands of soldiers in Huyan City, whether it was the Tai Yiwei or the Nirvana Realm cultivators, seemed to have fallen into an ice cellar. They could not move at all.
In front of this young man, everything in the world was as small as dust, as insignificant as ants …
This was the strength of a supreme expert like Di Jun. He did not need to reveal his identity, nor did he need to reveal his aura. Everything was just a look.
After Di Jun retracted his gaze, he stretched out his white palm. A small cubes appeared in his palm. It seemed as if there was only one side of the world in the cubes. The world in the cubes reflected the people who were fighting in the Greater One Heavenly Palace.
His personal guards and the experts of the Greater One Heavenly Palace were chasing after the You Xiong Clan.
"Phoenix, Ming Shi, Bai Hu, why did you betray me?"
Di Jun's face revealed a puzzled expression.
He would not be angry because of the betrayal of his subordinates.
This was because Di Jun was very clear that under the blessing of the bloodline worship, Phoenix, Ming Shi, and the others were absolutely loyal to him. There was no possibility of them betraying him.
Suddenly changing sides, there must be something fishy about it!
The confusion that this mystery brought to Di Jun was almost on the same level as Luo Zheng's arrow that destroyed the sun!
Now that he had personally come to the Center God Province, the truth would naturally be revealed.
He dragged the "Cubes World" with one hand and reached out with the other. A Nirvana Realm Token appeared in his right hand. It was a long blue spear. The spear tip and the spear body were translucent, as if it was made of ice.
This was the Star Mark Spear, excavated from the 33 Levels of Heaven. It was a unique Nirvana Realm Token.
"Starlight Token."
Di Jun muttered silently.

Chapter 3332
The mountain lords looked at the stars floating above their heads in astonishment.

People like Emperor Jun wouldn't fight for tens of thousands of years. Although he had been carrying the Star Mark Spear for thousands of years, the outside world knew nothing about it. Only a few people close to Emperor Jun knew about it, such as Phoenix.
So when they saw the stars, they knew that Emperor Jun had come to the Center God Province.

Small thunderstorms wrapped around him. The thunderstorms formed an absolute energy zone around him. Anything that passed through this zone would be torn to pieces.

The mountain lords of Mount Tai Ming and Mount Tai Qingshan also activated their protective skills.

"Pu!"
The blue spear pierced through the Mountain Lord's body.

The tip of the spear didn't even leave his body before it strangely disappeared. In the next instant, it had already entered the body of Mount Taixiu's Lord.
"Pu!"
Penetrate, disappear, reappear …
In another instant, the spear pierced through the Mountain Lord of Mount Great Gold once again …
…
There was a sequence to killing the four Mountain Chieftains.

At this point, the others finally saw his face. It was the Demon Emperor Jun of Jinwu!
Zhu Hongkuan and the others who were being hunted down kept wailing. When they saw Emperor Jun suddenly appear, it was as if they saw a glimmer of hope in a hopeless situation.

In contrast, the atmosphere at Taiyi Heavenly Palace instantly fell to the freezing point.
Unless Elder Ying or Donghuang personally took action, no one could do anything to an existence like Emperor Jun …
If they were fully prepared, the six Mountain Chieftains might have a chance to fight him.
However, Emperor Jun had already made his move and killed the Mountain Chieftains of the four Mountain Chieftains in an instant.

Emperor Jun floated in the sky. As he slowly spread his wings, the shadow of a golden disc appeared behind him. It was the Sun Totem, the symbol of faith in Jinwu!

When the Sun Totem appeared, everyone in Jinwu had to kneel down and worship it.
For thousands of years, tens of thousands of years, or even hundreds of millions of years, this was an inviolable ritual in Jinwu.
When Phoenix, Ming Shi, Bai Hu, and the rest of the Jinwu people saw the shadow of the golden sun, their legs trembled and reflexively bent down. They also wanted to kneel down and worship it in their hearts!
However, their instincts restrained them …
All of the Jinwu people showed an extremely torturous scene. Their bodies trembled and their instincts and habits were in a tug-of-war.
In the end, they did not kneel down and their instincts prevailed.
Looking at Phoenix, Ming Shi, and the rest floating motionlessly in the air, Emperor Jun's face was filled with disappointment. "Why did you betray me?"
Ming Shi, Bai Hu, and the rest of the Jinwu people were silent. They had not betrayed Di Jun, but they had betrayed Di Jun.
Di Jun did not force the Jinwu people to answer.
He waved the azure spear in his hand, and starlight once again shrouded the sky.

This time, there were seven streaks of starlight, which appeared above the heads of the seven Celestial Palace cultivators.
The deaths of the four Mountain Lords were still shocking the people of the Heavenly Palace.
They knew very well that the appearance of starlight above their heads meant that they were already dead …

When Zhao Han of Mount Taixiu saw the starlight above his head, his face turned ashen. Driven by instinct, he chose to flee.
The other six made similar choices. They either used secret techniques to strengthen themselves or chose to flee in different directions.
Just as they were fleeing, Emperor Jun raised the heavy blue spear in his hand.
"Pu pu pu pu pu pu!"
In front of everyone's eyes, the six of them were pierced through by the heavy spear at the same time.
This person's method of killing was powerful and tyrannical, almost unstoppable!
Everyone's heart sank again. They were like a flock of sheep that had lost their ability to resist. There was nothing they could do to Di Jun!

Chapter 3333
If these experts were placed anywhere in the mother world, they would be able to dominate a state.
However, their lives were at Di Jun's mercy.

Another ray of starlight descended and landed on the head of a bald man.

His eyes were bloodshot as he watched the Hill Master being killed by Di Jun.
Now that the starlight had landed on his head, Chen Lie's temples suddenly bulged as he roared, "This winged demon is disturbing my Heavenly Palace. Let's fight it out with him!"
Whoosh!
Chen Lie stomped his feet in the air, and the space under his feet was distorted.
After his Nirvana Realm Token appeared, it appeared on his right hand. It was a tiger-shaped gauntlet!
Buzz!
When Chen Lie charged towards Di Jun, the eyes of the tiger head on the gauntlet turned red, and a terrifying force gathered in it.
Chen Lie's Nirvana Realm Token came from the 32 Levels of Heaven, and it was also an enhanced Nirvana Realm Token!
Moreover, Chen Lie had more than one Nirvana Realm Token!
In addition to the Tiger-Shaped Gauntlet, he also released another Nirvana Realm Token. It was a palm-sized wooden compass, and it began to spin crazily as soon as it was released.
Ka Ka Ka Ka …
When Di Jun saw Chen Lie take out the compass, he raised his slender eyebrows slightly.
Chen Lie's Tiger-Shaped Gauntlet was an ordinary Nirvana Realm Token. Although it came from the 32 Levels of Heaven, it could only burst out a force of 50,000 Godly Jun.
However, this wooden compass was somewhat powerful!
This compass was made of withered wood. Three of them had been excavated in the Thirty-two Levels of Heaven. It was still unknown who had made these three compasses. Some people had speculated that these wooden compasses were deliberately left behind by the Cha Na civilization as a malicious joke for the latecomers.
There were three scales on the compass. The first scale had a multiplier of one. When the compass rotated to "one", the force released would not change at all.
The second scale was zero. When the compass rotated to zero, no matter how powerful the force was, it would completely return to zero!
The third scale was three hundred!
When the compass rotated to three hundred, the force exerted would be multiplied by three hundred times!
In other words, Chen Lie could unleash a force of 10,000 Godly Jun. Under the influence of this compass, it could be transformed into a terrifying force of 3,000,000 Godly Jun!
Three million Shen Jun …
Not to mention the supreme experts in the Mother World, even the gigantic Chaos Ancient Gods would not be able to withstand this attack.
If there were no other restrictions on the compass, it would probably be the strongest Nirvana token within the thirty-two levels of heaven!
Although the probability of three hundred times was only one-third, once it was used, it was enough to instantly kill the opponent.
However, the compass itself was not a Nirvana token of the descent type …
All the power it released had to be borne by the physical body that was releasing it.
Amplifying one's strength by three hundred times might seem wonderful, but when the compass rotated to this number, one's body would be crushed.
However, even if his physical body collapsed, there was still a way to release this portion of power.
Before Chen Lie, someone had already tried it.
In the past, the chief of the Yue Monkey Tribe in the Mother World had once carried this compass. The Yue Monkey Tribe was a small tribe that was located beside the Liyuan Tribe. For generations, they had been bullied by the Liyuan Tribe. During one of their rebellions, the chief of the Yue Monkey Tribe finally erupted and used this compass to support another Nirvana token.
The Heaven-Fixing Pearl!
After using this uncontrollable power, the chief's body directly collapsed. However, the Heaven-Fixing Pearl that was thrown out smashed the chief of the Liyuan Tribe to death …
Its power was evident.
Although the Yue Monkey Tribe was later easily annihilated by the Liyuan Tribe, it was ultimately a life-and-death struggle.
Thus, the experts in the Mother World also knew the use of this compass. It was used to fight to the death!
"Power! Gather! "
Chen Lie roared as the power in the Tiger Gauntlet on his right hand continuously surged.
"Ka ka ka ka …"
After a while, the compass finally stopped. The needle on the compass slid past zero, past one, and finally stopped at '100'.
"Hu …"
Under the influence of this compass, the power in the Tiger Gauntlet was rapidly magnified.
The energy in the Tiger Gauntlet was originally 60,000 Divine Jun. After multiplying it by a hundred times, it became 6,000,000 Divine Jun!
"Bi bi bi bi bi …"
Under the pressure of the power, even the space sealed by the World Cage started collapsing.
Seeing the Mountain Master dead, Chen Lie also decided to die. Since he had chosen to fight to the death, why would he hesitate?
"Xing Tiger Splitting Sky!"
"I want you to die!"
Just as Chen Lie roared out those words, 6,000,000 Divine Jun erupted.
Even the Chaotic Origin Spirit that he had nurtured for many years dimmed and fell into exhaustion.
At the same time, this fist also burst out along the Tiger Gauntlet!
"Hong!"
A Tiger Gauntlet was blasted out from Chen Lie's hand.
This was an even more desperate and powerful fight to the death than burning the Paramita Token.
The entire Greater One Mountain shook violently under the influence of this punch.
Even the disciples of the three great sword sects who were far away on the mountain felt their legs turning soft as they sat on the ground.
That Tiger Gauntlet tore the space apart as it charged towards Di Jun at a terrifying speed.
"The power of this fist should be enough to kill Di Jun, right?"
"What a terrifying power. That Chen Lie is not famous in the Greater Xiu Mountain, yet he could unleash such a powerful strike before his death …"
"Millions of Divine Jun. That Nirvana token is truly magical!"
Even though the power of the Divine Jun was terrifying, Di Jun's expression remained calm.
"No matter how strong the power is, what's the use if it can't hit the target?"
Just as the Tiger Gauntlet was about to reach Di Jun, his body once again turned into fist-sized cubes.
The Golden Crow Clan was most proficient in fire and space. This world-destroying punch indeed could not hit him.
But just as Di Jun was about to leave, two people appeared not far away from him.
One of them was holding a leaf.
The leaf of the Great Hidden Tree!
Under the cover of this leaf, even an expert as sharp as Di Jun could not detect the two approaching.
The other person was holding a small black snake in his hand.
This little snake was a living creature from the 31st Layer of Heaven. It had accompanied this man for many years. The relationship between the man and the snake was very deep. He had even found a female body for this little black snake, taught it how to read and speak, and married her as his wife.
But at this moment of life and death, he chose to fight with this small snake.
"Hua …"
The small snake's entire body was set ablaze, and it bit Di Jun's body at an unimaginable speed.
Di Jun was about to use his spatial ability to leave, but after being bitten by the small snake, he felt his body tremble and numb. His spatial ability was not very effective, and the blue cubes that originally appeared disappeared one by one.

Chapter 3334
The tiger-shaped fist shadow rushed toward Di Jun. The surrounding space was constantly distorted and shattered, forming a warped "bubble".
Seeing that the "bubble" was rapidly approaching, Di Jun finally showed a sense of crisis on his face.
If this punch hit him, he would also be smashed into pieces.
Di Jun's eyes flashed. He wanted to forcibly move away, but the small cubes disappeared as soon as they appeared. After being bitten by the little black snake, Di Jun had indeed lost the ability to escape.
This was how the battle between experts was like.
Even if you were a powerful existence in the mother world, you would still be defeated if you encountered some special abilities.
If the "Accomplice's Heart" had not been used to kill Elder Wei, the one who would be dead now would be Di Jun himself.
Similarly, Di Jun's "Star Mark Spear" was a very special weapon. This spear could only kill the target who was equipped with the "Starlight Proof". As long as the target was equipped with the "Starlight Proof", he could kill them in one shot. Therefore, he could kill the Mountain Masters before they could react.
And now, Di Jun was facing the same problem. The two from Mount Taixiu approached him in a strange way and used the little black snake to bite him. He did not know the ability of the little black snake, so naturally, he could not escape from it. Coupled with Chen Lie's earth-shattering punch, it was a sure-kill.
In the end.
Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and the others were looking forward to it. If this blow could kill Di Jun, that would be the best!
A trace of urgency finally appeared on Di Jun's face. He gritted his teeth, and a small jade stone suddenly appeared between his eyebrows. This jade stone was also a Nirvana Realm Token. After it appeared between his eyebrows, it flew upward.
Just as the small jade stone left him, the tiger-shaped fist shadow arrived as expected and blasted Di Jun's body.
"Cha cha cha …"
The power of six million Divine Jun was definitely not something that could be blocked by the body.

"It's done!"
"Di Jun was killed?"
"I can't believe it!"
A trace of joy appeared on the faces of the people from the Heavenly Palace. Whether they believed it or not, this scene in front of them had happened.

The faces of those You Xiong Clan people turned ashen …
They were hoping that Di Jun would help them out. Who would have thought that the dignified Demon Emperor would be smashed into pieces by that punch?
Most of the people missed the floating jade stone …
After the jade flew for a distance, it suddenly emitted a green light. The green light quickly became thicker, and Di Jun, who had been annihilated, was reborn from the green light.
The joy on everyone's faces stayed for a few breaths of time, and then they fell into a daze.
Luo Zheng stared at the scene with puzzlement in his eyes. "It's so strange. Emperor Jun should have been killed."
That scene just now was definitely not an illusion. The tiger-shaped fist shadow really smashed Di Jun into powder like crushing dry weeds and smashing rotten wood.
"Who doesn't have some life-saving means after ascending to the 33rd Layer of Heaven? That Duplication Gem is not rare in the Heavenly Treasures Dao Cave. After receiving it, you can perfectly duplicate yourself, which is equivalent to having an extra life, "Hou Yi explained.
"Duplication Gem? Perfect duplication? "Luo Zheng was stunned." If you have a few more of these Duplication Gems, won't you have an immortal body? "
To be able to perfectly duplicate yourself, such a Nirvana Realm Token was too heaven-defying. According to Hou Yi, there seemed to be a lot of such Nirvana Realm Tokens.
"Each person can only receive one. If you use it, you will be qualified to receive a second one," Di Jun added in his mind.
Such a treasure was excellent, but each person only had one chance to use it.
Di Jun had relied on the Duplication Gem to avoid the misfortune of death, but he had indeed lost his life here.
He looked down at the people below, the muscles on his face stiff and angry. "Very good. If I didn't have a way to deal with it, I would have been killed on the spot!"
"Buzz …"
The Star Mark Spear in Di Jun's hand shook again.
Six rays of starlight fell …
Chen Lie had died because his power was too strong.
The person who had used the little black snake had burned himself and the little black snake to death because he had burned the Nirvana Realm Token. Only the person who had provided the leaf of the Big Hidden Tree was still alive. One of the six rays of starlight fell on the person's head.
Seeing the Starlight Proof above his head, the person's face turned pale. Out of instinct to escape, he suddenly raised the leaf of the Big Hidden Tree. His breath was completely hidden, and he disappeared.
However, the Starlight Proof followed him like a shadow. No matter which direction the invisible person moved, the Starlight Proof followed him!
"Since you have offended me, you should be prepared to die." Di Jun shook the Star Mark Spear suddenly.
"Puff, puff, puff, puff, puff!"
At the same time, six figures of Di Juns appeared behind the six experts of the Heavenly Palace. Six rays of spear light pierced through them at the same time.
After killing the six people, Di Jun returned to his original position.
After his anger subsided, his expression became cold and arrogant again. His gaze swept across the people below one by one. He was choosing a new target.
When Di Jun's eyes swept over Luo Zheng, they paused for a moment.
It was this brat who had extinguished his sun …
Executing the Nine Suns Apocalyptic Divine Ability had greatly depleted Di Jun's energy.
If not for the fact that he needed to help Lie Shan Clan and Di Hong Clan, he would not have executed the Nine Suns Apocalyptic Divine Ability.
But Luo Zheng had successfully piqued his interest!

The Three-eyed Dragon Life Sparrow was retreating. It seemed that there was no better way except to lower its head and peck …

After missing five consecutive strikes, Di Jun finally calmed down. Anger was brewing in his delicate eyes, as if it would explode at any time.
On the other hand, Donghuang Taiyi looked at Di Jun calmly …
In Chaos No. 77 and the Nirvana Realm, Donghuang Taiyi and Di Jun had a good relationship. Of course, he knew that this "Di Jun" was extremely angry.
"Whoosh …"
After a moment of silence, Di Jun spread his wings and his body began to expand rapidly.
"The Rage God Body …"
Concubine Yan's eyes narrowed.
The strength of the cultivators in the mother world was actually divided into different levels.
At the end of the ancient times, the embryonic form of the various superpowers had been formed.
At that time, the Three Tycoons of the Ear Rat, Black Ship Boss, Ji Xuanyuan, Shennong, Nuwa, Fuxi, and Donghuang …
