Before the Gate of Truth closed, these cultivators entered the Nirvana Realm with the Rage God Body to cultivate the Body of Innumerable Calamities and improve their physical bodies to a higher level.
But ever since the Middle Ancient Era, the Rage God Body had been crippled. Those who came later could not catch up with them at all!
Hence, even though there were many outstanding cultivators, their fleshy bodies were naturally flawed. The method to remedy this flaw was the "Spirit of Chaotic Origin". Nevertheless, the Spirit of Chaotic Origin had a limit. Even if they reached the Late Stage Chaotic Origin Realm, they were still on par with Di Jun and Fuxi.
There was a huge gap!
More importantly, there were two conditions to enter the 33 Levels of Heaven. First, they had to enter the Soul City. Second, they had to pass through the Nirvana Realm. Hence, no matter how hard Qiu Yin He, He Chi, Lin Zhanting, Phoenix, and other cultivators tried, they could not enter the 33 Levels of Heaven.
They could not become supreme experts!
And there was only Elder Ying and Donghuang who had entered the 33 Levels of Heavenly Palace.
"Hua …"
Following that, Di Jun's body rapidly expanded. He had already turned into a giant with two wings.
As he flapped his wings, a gale was formed.
For a moment, rocks flew everywhere. Sand and dust billowed!
"Is he serious now?"
Donghuang Taiyi chuckled.
He also activated the power of the Rage God. His body began to expand rapidly.
In the blink of an eye, he had also turned into a giant with a height of one hundred thousand feet. He stood not far from Di Jun!
Donghuang Taiyi stretched out his hand and grabbed the air. At the same time, he spat out a word, "Grow!"
The Purple Rainbow Sword also continued to grow bigger and bigger.
This Purple Rainbow Sword was forged by Donghuang Taiyi with the Heavenly Gold Lock Iron. It was originally sixty-six thousand feet long. However, after forging it, it was shrunk to six feet and six inches with a secret technique.
Now, Di Jun had completely lost his patience. With a flap of his wings, a blazing sun appeared behind him. He rushed towards Donghuang Taiyi without any hesitation.
Donghuang Taiyi did not panic. He took a step back in the air. The space around him fluctuated quietly. He had already moved to a higher level in the sky.
The battle between the Rage Gods could easily harm the innocent. Naturally, he did not want to involve Luo Zheng in the battle.
After Di Jun's huge body pounced on the air, countless blue cubes appeared from his huge body and chased after him in the sky.
Just as Di Jun chased after him …
A huge golden sword shadow appeared on the dome in the sky. The golden sword shadow was so huge that it almost crossed the dome in the sky above the Center God Province!
"Hum …"
Those golden sword shadows rotated to one side and turned into dozens of golden sword shadows. These sword shadows directly blocked the sky above the Center God Province!
In the vast land of the Center God Province, trillions of living beings were bathed in the golden light.
Concubine Yan, Feng Ge, and the people from the Taiyi Palace covered their mouths when they saw this scene.
Those were the Golden Rune Swords activated by the power of Donghuang Taiyi's bloodline!
However, Donghuang Taiyi could only activate three Golden Rune Swords at most when he used all his strength. At that time, the power was already terrifying. The other powerhouses in the mother world were also very wary of it.

Chapter 3344
Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and the others did not know what had happened to Donghuang.
Looking at the dazzling golden sword light, a smile appeared on their faces.
"It seems that His Majesty has gained a lot in the Nirvana Realm!"
"Did he get a Nirvana token that can enhance his bloodline?"
"Golden Rune Swords … His Majesty has not used them for many years, but they are so powerful!"
Taiyi Heavenly Palace was not a clan force, and Donghuang's bloodline was also confined in Taiyi Palace. Therefore, there were very few people with Donghuang's bloodline …
"Hum …"
In the center of those Golden Rune Swords, there was a huge vortex, as if there was energy constantly being drawn to the center.
Luo Zheng easily saw the huge vortex in the center of the Golden Rune Swords, and asked, "Senior Hou Yi, Senior Di Jun, isn't that vortex connected to another place outside the Chaos?"
Hou Yi and Di Jun had previously explained that all the energy in the Chaos did not belong to the Chaos.
But usually, the energy released was too little to be noticed.
When the energy reached a certain level, the energy released would take the form of a vortex, and this vortex was the evidence that it was connected to the outside of the Chaos.
The power of those Golden Rune Swords was extremely terrifying. Luo Zheng observed and found that they also took the form of a vortex.
"That's right!" Hou Yi replied.
"Then can Senior Hou Yi's Sun-Shooting Divine Bow destroy these Golden Rune Swords?" Luo Zheng asked.
"This …"
Just as Hou Yi was hesitating, Di Jun laughed disdainfully, "How is that possible? Everyone said that he was just lucky back then. His bow and arrow could only extinguish my sun … "
"So there are differences between vortexes," Luo Zheng murmured.
"It's not that there are differences between vortexes, but different energy sources. Different methods of cutting off energy are also different," Di Jun replied.
"In other words, there are many methods like the Sun-Shooting Divine Bow?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Di Jun nodded and said, "Yes, the one who has studied this the most is the Primeval Lord of Heaven. But even someone as smart as the Primeval Lord of Heaven has studied this path for countless years to no avail …"
"Studying things outside of the Chaos is nothing more than looking at a leopard through a tube," Hou Yi said lazily.
The experts of the Human Clan admired the wisdom of the Heavenly Lord of Primeval Beginning. However, even someone as smart as the Heavenly Lord of Primeval Beginning could not find the pattern …
"Phew …"
Golden light flashed between heaven and earth.
Those Golden Rune Swords simultaneously shot towards a certain spot in the sky.
When all the Golden Rune Swords converged, an extremely sharp aura spread out, and the resplendent golden light covered all the light in the world.
"This is the Sword Dao's Mastery!"
"Your Majesty's strength has indeed made a breakthrough!"
"He's too strong. Di Jun is definitely no match for him!"
When the cultivators of the Heavenly Palace saw this scene, they were so emotional that they almost cried.
As for You Xiong Family people, their faces were as pale as ash.
Their only hope was Di Jun.
If Di Jun was defeated, how could the experts from Taiyi Heavenly Palace and Jinwu let them off?
Moreover, there was also the powerful Donghuang Taiyi!
As the golden light flashed, a huge red fireball suddenly bloomed in the sky!
It was another sun!
Di Jun was now in a difficult situation.
He had already released the Nine Suns Destruction in advance, and could not release it again in a short period of time.
He had never thought that he would have to fight with Donghuang Taiyi, let alone that the imposter controlling Donghuang Taiyi would be so ridiculously powerful.
Based on the relationship between Di Jun and Donghuang Taiyi, his understanding of Donghuang Taiyi was far beyond that of Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and Concubine Yan …
He could not understand how the power of these Golden Rune Swords could suddenly increase by ten times!
Was the soul curled up in Donghuang Taiyi's mind a monster?
When the sun had just bloomed, the huge sword light had already pierced through …
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
Di Jun, who was inside the sun, had to move again. This sun was directly slashed into pieces by the Golden Rune Swords, turning into balls of irregular flames and quickly disappearing between heaven and earth.
However, Donghuang Taiyi's Golden Rune Swords had almost occupied the sky above the Center God Province. No matter where Di Jun moved, there would always be a Golden Rune Sword piercing through the sky.
Donghuang Taiyi moved the Golden Rune Swords faster and faster, but Di Jun moved faster and faster as well.
"Buzz buzz buzz buzz …"
Countless blue cubes formed a figure, flashing all over the Center God Province.
Above the lake, as soon as those blue cubes appeared, a golden light shot from the sky. Before Di Jun's figure formed by the blue cubes could condense, it directly disappeared …
"Swoosh …"
The golden sword light flew over, and the surging aura caused the water of the lake to start flowing backward. It was like a tsunami.
Fortunately, in the many big cities around the lake, there were quite a few strong cultivators from the families. They all took action to stop the lake water from flowing backward.
These strong cultivators were not worried about the loss of the lake water. They were more worried about the battle in the sky.
As the natives of the Center God Province, they could naturally see that the one in the sky should be Donghuang Taiyi himself. Only he had the ability to do so. As for who the other blue shadow was, there were relatively few people who could see it …
No matter what, they still hoped that Donghuang could win. At least that way, the Center God Province would be safe.
"Buzz!"
The moment Di Jun disappeared, he had already appeared on a plain in the west of the Center God Province. He did not even have a chance to breathe before the Golden Rune Swords slashed at him again.
"Swoosh, swoosh, swoosh, swoosh …"
The blades of the Golden Rune Swords slashed across the ground, cutting the ground on both sides and turning it upside down. The plain that was originally a whole was cut by the Golden Rune Swords, forming a huge canyon.
Seeing that the Golden Rune Swords were in front of him again, Di Jun could only move again …
Donghuang Taiyi put his wings behind his back, and all the Golden Rune Swords moved according to his will. He looked like he was handling a butcher's cleaver with ease.
"If you are smart enough, you still have a chance to escape now …"
"But you don't know how to escape."
"Although you've been able to dodge my Golden Talisman Sword multiple times with your Greater Teleportation, I only need one chance …"
"One chance!"
As soon as Donghuang Taiyi finished his words, countless small cubes suddenly appeared behind him. Di Jun had chosen to teleport behind him to launch a sneak attack.
However, Donghuang Taiyi seemed to have guessed Di Jun's movement!
A huge Golden Rune Sword fell from the sky, piercing through Di Jun's head from top to bottom.
Seeing that the Golden Rune Sword came at such a timely time, Di Jun's expression changed, and he flapped his wings to retreat quickly.
However, as he was retreating, another Golden Rune Sword slashed at Di Jun from behind!
This time, Di Jun had no time to dodge. He could only turn around abruptly to avoid being hit on his vital parts.
"Puff!"
The Golden Rune Sword cut off one of Di Jun's arms and half of his wings.
The arm that was more than 40,000 feet long and the wings that were 200,000 feet long were cut off. The moment they were cut off, they returned to their normal size and fell from the sky.

Chapter 3345
Di Jun looked at his wound, his entire body trembling.
Donghuang Taiyi was still standing with his hands behind his back.
There was an indescribable sadness on his face.
He had also read all of Donghuang's memories after taking over his clone's body.
In this life, Donghuang and Di Jun had been as close as brothers, but in the end, they had turned against each other and met on the battlefield.
"Swoosh, swoosh..."
Blood dripped from Emperor Jun's body, but no one dodged. They just raised their heads and stared at the movement in the sky.
"You are no match for me," Donghuang Taiyi said calmly.
Donghuang Taiyi was much better than Di Jun's clone in terms of bloodline control and battle experience. If it had been the real Di Jun, it would have been hard to say who would have won.
The corner of Di Jun's eyes twitched. He raised his hand, and a huge flame condensed into a spear.
He could ignore his own life, but he would not allow himself to fail!
However, just as the huge flame spear was condensed, a crack appeared between his eyebrows, and an eyeball came out.
"Come back. You are no match for him," Ji Xuanyuan from Dihong Clan said.
This eyeball not only allowed them to share the Nirvana token, but also allowed them to observe each other and exchange information.
Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno had seen the fight between Di Jun and Donghuang.
"Not only are you no match for him, but even if I were to fight him, the result would be the same," Shinno from Lie Shan Clan said.
Both of them were very cautious. Apparently, they regarded Donghuang Taiyi as a strong opponent.
"Why?" Di Jun asked unwillingly. "He is not Donghuang!"
"He is not, but he could be another one," Ji Xuanyuan said.
"Another one? What do you mean..." Di Jun was shocked.
"The reason why your Nine Suns Destructive Divine Power was destroyed by Luo Zheng's small bow and arrow was because of another person called Hou Yi. There is another me, another Lie Shan, another Di Jun in this world …"
"What!" Di Jun was shocked when he heard the news. "How could these people exist?"
Like Li Shan, Ji Xuanyuan and Shennong had been searching for the Nirvana Realm humans since they were born.
However, the clues left behind by the Nirvana Realm humans were all concentrated in the Six Stripes Blood Steadying Tree, which was in the hands of Nuwa. Therefore, Ji Xuanyuan and the others knew less about the Nirvana Realm humans than Nuwa.
Genryū knew that Luo Zheng had entered the Heaven Splitting Divine Temple. What the Moon Fire Slave had seen was also seen by the Meta-Spirits in the upper heavens. Naturally, they knew that Luo Zheng had taken the Weapon of Civilization …
As the second dominant civilization, Genryū was naturally aware of the movements of the Nirvana Realm humans. The so-called "secret of the Nirvana Realm humans" was no secret to them.
But from the beginning to the end, the Yuanling Clan never revealed any information about the Nirvana Human Tribe to Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno. The reason was very simple. The Yuanling Clan supported Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno in order to separate the Human Tribe. Why would they tell them about the past of the Nirvana Human Tribe?
It was only when Ji Xuanyuan had just gone to the other shore and asked about the Sunslaying Divine Bow in Luo Zheng's hands that the Elemental Spirit Race had revealed their secrets. Now that things had come to this point, the Elemental Spirit Race had completely controlled them, so Ji Xuanyuan and the others naturally had no right to turn back.
However, he was also shocked after learning the truth. Naturally, he told Di Jun the truth as soon as possible.
"So, I'm just a replica?" Di Jun murmured.
Donghuang Taiyi was still looking at Di Jun calmly. Di Jun had transformed into a huge Rage God. Even if he murmured softly, Donghuang Taiyi could still hear him clearly. Since Di Jun knew that he was a replica, he probably knew some of the truth...
Di Jun lowered his head and pondered for a while. Suddenly, he dispersed the power of the Rage God.
His huge body disappeared and returned to normal size.
Donghuang Taiyi stared at the ant-like Di Jun and dispersed the power of the Rage God as well.
"You're the True Body of Donghuang Taiyi, right?" Di Jun asked.
Donghuang Taiyi nodded. "Yes."
Di Jun looked down at the Greater One Mountain and said, "I want to see... my True Body."
Donghuang Taiyi finally showed a surprised look when he suddenly made such a request, but he quickly made a decision. "Okay."
Donghuang Taiyi and Di Jun descended one after another.
The experts of the Heavenly Palace did not know what was going on, but they still looked nervous when they saw Di Jun descending.
There were still a few experts of the Heavenly Palace who had maintained their fighting strength, such as Li Bianxue and the others. At this time, they also came forward in advance to prevent Di Jun from attacking.
"Luo Zheng, he wants to see his True Body," Donghuang Taiyi said through sound transmission.
Luo Zheng also showed a look of astonishment. While he was hesitating, Di Jun's True Body said in Luo Zheng's mind, "See him, I can talk to him." “
After Luo Zheng nodded, Donghuang Taiyi had already stood beside Luo Zheng, while Di Jun, who had lost an arm and a wing, stood opposite Luo Zheng.
"Buzz..."
With a faint flash of light, the shadow of Di Jun's True Body appeared above Luo Zheng's head.
Di Jun looked at Di Jun for a while, and then asked, "They said I'm just a copy of you?"
"Yes," Di Jun replied.
"What's the meaning of copying us?" Di Jun asked again.
"To let you go on the Path of the End and make the last fight," Di Jun said again.
"Lie Shan, Dihong and the others... are they the same?" Di Jun asked.
"Same. In fact, we have spread a lot of seeds, but most of them did not survive. You are one of the lucky ones. Ji Xuanyuan is one of them, and so is Shennong." Emperor Jun smiled and said, "I can ask their Spirits to come out..."
"No need." Di Jun waved his hand and continued, "But why did we go on a different path? Why didn't we go on the same path as Donghuang, Nuwa and Fuxi?"
Di Jun felt a little wronged. He was very stubborn. After finding out that he was a traitor, he regretted it. However, he had chosen to betray them unconsciously, so he blamed his True Body.
Di Jun sighed, "Because of some accidents, Genryū intervened, and we were trapped in the 33 Levels of Heaven. We couldn't do anything until... Luo Zheng opened the Heaven-splitting Divine Temple and carried the Instrument of Civilization. Then our Spirits could see the light of day again."
"I see. I was the one who went on the path." Di Jun lowered his eyes and said, "Then I can choose another path..."
When Di Jun asked these questions to Luo Zheng, Ji Xuanyuan and Shennong were listening attentively from another place in the mother world. When they heard Di Jun's words, their faces changed.
"Oh no!"
Genryū dared to tell them the truth because he was sure that they would not dare to betray him.

Chapter 3346
Luo Zheng, Concubine Yan, and the others were also shocked.
Experts like Emperor Jun had extremely strong willpower. Once they chose a path, they would continue to walk it even if it was wrong.
Now, Emperor Jun was going to change his mind with just a few words.
However, Emperor Jun seemed to have expected this, and his expression was normal.
Emperor Jun was similar to Emperor Jun in character. He knew that this replica was the same as himself, and could not accept deceit and betrayal.
Once, the replica was the king of the whole Jinwu. He did not care about betrayal, and everything in the Jinwu would go according to his willpower. However, after the replica found out about his origin, the situation was different.
Back then, the replica and the replica Donghuang Taiyi had been arguing about which path to choose. Now that he had been proven wrong, he certainly could not accept it.
"If you want, of course," Emperor Jun replied.
Emperor Jun nodded.
He was very interested in the power of the Jinwu bloodline.
He also wanted to know more about the human race and what kind of existence Di Jun was in the past.
However, just as Emperor Jun nodded, the eyeball on his forehead suddenly flashed a triangular black light, and a strange sound came from the eyeball. "Hahaha, what's the use of your willingness? You have to ask whether we are willing or not! "
Hearing this, Emperor Jun's face suddenly changed, "Who, who are you?"
This eyeball was given to Emperor Jun by Genryū, but Emperor Jun had always used it to communicate with Ji Xuanyuan and Shennong, or to borrow each other's Nirvana Token. Now, the sound from the eyeball was definitely not from any of them.
"Who am I? Hahaha … "The voice did not answer.
"I know who you are."
A figure appeared above Luo Zheng's head again. It was the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi.
When Luo Zheng asked the souls of Emperor Jun, Hou Yi, and Donghuang to come out to help, the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi began to pay attention to the outside world.
When the voice in the eyeball appeared, the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi finally chose to show himself.
The triangular black light in the eyeball stared at the Primeval Lord of the Origin's spirit and laughed even more wildly. "We meet again, Primeval Lord of the Origin. Hahaha, now you want to stir up trouble with just a trace of your spirit?"
Yuanshi Tianzun stared at the triangular black light in the eyeball calmly and said, "Opportunity is on my side."
"Opportunity is on your side? That was simply wishful thinking. If that was the case, why bother hiding in the 33 Layered Heavens? You are not even as good as the Wu Kong Clan! "the triangular black light continued.
In the entire Nirvana Realm, any race that believed in the Path of the End was suppressed. They either disappeared from the Nirvana Realm or joined the Genryū.
Even the once prosperous Human Race was trapped in the 33 Heavens.
Only the Wu Kong Clan was an exception.
The Wu Kong Clan had chosen a completely different path from Genryū, but before Genryū started to suppress them, the Wu Kong Clan had successfully attracted many geniuses and experts from large clans.
When Genryū turned back to prepare to suppress the Wu Kong Clan, the Wu Kong Clan had already grown to the point where they could not be suppressed …
"Unfortunately, your plan was too late," Yuanshi Tianzun said with a smile. "As far as I know, your forces in the Chaos do not have the upper hand."
"This bit of advantage is nothing to my Elemental Spirit Clan!"
Perhaps to prove that he did not care, the eyeball suddenly blinked.
"Buzz!"
The entire eyeball instantly turned crimson red, and a terrifying energy was brewing in it.
Donghuang Taiyi stood at the side and watched Di Jun's every move. He was afraid that Di Jun would suddenly kill someone, and that would be troublesome.
When the energy was ready, his figure flickered and appeared in front of Luo Zheng, blocking his path.
Meanwhile, Di Jun himself had also expected something …
At this moment, he turned to look at his subordinates, Phoenix, Ming Shi, Bai Hu, and the other Jinwu people, and said, "This Di Jun is the real Di Jun, and I am just a copy. Perhaps this is the reason why you betrayed me. I am willing to believe it, but please follow him …"
As he spoke, many small blue squares appeared on his body, and then he appeared in the sky.
"And, Concubine Yan, please forgive me …"
Di Jun looked down at Concubine Yan and said.
Concubine Yan had the blood of the Jinwu people flowing in her body, and they were both Jinwu people.
When she married him, Di Jun naturally agreed.
However, the initial conflict between the two clans was caused by Concubine Yan, and the entire Divine Nest regarded her as a traitor.
"Bang!"
Before he finished speaking, the red eyeball suddenly exploded.
The reason why Di Jun rushed into the sky was that he did not want to hurt Luo Zheng, Concubine Yan, and the others.
However, although the eyeball was powerful, the range of the shock wave was not large. It only shattered Di Jun's head and upper body, and the other half of his body fell from the sky.
"My Emperor …"
After seeing this scene, the Jinwu people were heartbroken.
Phoenix flapped her wings, flew up, and held Di Jun's broken body in her arms.
Although the Jinwu people had to listen to the Di Jun in Luo Zheng's mind because of the "Bloodline Worship," their memories and emotions were still there, and this sadness came from the depths of their hearts.
Concubine Yan also bit her lips, and her snow-white face was full of sadness.
Feng Ge did not have any feelings for Di Jun. After all, when she was born, the Heavenly Palace and the Jinwu people were already at odds with each other. She only hated the winged Divine Nest Demons.
However, she felt Concubine Yan's sadness, and she also felt sad.
It was indeed sad that a Patriarch of a superpower would die in this way.
After Di Jun's death, everyone's eyes fell on Zhu Hongkuan and the others again.
Zhu Hongkuan and the others wanted to escape at first, but after Di Jun came to the Center God Province and killed everyone, they did not want to leave.
After all the masters of the Heavenly Palace were killed, they could freely plunder the Seven Mountains. A superpower's foundation was very deep.
They did not expect Di Jun to be defeated by Donghuang Taiyi …
At this time, they were wondering if Di Jun could take them away. With Di Jun's strength and mastery of space, it was easy for him to do this.
However, they did not expect Di Jun to sit down and talk with them for a long time.
They talked about something, and Di Jun suddenly rose into the sky and exploded …
From the beginning to the end, Zhu Hongkuan and the others were dumbfounded. At this point, it was impossible for them to escape even if they wanted to.
The moment the eyeball on Di Jun's forehead exploded, in another part of the mother world.

Chapter 3347
Ji Xuanyuan, Shinno, and Min Hu fell silent.
They'd expected Genryū to punish Di Jun, but they hadn't expected Genryū to be so ruthless.
Di Jun had only shown a hint of rebellion and hadn't made up his mind, but Genryū had killed him without hesitation.
Such a method was simply too ruthless.
And on their foreheads, there was also an eyeball like this.
They'd once thought that this token would bring endless possibilities. With multiple tokens, they could greatly increase each other's strength.
Now, it seemed that the eyeball was a fatal threat.
How could experts like Lie Shan and Shennong allow others to take their lives at any time?
Not even the high and mighty Genryū!
While they were silent, the eyeballs in their foreheads opened at the same time. A triangular black light emerged from the center of the eyeball, and the voice spoke again.
"Did you see what happened to Di Jun?" the triangular black light asked.
"Yes," Ji Xuanyuan replied.
"What do you think?" the triangular black light asked.
A harsh light flashed through Ji Xuanyuan's eyes. "We've already chosen our own path and should guard it. Di Jun wanted to rebel on the spur of the moment. We don't need an ally like him!"
The triangular black light trembled for a while before saying, "You're right. Remember, this world will eventually prove that we've chosen the right path. Order is the only path to survival. I don't want to see this happen again. You've seen what happened to him!"
With that, the triangular black light vanished.
Ji Xuanyuan, Min Hu, and Shinno remained silent.
The experts behind them were silent as well.
Their patriarch's life was under someone else's control. They didn't feel good that someone else could kill him with a single order.
However, the eyeballs were still rooted in their foreheads. They couldn't even discuss it in private.
"Get to work."
After a short silence, Ji Xuanyuan turned to Huo Xing.
Huo Xing glanced worriedly at Ji Xuanyuan and nodded.
He stretched out his hand and made a lifting gesture towards the sky. Wisps of green flames rose from his hands and rose into the air.
Hiss … hiss … hiss …
After these green flames rose up, they were drawn into the space above. The wisps of flames formed a vortex and began to compress towards a point.
Within the Primal Chaos, there was a minimum unit of space. By continuously dividing a part of space, one could obtain a minimum unit of space. Only the Primal Chaos Ancient God "Space" could do such a thing.
And now, Fire Execution was compressing all the flames into this extremely small space.
More than an hour later, the flame in that extremely small space finally reached a critical point. No matter how hard Huo Xuan tried, he could not compress the flame in.
"Alright," Huo Xing said to Ji Xuanyuan.
Ji Xuanyuan nodded at the huge eye in the Abyssal Chasm, and that tiny space slowly rose …
In the sky above the pitch-black abyss, there were already over a hundred such tiny spaces. Even though they were very small, even the tip of a hair could contain over a million of these "tiny spaces".
However, because there were too many flames poured into it, it was still very conspicuous. Especially in the darkness, the green light spots were as dazzling as stars.
"Is a concentrated flame really that strong?" Huo Yi asked.
Ji Xuanyuan smiled faintly. "You can try."
"Alright!"
Fire Execution was already very curious. He reached out towards those green light spots in the air and suddenly squeezed them.
Whoosh!
That extremely small space exploded.
Huo Xing focused the flames in that tiny space. This extreme space had already reached its limit. If not for the Primal Chaos Ancient God "Space" personally controlling it, the tiny space would have collapsed long ago.
It did not matter that "Space" was able to achieve a wonderful balance in this tiny space.
The moment the extremely small space exploded, green flames spread out and rushed down from the sky.
After the green flames touched the bottom of the abyss, they once again soared upwards!
This abyss that was big enough to crash into a large state was actually enveloped in flames in an instant!
"The fire is indeed very fierce!" Fire Execution exclaimed as he looked at the raging flames above his head. "But just the flames I poured in shouldn't be this much."
"The entire space exploded with your flames. That space itself became a part of the flames," Ji Xuanyuan explained.
"That tiny bit of space has such power?" Fire Execution exclaimed.
"Yes. The mechanism is very complicated and hard to explain. All you need to know is that all matter in the Primal Chaos is made up of countless tiny spaces. No matter how the Primal Chaos is destroyed or reborn, these tiny spaces will always be here. Ordinary methods cannot use these tiny spaces."
Shinno reached out and touched the flames around him. "The flames are not very strong, but they are very destructive. They are enough to destroy all living beings in a state."
Those flames had already filled up the huge abyss. Such powerful flames could not cause any harm to people like Shinno or Ji Xuanyuan. But if they were placed in a large state, the situation would be different …
As long as a small space exploded, an entire province would be burned to the ground, and millions of living beings would be burned to ashes.
"How many states do we have to burn before the Mother World will react?" Fire Execution asked with a complicated expression.
Human nature was complicated.
Fire Execution was a ruthless character in the You Xiong Clan. Countless people had died at his hands.
But to burn and kill like this was truly too heartless.
"We have calculated that we should burn at least 300 large states," Shinno replied.
After saying that, everyone fell silent again.
The Meta-Spirit Clan's action of killing Di Jun had made them want to rebel.
Some of the plans they had made before seemed reasonable. But when it was time to carry them out, they wanted to retreat.
"Let me go first!"
When everyone was silent, Min Hu took the initiative to stand out.
Among the superpowers, the Minyue Clan was the smallest. At that time, the Minyue Clan was not qualified to be called a superpower at all. The Minyue Clan could develop to its current level only because of the support of the You Xiong Clan and the Divine Farmer Clan.
At this time, Min Hu naturally did not pass on his responsibility.
When the green flame in the abyss disappeared, he flew up and put the "tiny space" into his Sumeru Ring. Then, he teleported out of the abyss. Meanwhile, Ambassador Huo continued to compress the flame into the "tiny space".

Chapter 3348
Before long, Min Hu arrived in the sky above the Ying Sea Continent. He released an Extremely Small Space from his Sumeru Ring.
This Extremely Small Space had almost no weight at all. If a normal person touched it, the Extremely Small Space would pass right through his body. This was because it was so small that it was almost negligible.
That was why Min Hu had to use his spatial abhijna to move this Extremely Small Space.
After the Extremely Small Space was released, it slowly floated in the sky.
As a light breeze blew, the Extremely Small Space, which was a million times smaller than a speck of dust, spiraled in the sky and floated downwards.
The Ying Sea Continent was bright and sunny. It was not as dark as the bottom of the abyss.
That was why even though the Extremely Small Space was radiating light, it was not as bright as the stars.
This object of light floated down from the sky and landed on an island below.
The Ying Sea Continent was almost entirely water. There were only a few huge islands.
On the island below lived a small tribe called the Kun Fox Tribe. The Kun Fox Tribe was one of the more powerful tribes in the Ying Sea Continent. Even if they were to include the tribes in the Sea Continent, they would still be in the top ten. However, in the entire Mother World, they were just a small tribe among small tribes.
For example, the Shennong Tribe could easily send a powerful cultivator to wipe out a small tribe like Youxiong.
However, the Ying Sea Continent was located in a remote location. Furthermore, it did not have any unique products. Thus, it had always been relatively peaceful.
Other than the occasional war between the two tribes underwater, the Ying Sea Continent maintained peace most of the time. Such a scene was rare even in the Mother World.
"Look, what is that …"
One of the Kun Fox Tribe members noticed the light that floated down from the sky.
Many of the Kun Fox Tribe members looked up and revealed a weird look on their faces.
"Is that a star?"
"It is broad daylight …"
"Let's go over and take a look!"
The Kun Fox Tribe members did not realize that danger was approaching. Many of them even chased after the light.
One of the Kun Fox Tribe members was the fastest.
This Kun Fox Tribe member was already in the Absolute Stage. As his body flashed, he was already below the light.
"What a small light! However, the light was so strong that it could be seen clearly even from dozens of miles away. What kind of treasure is this?! "It exclaimed as it stretched out its hand toward the light spot.
The moment it came into contact with this "extremely small space", the extremely small space passed through its hand and directly fell to the ground, sinking deep into the ground.
"How …"
The Kun Fox people were a little confused. Was that dot of light really just a dot of light?
Naturally, it couldn't understand how small the 'tiny space' was. Compared to the tiny 'tiny space', its entire body was like a sieve …
Just as it was feeling strange, a wave of heat came from under its feet.
"Hua!"
Blue flames soared into the sky.
When the members of the Fox and Kun clansmen saw the towering pillar of fire, their faces were filled with terror, and they all turned to flee.
But the speed at which the green flames spread was too fast …
Before they could even turn around, their bodies had already sunk into the green pillar of fire.
Just like what Shinno said, the flames released from an extremely small space were not that strong. This flame could not cause any harm to Chaotic Origin Experts at all. Even Paramita Realm experts could resist against it.
Nevertheless, 99% of the sentient beings in the Mother World could not resist against it …
As the green flames rapidly spread, the members of the Fox and Kun clansmen screamed, roared, and cried. But the majority of them were rapidly burned into ashes.
All the trees and buildings on the island were similarly burned to ashes …
Even that Grand Perfection Kun Fox clansman could only form an enchantment when facing the soaring flames. The enchantment lasted for a few breaths of time before it was burned down.
It attempted to use Great Teleportation to avoid the green flames.
But just as it teleported to a safe location, the green flames had already spread over, enveloping it once again.
It once again used Great Teleportation, yet it was still caught up by the rapidly spreading green flames …
In the end, it teleported to the surface of the sea. Only then did it discover in despair that the green flames had already enveloped the entire Ying Hai Prefecture!
"Hua la!"
It leaped into the sea.
In a short period, the seawater had already started boiling.
But compared to the burning of the green flames, the boiling seawater was much more 'cool'. At least this Kun Fox clansman could easily endure it.
But soon, it discovered that the bottom of the sea was not safe either …
The green flames filled the entire space within the Ying Hai Prefecture. As the flames burned furiously, the seawater was also rapidly evaporated. The sea level was also dropping at a visible rate.
The large tribes at the bottom of the sea started to panic.
They did not know where the flames on the surface of the sea came from. But at the rate that the flames were evaporating, the seawater would disappear in no time. Then, they would end up just like the sentient beings on the island.
But what was happening in front of them was completely out of their expectations.
After all, they could not understand why someone would kill for no reason …
As the flames continued to burn in the sky, the seawater within the Ying Hai Prefecture was completely evaporated.
Min Hu looked down from high up in the sky.
After the seawater was evaporated, a huge amount of salt was seeped out. But as the salt mixed with the charcoal, it formed a boundless land of black salt …
"Feng …"
The flames in the 'Extremely Small Space' finally burned out.
The blazing Ying Hai Prefecture regained its coolness.
Only a small number of sentient beings survived in such a huge prefecture. They were either Paramita Realm experts who had unique Paramita Talismans or Chaotic Origin Realm experts. And the two Daoist Elders seated at the bottom of the sea were the only two Chaotic Origin Realm experts in the Ying Hai Prefecture.
Not long ago, these two Chaotic Origin Realm Daoist Elders were the strongest experts in the two great clans. These two great clans had been fighting for resources in the Ying Hai Prefecture for several tens of thousands of years. And the two of them had always been the main characters in this battle.
How could they have imagined that in a short period of time, the seawater would disappear. Their clansmen would be burned to death. Everything they had was gone.
The two of them were speechless. Their expressions were stiff.
Even though they were Chaotic Origin Realm experts, other than protecting themselves, they were helpless against such a world-destroying technique.
Tens of billions of living beings were filled with deep fear and anger before they died.
Negative emotions were contagious.
A single person could infect a group of people, and a group of people could infect an entire world.
The negative emotions of billions of sentient beings were like an invisible hammer that started shaking the entire Mother World.
"Hu …"

Chapter 3349
The battle between the Taiyi Heavenly Palace and the Jinwu Clan finally came to an end. The remaining Youxiong Tribesmen either died in battle or surrendered under the attack of Donghuang Taiyi.
Strictly speaking, Jinwu did not suffer a heavy loss. The most painful loss was the death of Di Jun.
Most of the elites of Jinwu, including the Hive and Di Jun's guards, survived.
Previously, because Di Jun was still in Phoenix, Ming Shi and the others were still in a dilemma. When they really followed Luo Zheng's order to attack Di Jun, they did not want to kill him.
Therefore, when Luo Zheng ordered them to delay Di Jun, they were easily shaken off by Di Jun. Di Jun also did not hurt them.
Now that Di Jun was dead, the focus of Jinwu had also disappeared. They seemed to have no other choice but to follow Luo Zheng wholeheartedly.
After defeating Di Jun, Donghuang Taiyi's spirit took the initiative to give up Donghuang Taiyi's body.
At this time, Concubine Yan, Gan Gaohan, and the others understood that Donghuang had not truly awakened. Someone else had taken over Donghuang Taiyi's body. However, this person was also Donghuang Taiyi's true body!
"Actually, now that the world is in chaos and Donghuang Taiyi has not returned, Donghuang Taiyi's true body can still stay in Donghuang Taiyi's body," Gan Gaohan suggested.
In Gan Gaohan's eyes, the most important thing now was the safety of the Taiyi Heavenly Palace. If another powerful expert like Di Jun came, how could they deal with it?
At this time, Donghuang Taiyi had already returned to Luo Zheng's Inner World and entered the Instrument of Civilization. Luo Zheng helped Gan Gaohan pass on his words.
In fact, Luo Zheng also felt that Donghuang Taiyi's true body did not need to leave Donghuang Taiyi's body so quickly.
After a while, Luo Zheng said awkwardly, "He refused. He said that if the Heavenly Palace was in danger, he would help again, but he did not want to stay in Donghuang Taiyi's body."
"I heard that the creatures of the Nirvana Realm are reluctant to leave the Chaotic World. Why did Donghuang refuse?" Gan Gaohan asked curiously.
Gan Gaohan had followed Donghuang Taiyi for many years and was not an ignorant person.
Those who were born in the Chaos and entered the Nirvana Realm missed their past in the Chaos. After all, they were born in the vast Chaos World and were eventually trapped in the Nirvana Realm.
Some Nirvana Realm creatures were willing to be carried and become the Nirvana token of others. On one hand, they were forced to do so. On the other hand, they wanted to see the Chaotic World again.
But this Eastern Emperor Tai Yi did not seem to be willing …
"Maybe this feeling is too good, and she's afraid that she can't let it go," Ning Xuyuan said faintly.
As long as the cultivators of the Celestial Palace were still alive, they were all severely injured.
However, at their level of cultivation, as long as they didn't die, as long as they still had a breath of life, their recovery wouldn't be too slow.
By now, Ning Xu Yuan's complexion had become much better.
"That's it. Being locked up in the dark temple for countless years, motionless like a statue, without any interest in life, without any desire to speak, without any desire for greed, without any anxiety in the heart. Tsk tsk, what a boring life. How wonderful it is to be able to descend into this world again and experience it.
That brat Donghuang must have been afraid that he would not be able to bear to leave, so he simply slipped away! "
Hou Yi suddenly appeared and said a long paragraph.
Then, he saw everyone staring at him dumbfoundedly, and said, "I'll slip away too!"
"Whoosh!"
In the blink of an eye, he had already slipped back into the world inside Luo Zheng's body.
"Luo Zheng, what kind of expert is that?" Gan Gaohan asked.
Now, they understood that none of the guys stationed in Luo Zheng's body were simple. Especially the old man who appeared at the end, he even directly talked to Genryū.
"This is Hou Yi. He destroyed Di Jun's sun with his bow and arrow …"
As Luo Zheng spoke, he recounted the story of Hou Yi shooting down the sun.
Gan Gaohan and the others were also amazed. They didn't expect the past of the Human Clan to have such a history.
Li Bianxue was healing people not far away. Naturally, she also heard Luo Zheng's story. A trace of resentment appeared on her delicate and beautiful face.
After this battle, all of her pride had turned into dust.
This young man who was advancing faster than her, relied on his own strength to reverse the entire battle. It could not even be said to be reversed, he completely relied on himself to defeat Jinwu!
Controlling the Golden Crow corpse spirit, controlling the people of Jinwu, shooting down the nine suns, defeating Di Jun...
In contrast, everyone in the Greater One Heavenly Palace was just a supporting role.
As for Li Bianxue herself, the fact that she could follow Qiu Yinhe and contribute to the battle was already a very amazing performance.
"Ka, ka, ka..."
Just as Li Bianxue was thinking this, there was another burst of explosion inside Greater One Mountain.
In the battle between Donghuang and Di Jun, the middle section of Greater One Mountain was already riddled with holes. Coupled with the fact that the middle section was originally a weak point, the mountain was unable to withstand the pressure from above. The inside of the mountain was constantly tearing apart, and began to slowly collapse.
"Greater One Mountain is going to collapse!"
Someone shouted at the top of his lungs.
If the upper part of Greater One Mountain really fell, the Dragon City below would most likely be directly smashed into powder.
Although the Dragon City had been destroyed by the people of the You Xiong tribe, the destruction was not complete. If it directly fell, there would be nothing left.
"Rumble..."
Seeing that the mountain above was constantly tilting to one side, Luo Zheng immediately ordered, "Everyone, block the Greater One Mountain!"
As soon as his voice fell, the people of Jinwu who had received the order disappeared and appeared on the side of the upper part of Greater One Mountain.
"Ka!"
"Ka!"
"Ka!"
Dozens of Jinwu experts, such as Ming Shi, Bai Hu and others, erupted with power that could not be underestimated.
The power of these people combined was enough to block the slide of the upper part of Greater One Mountain!
In addition to them, some of the uninjured experts of the Heavenly Palace also rushed up.
But these people rushed up but did not stop the slide of Greater One Mountain!
Greater One Mountain was really too huge. Compared to the mountain itself, their body size was like a small sharp needle.
When they pushed the mountain wall with their hands and erupted with a huge force, the soft mountain wall simply could not withstand the power of tens of thousands of Shen Jun. Their whole body directly sank into the mountain wall!
When the people of Jinwu discovered this problem, they spread out their wings and tried to increase their contact area with the mountain, but they still sank into the soft mountain wall with their wings...
In this situation, it was easy to let Donghuang Taiyi control Donghuang's body and transform it into the power of the Huangshen.
But Donghuang Taiyi had returned to his inner world, and there was no need to call him out again.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng's figure flashed and appeared on the side of the mountain.
The body of the Huangshen!

Chapter 3350
Although Luo Zheng's strength wasn't small, he couldn't stop the mountain from sliding down by himself.
The speed of the mountain's slide only slowed down a little.
Li Bianxue also flew up and tried to stop the mountain, but like the others, she sank into it as soon as she released her Heavenly Force.
After coming out of the mountain, she had an idea and said, "Senior Qiu, Heavenly Chasm Sword!"
The Heavenly Chasm Sword's body was huge and sturdy. The Heavenly Chasm Sword could be used!
Qiu Yinhe nodded at the mountainside and chanted. Large halos appeared in the air and the Heavenly Chasm Sword flew out from the halos.
Not long after, the huge Heavenly Chasm Swords stuck to the mountain. Phoenix, Li Bianxue and the others used their strength to stop the Heavenly Chasm Sword.
When Qiu Yinhe used the Heavenly Chasm Sword, he also distributed it evenly.
Under the push of the huge force, the slowly sliding upper half of the mountain finally stopped!
At this time, Gan Gaohan said, "Xu Yuan, lead Qiu Yinhe and the others to set up a formation. Stabilize the mountain first."
Ning Xu Yuan nodded and flew up with Qiu Yinhe and the others. They started to set up formations at the broken parts of the mountain.
It wasn't easy to set up a formation that stabilized a huge mountain.
Although it was only a temporary reinforcement, it still took two hours.
Even Phoenix and the others, who were continuously releasing their Heavenly Force, found it difficult to hold on for two hours. They had to replenish their Nirvana Realm tokens with a certain amount of Divine Crystals.
However, after two hours, Ning Xu Yuan finished setting up the formation with Qiu Yinhe and the others. They finally stabilized the mountain and everyone let out a sigh of relief.
Luo Zheng dispersed his Rage God Body. When he landed again, Gan Gaohan stared at Luo Zheng with a strange gaze.
Regarding Luo Zheng's Rage God Body, the Heavenly Palace had already heard about it.
In fact, even in this era, the Rage God Body wasn't something rare.
The desolate bones had indeed dried up, but it didn't mean that the super forces didn't collect them. The stock of desolate bones was enough for a small number of people to cultivate the Rage God.
However, the problem that the Aragami faced was that they could not enter the Nirvana Realm. If they did not enter the Nirvana Realm, they would not be able to break through the limit of one Jun of strength just by relying on the strength of the Aragami. Hence, the cultivation of the Aragami was almost meaningless. Naturally, no one would cultivate it.
However, Luo Zheng was a little different. Not only did he cultivate the Aragami, but he had also entered the Nirvana Realm. Gan Gaohan then realized another very important problem.
"Luo Zheng has only been in the Heavenly Palace for a short period of time, but he's made rapid progress," Gan Gaohan suddenly said.
Luo Zheng looked at Gan Gaohan strangely, not knowing what he wanted to say, he just said modestly, "Elder Gan is too kind."
Gan Gaohan shook his head, "I know that your physical body can enter the Nirvana Realm, so you naturally have great potential in the future. However, more than a year ago, you were still wandering in the Thirteen Heavens of the Color Realm, which was too far away from the Upper Heavens. I didn't expect you to step into the Desire Realm so quickly.
Is your soul at its limit? "
Luo Zheng nodded.
It wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng to reach the limit of the Indigo Soul. He could even help many people reach the limit of the Indigo Soul.
"Since ancient times, there have been many people who have reached the limit of the Indigo Soul," Gan Gaohan said indifferently, "However, none of them have achieved the Body of Ten Thousand Tribulations. Therefore, the powerhouses after ancient times didn't have the chance to enter the Thirty-third Heaven."
"No one has entered the Thirty-third Heaven after ancient times?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
"Yes, there are two conditions to enter the Thirty-third Heaven. The first is to reach the Divine Soul after reaching the limit of the Indigo Soul. The second is to achieve the Body of Ten Thousand Tribulations. After combining the two, one will enter the Immortal Picture Scroll in the Thirty-second Heaven. Only then will one be qualified to step into the Thirty-third Heaven, Three Pure Heavens!" Gan Gaohan said as his eyes revealed a trace of longing.
A look of yearning.
People like Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and Qiu Yinhe had all reached the Divine Soul Realm. However, because they couldn't cultivate the Body of Ten Thousand Tribulations, they couldn't enter the Immortal Picture Scroll, nor could they go to the Thirty-third Heaven. Their cultivation was forever stuck at the Thirty-second Heaven.
One could see the regret in his actions.
Even Ming Shi, Bai Hu, and the others who were not far away also revealed a trace of helplessness on their faces. It wasn't that they weren't talented enough or that they weren't strong enough. It was completely because of the Nirvana Realm's rules …
"Immortal Picture Scroll?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
"Yes, it's a huge picture scroll that floats above the Thirty-second Heaven. There are all kinds of powerful creatures in the picture scroll. The experts who enter the Immortal Picture Scroll are called the people in the picture scroll. There are also other names, such as the 'Immortal', or the Immortal Realm," Gan Gaohan said with a smile.
"Is Donghuang also considered as one of the people in the picture scroll?" Luo Zheng asked.
Gan Gaohan replied, "Yes, there are two people in our Tai Yi Heavenly Palace who are in the picture scroll. One of them is Donghuang, and the other is Elder Ying. Therefore, the strength of these two Mountain Masters is one level higher than the other five Mountain Masters!"
After experiencing the battle between Tai Yi Heavenly Palace and Jinwu, Luo Zheng had a rough understanding of the situation.
Previously, he felt that the gap between the Mountain Masters and Donghuang was not big, but now it seemed that the gap was not small. The Thirty-second Heaven and the Thirty-third Heaven were only separated by one day, but in fact, they were separated by one realm!
"Your soul and physical body are qualified. I hope that you can quickly enter the Soul City, pass through the land of Calamity Bone, and become another person in the picture scroll," Gan Gaohan said as he looked at Donghuang who was sitting on the ground, "Perhaps, you can go to the Thirty-third Heaven to save Donghuang …"
"I am qualified to become a person in the picture scroll because my physical body can enter the Nirvana Realm. What about Feng Ge whose physical body is also in the Nirvana Realm?" Luo Zheng said as he looked at Feng Ge who was not far away.
Feng Ge's situation was a little special.
Her physical body had not entered the Nirvana Realm, but the physical body of a Pure One.
Gan Gaohan said with a smile, "It may be possible. After all, the test of the Immortal Picture Scroll is in the Nirvana Realm. As long as one passes it, it is possible, but I am not completely sure."
Since ancient times, no one had experienced a situation as strange as Feng Ge's. Even Donghuang himself could not be sure.
"I will definitely become a person in the picture scroll," Feng Ge suddenly said, "I will save Father Emperor!"
Gan Gaohan's wrinkled face revealed a look of love, encouraging, "I believe that His Highness Feng Ge will do it!"
Feng Ge's gaze drifted faintly to Luo Zheng.
She knew in her heart that whether or not he could enter the picture scroll and become a person in the picture scroll was still him.
Under Feng Ge's gaze, Luo Zheng's expression was a little unnatural. He glanced at the stabilized Greater One Mountain and said, "I need to go back to the Heart Flow Sword Sect!"
Although the possibility of the You Xiong clan going to the three major sword sects was not high, Luo Zheng was still concerned. Now that the chaos in the Heavenly Palace had been quelled, it was naturally time to go back.
As his voice faded, Luo Zheng spread out his arms and flew away.

Chapter 3351
The disciples of the Heart Sword Sect also saw the earth-shattering scene.
As long as one was not blind, one could clearly see the sun and the golden formation sword blooming in the sky. And in the area of the Greater One Mountain, one could see the huge figures of Emperor Jun and Donghuang.
If such a colossus were to stomp down, the entire Heart Sword Sect would most likely cease to exist.
Xiaoyin and Little Xin still took turns to walk out of the independent courtyard, inquiring about the news outside.
After realizing that it was more dangerous outside, Ning Yudie made a wise decision to stay in the courtyard and not go out.
Xi Youqin was surprisingly calm in the face of crisis. She only asked a few times when Luo Zheng would return, as if the earth-shattering outside had nothing to do with her.
The courtyard felt the mountain shaking one after another, followed by ear-piercing explosions. At first, the shaking of the mountain was small, but then the mountain began to slide down!
If the huge mountain fell down, the consequences would be unimaginable.
But the disciples of the three major sword sects could all fly. If the top of the Greater One Mountain fell down, they only needed to fly out.
Fortunately, the mountain only continued to slide for a short while before it abruptly stabilized.
Xiaoyin went out again to inquire, and learned that the battle in the Sky Palace had subsided. Everyone breathed a sigh of relief...
After waiting for a while, Luo Zheng descended from the sky above the courtyard. The girls in the courtyard breathed a sigh of relief when they saw Luo Zheng, and their faces lit up with joy.
Although they believed that Luo Zheng would be able to return unscathed, but this time even the Greater One Heavenly Palace had almost been destroyed. In the face of experts like the Jinwu, they could only silently pray for Luo Zheng in their hearts.
Luo Zheng did not let them down, and returned safely.
After Luo Zheng, Ning Yudie, Xi Youqin and the other girls had a small gathering, Xiuzhu quietly came out from the corner and asked in a low voice, "Lord, I wonder if the Evil Deities are safe."
Luo Zheng had corrected Xiuzhu several times, asking her not to call him "Master", but Xiuzhu seemed to be unable to change it.
Speaking of Evil Deities, Luo Zheng slightly frowned.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Xiuzhu's heart sank. Could it be that the Evil Deities had fallen?
She knew that her husband had been refined into a doppelganger, which was equivalent to Luo Zheng's doppelganger puppet. Under such circumstances, no matter how capable the Evil Deities were, it would be difficult for them to betray Luo Zheng. Most likely, they had already perished …
Xiu Zhu had prepared herself mentally and was prepared to receive this answer.
"He escaped," Luo Zheng said helplessly.
"He got away? How is that possible …" Xiuzhu's expression froze.
Luo Zheng shrugged, "He shouldn't have died outside."
Evil Deities being able to escape was purely an accident. Theoretically speaking, Evil Deities should not have disobeyed his orders. It was just that Evil Deities happened to be fighting against a sentient being that could not be killed, Chun Zai. He made use of Luo Zheng's orders to pull apart the distance between them before escaping.
"So, sorry …"
Xiuzhu hurriedly apologized after regaining her senses.
Luo Zheng shook his head, "It's not your fault. It's my own negligence. But I'll find him sooner or later."
In Luo Zheng's opinion, even if Evil Deities had a way to escape, they were ultimately his Dao Refinement doppelganger.
If they met again in the future, the incident in the Chaotic Origin Great World should not happen again. As long as they were close enough, he would not be able to disobey him.
"Then, then can I return to your inner world?" Xiuzhu asked.
Xiuzhu viewed leaving her inner world as a gift from the Lord. Now that Evil Deities had committed a mistake, the gift from the Lord should naturally be retracted.
She knew that Evil Deities was unwilling to accept Luo Zheng in his heart. He would never compromise.
But she thought that the Dao Refinement doppelganger was sufficient to suppress Evil Deities. Who would have thought that he would escape not long after. She felt extremely uneasy.
Luo Zheng glanced at Xiuzhu indifferently before saying, "This time, I'm returning to the Heart Flow Sword Sect because I want to keep Little Die, Youqin, and Ling Yun back into my inner world. If you are not willing to return to your inner world, I will not force you. I can let you go."
Xiuzhu did not have a place to send her soul back in Luo Zheng's inner world. The moment she left, it was impossible to find her.
"But the Mother World has not been peaceful recently. You are traveling alone. The possibility of finding Evil Deities is very small." Luo Zheng added.
Xiuzhu's strength was considered top-notch in Luo Zheng's inner world. But in the Mother World, she was at the bottom. With her talent, it was possible for her to step into the Nirvana Realm. But even so, it would not help her.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Xiuzhu was startled for a moment. Her pair of dim eyes suddenly lit up. It was like a dusty gem that saw the light of day again. "Is Lord willing to … let me go?"
"If you are willing, I will not stop you." Luo Zheng replied.
"Then I want to leave!" Xiuzhu's face glowed.
She could stay in Luo Zheng's inner world and wait for the return of the Evil Deities, but she was more willing to take the initiative to find them. As for how dangerous it was, she did not care at all.
"But my children are still in Lord's inner world. I wonder if Lord …" Xiuzhu hesitated. Although she loved Evil Deities deeply, she was different from Evil Deities. As a mother, she still cared about her children.
"I will not interfere with the development of the inner world. I believe you also know that." Luo Zheng said.
In most cases, Luo Zheng allowed the inner world to develop. Unless there was an extreme situation like the Evil Deities, he would not interfere.
After getting a reassuring answer from Luo Zheng, Xiuzhu left. Now that the Greater One Heavenly Palace had just recovered from the turmoil, no one would stop her.
"Now that the mother world is in chaos, I have taken Xun and the others into my inner world. You should also take shelter there. Especially Youqin, she needs a stable environment," Luo Zheng said.
Xi Youqin seemed to have changed into a different person recently. She obeyed Luo Zheng's words without any objection.
Hearing that Luo Zheng was going to take Ning Yudie and the others into his inner world, Xiaoyin and Little Xin's expressions suddenly changed.
Over the past few years, as Luo Zheng's status in the Heavenly Palace rose steadily, their status also rose. At least among the servant girls of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, they were the leaders.
Even the disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect were polite to them when they saw them. In the past, this kind of thing would have been unimaginable.
The two of them had made up their minds to follow Luo Zheng.
Now that Luo Zheng had withdrawn all his family members, how could they not panic?
"Gongzi Luo, are you not going to return to the Heart Flow Sword Sect?" Xiaoyin boldly asked.
Luo Zheng glanced at Xiaoyin and then realized that he had neglected the two servant girls.
"The Heavenly Palace is not peaceful now. If you two are willing to enter my inner world, you can..."
Luo Zheng was still a little hesitant. After all, they were born in the mother world and were not willing to live in a small inner world.
But before he finished speaking, Xiaoyin and Little Xin answered in unison, "That would be for the best!"

Chapter 3352
For the next few days, the Taiyi Heavenly Palace was cleaning up the war.
Fortunately, Di Jun and the You Xiong Clan only destroyed the Taiyi Mountain and Dragon City, and the Taiyi Palace was the most seriously damaged.
The entire Taiyi Palace was turned into dust, and there was no other choice but to rebuild it.
Three days later, the Taiyi Heavenly Palace waited for Li Shan's reinforcements.
Before the Jinwu launched the attack, the Taiyi Heavenly Palace had seen the determination of the Jinwu, and naturally sent a request for help from Li Shan.
But Li Shan came to the Center Divine Land, and it was a long journey.
But the problem was not the journey. When Li Shan was about to send many leaders, the powerhouses of the Divine Farmer Clan suddenly made things difficult in the north of Li Shan.
When the people of Li Shan appeared, the people of the Divine Farmer Clan quickly retreated.
This repeated several times, and the powerhouses of the Divine Farmer Clan did not choose to make a move, nor did they choose to escape. They kept watching from the north.
Li Shan was very clear that this should be the Divine Farmer Clan's plan to delay Li Shan's reinforcements, but they still saw through it, and there was nothing they could do.
Fuxi had left the mother world because of an important matter, and Goddess Nuwa had been rescuing Donghuang on the other side.
Although there were many powerhouses in Li Shan, they had entered an eventful period, and the powerhouses of Li Shan had to be cautious.
After the powerhouses of the Divine Farmer Clan retreated, they sent more than 60 people to the Taiyi Heavenly Palace.
But at this time, the matter of the Taiyi Heavenly Palace had calmed down, and after hearing about the death of the Demon Emperor Jun, the powerhouses of Li Shan were also surprised.
If Di Jun came to the Center Divine Land in person, no one could take him down unless Goddess Nuwa and Fuxi came personally.
But the Heavenly Palace defeated Di Jun?
Gan Gaohan did not disclose all the details.
What happened to Luo Zheng was simply inconceivable to Gan Gaohan. Even if such a thing was disclosed, it should be Donghuang who told Li Shan, and not Gan Gaohan.
…
The background of the chaos was pitch black.
No matter how strong the light was, it could not illuminate.
Even if thousands of suns were thrown into the chaos, from afar, they would only be dim light spots.
Instead, the clouds of chaos emitted a faint brown light, and from afar, they were huge clouds that were boundless...........
A huge black ship was moving silently in the clouds of chaos.
The Kurofune had always been famous for its whereabouts, and there were very few people in the world who could find the Kurofune.
Compared to the other black ships, this black ship was somewhat special.
The front of the ship was polished with pieces of Seven Star Obsidian, and the sides of the ship were engraved with blurry words, which should be enlarged Sanskrit.
"Phew …"
Just as the black ship rushed out of the chaotic clouds, a huge Eight Trigrams suddenly appeared in front of them.
The black ship's path was blocked, and it stopped abruptly above the chaotic clouds. As the eight trigrams rotated, Fuxi's figure appeared behind it.
"Heavenly Eight Trigrams Rope!"
Fuxi let out a soft shout.
Eight similar Eight Trigrams quickly appeared in all directions of the black ship, firmly imprisoning the black ship within.
In terms of strength, Fuxi was not afraid of anyone in the Origin World!
Even if it was the most mysterious captain!
However, Fuxi had a weakness, which was their slow speed.
Last time, Lei Meng was able to escape from Fuxi because of his speed and doppelgangers.
If Lei Meng was entangled by Fuxi, given his strength, he would be punched to death by Fuxi.
This time, after Fuxi captured the position of the captain, the first thing he did was to trap him so as to prevent the same thing from happening again.
"Shua …"
A strand of black smoke kept transforming, turning into a black hood. Under the hood, one could not see the appearance of the person within.
No one had ever seen the appearance of the captain before.
His existence was like a symbol in the eyes of the crew members of the black ship.
"So Human Emperor has come personally. I wonder what is the matter?" The captain asked indifferently.
"The Ring of Descent of my Chaotic Origin Great World has been snatched away by Lei Meng." Fuxi said with a sullen expression.
The captain's voice remained calm, "Then Sir Human Emperor can go and find that Lei Meng and snatch it back from him."
Fuxi snorted. He extended his hand and tossed upwards.
A spatial ring unfolded, and an enormous bat head slowly fell out from that space.
When that black hood saw the bat head, his body trembled slightly. One of the Ten Great Primal Chaos Beasts, Lei Meng, was actually killed by Fuxi?
"I haven't fought for many years. Some idiots seem to have forgotten about my methods. Daring to snatch food from me? They must be tired of living." Fuxi said as he punched out towards the bat head.
A fist imprint with the shadow of the Eight Trigrams was imprinted on the bat's head.
"Bo!"
Lei Meng's head was instantly smashed into powder.
The Ten Great Primal Chaos Beasts were all famous. They were not any weaker than a 'Origin World'.
But even a powerful existence like Lei Meng was still killed by Fuxi. Fuxi's true strength was indeed frightening.
"Lei Meng took away 12 Rings of Descent. Six of them landed in your hands. Return them to me." Fuxi said directly.
Shinno and Ji Xuanyuan had paid a huge price to invite Lei Meng to help them.
Lei Meng didn't expect to be hunted down endlessly by Fuxi after receiving such a handsome reward!
He had escaped several times in the chaos, but Fuxi had caught him in the end. Before his death, Lei Meng had revealed the whereabouts of the 12 Rings of Descent. Six had been given to Shinno of Lie Shan, and the other six had been taken by the black ship.
After some consideration, Fuxi decided that it was better to look for the black ship.
The boatman was silent for a moment, then said, "Does Your Majesty think a single Heavenly Eight Trigrams Rope can trap me?"
His black ship had roamed the chaos and the motherworld. Even if he had angered many factions, he was still safe. This was because of his terrifying ability to escape.
"It can't trap you, but what about adding a Ring of Prophecy?" Fuxi asked coldly.
The boatman stood on the deck. The man under the hood seemed to have sunk into deep thought again. After a long time, he sighed faintly. He stretched out his hand and three Rings of Descent appeared. "I can return three Rings of Descent to you, but I have a condition. If you agree to it, I will tell you.
If you agree, I'll give you some valuable information. "
When Fuxi saw the three Rings of Descent, he revealed a strange expression.
He originally thought that he would have a fierce battle with the black ship. Who would have thought that the boatman would hand over half of the rings so easily.
"What condition?" Fuxi asked.
"I need those scarlet-gold Chaotic Origin Spirits as an exchange." The boatman said.
"Scarlet-Gold Chaotic Origin Spirits?"
Fuxi was startled for a moment before reacting.
After the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was killed, Luo Zheng dug out some unique Chaotic Origin Spirits from within.

Chapter 3353
After being reminded by the shipmaster, Fuxi immediately understood what was going on.
He was most likely thinking in the wrong direction. That Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit was not used to strengthen his body. Instead, it was a necessary item for him to descend!
"What if I am not willing to exchange?" Fuxi asked.
There were still 20 Golden Chaotic Origin Spirits in the hands of Luo Yan. According to what he knew, most of them were in the hands of that brat Luo Zheng.
Regardless of whether the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit was useful or not, Fuxi did not feel the need to agree to the shipmaster's request.
Today, he came here to force the shipmaster to hand over the Ring of Descent and not to trade with him.
"If you are not willing, you will never be able to get the Ring of Descent. At least, that is the case with me …" The shipmaster replied, "It is easy to hide a few tiny Rings of Descent in the vast Primal Chaos."
Fuxi's expression turned cold. At the same time, he revealed a savage look. A terrifying energy was brewing in his body, "It seems the shipmaster does not intend to admit defeat today."
"Admit defeat?" The ship captain smiled faintly and said, "With the Human Emperor's strength, you should indeed be so confident. But has the Human Emperor ever thought that the black ship that comes and goes without a trace in the mother world would be so easily found by the Human Emperor?"
Hearing those words, Fuxi revealed a solemn look. He even scanned his surroundings.
A strand of faintly discernible black aura was spiraling around him. It was just that it was hard for others to detect it due to the black background.
The shipmaster's intention was clear. He knew Fuxi was chasing after the black ship. Hence, he had set up a trap and waited for Fuxi to visit him so that he could discuss the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit with Fuxi.
"I don't have many Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystals in my hands." Fuxi extended his finger and lightly pinched.
That Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit that Luo Yan gave to Fuxi appeared in his hand.
Seeing this Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit, two golden lights appeared under the shipmaster's cloak. He then asked: "How many do you have?"
"About 20." Fuxi answered.
He only counted the portion in the hands of Luo Yan. Even though he could ask for more from Luo Zheng, Fuxi was not willing to hand over more Golden Chaotic Origin Spirits.
"We want all of them." The shipmaster said.
Fuxi frowned slightly and said coldly, "Captain, you're being too greedy."
"Don't be greedy. Your Li Mountain can get even more," the boatman replied faintly.
The Kurofune also targeted Luo Zheng, but they didn't have time to make a move yet. He was implying something, but he didn't point out Luo Zheng.
"Sure, but you have to tell me what's wrong with the Ring of Descent," Fuxi said calmly.
After killing Lei Meng, Fuxi tried to search the beast's memories.
However, before his death, Lei Meng had used a mystic art to mess up his mind and set up a soul trap.
When Fuxi was searching for the soul, he almost fell into the trap.
"Of course I'll tell you, but everything has to be done under the condition of abiding by the contract …" The ferryman said as he raised his sleeves.
A living token of the Nirvana Realm slowly crawled out from his sleeve. It was a double-headed snake.
The double-headed snake had a gorgeous color. The scales on its body were divided into two layers. The left head was red, and the right head was blue. After crawling out, the double-headed snake kept flicking its tongue.
"The Binding Contract Twin Snake?" Fuxi's expression changed slightly when he saw the double-headed snake.
"If the Human Emperor returns to Li Mountain, I really can't do anything to you. In order for the Human Emperor to keep his promise, please choose the snake!" The ferryman said.
The Binding Contract Twin Snake was a Nirvana token in the Thirty-three Layers of Heaven. It was also a snake with strange habits, but its abilities were abnormally strong.
In the Thirty-three Layers of Sanqing Heaven, it often appeared in desperate situations.
Once, Fuxi was chased by the Heart Bees in the Sanqing Heaven's Wasp Pool and was forced into a desperate situation. At the end of the Wasp Pool, he encountered a Binding Contract Twin Snake. The Binding Contract Twin Snake spoke human language and asked him to complete a task. If he agreed, it would help Fuxi escape.
In such a situation, Fuxi had no choice but to agree in order to survive!
When Fuxi agreed, the Binding Contract Twin Snake really did help him find a way to escape.
Later, in order to keep his promise, Fuxi spent five to six thousand years at the edge of the Sanqing Heaven to complete the contract with the snake.
As for why it appeared on time, no one could figure it out.
However, it was miraculously able to do so!
Some people treated it as a lucky charm, while others saw it as a devil. After all, it was very difficult to complete the contract with the snake, and the consequences of violating it were even worse.
People who had been to the Sanqing Heaven had more or less seen and heard of such a miraculous creature.
However, no one had ever fused it into a Nirvana token, carried it in their body, and descended into the Chaotic World!
The snake suddenly twisted its body and said in red, "The Binding Contract is the Poison of Life and Death. The contract is also the Poison of Life and Death. As long as you keep your promise, the poison will be dissolved immediately!"
"It's all the same!"
Fuxi's figure flashed and appeared on the deck. He reached out his hand toward the Binding Contract Twin Snake.
"Whoosh!"
That red snake bit down on Fuxi's arm at lightning speed. It did not bite through Fuxi's arm. Instead, it left a unique mark on it.
At the same time, the blue snake bit down on the arm of the boatswain.
The Binding Contract was effective at the same time. It was effective not only on Fuxi, but also on the boatswain.
After the double-headed snake bit each of them, the boatman pushed the three Descending Rings to Fuxi and said, "These Descending Rings can indeed bring the creatures of the Nirvana Realm down, but they can't avoid the punishment of the truth. I've tried a few times, but every time the creatures that came down were directly punished by the truth.
The Nirvana Realm beings will be erased … "
The boatswain told Fuxi the results of using the Descending Rings in detail.
After Fuxi finished listening, he was enlightened. If he was the boatswain, he would also attribute the problem to the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit. This judgment was not wrong …
However, just as Fuxi was enlightened, he suddenly realized something was amiss. His expression changed abruptly.
It wasn't a secret that Luo Zheng took away the Chaotic Origin Spirit. The You Xiong Clan and the Divine Farmer Clan must know about it too.
If Ji Xuanyuan and the others targeted the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit, wouldn't the Celestial Palace and Luo Zheng be in trouble?
"I implore Black Ship to send someone to Li Mountain. Li Mountain will naturally hand over those Golden Chaotic Origin Spirits. The few of us will take our leave first!" Fuxi cupped his hands before executing Great Teleportation and left the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
After Fuxi left, the boatswain said, "It's true that you have thought it through in a hurry. But the Celestial Palace should have been destroyed right?"
During the period when Fuxi was chasing after Black Ship, Black Ship was observing the Celestial Palace.
He knew the movements of the Celestial Palace and Jinwu like the back of his hand.

Chapter 3354
On the fifth day after the Heavenly Palace stabilized, Luo Zheng sank into the Inner World.
As soon as he formed his Inner Incarnation, he saw a huge circular leaf in the distant sky. The leaf was constantly rotating in the sky, emitting a pale green light.
Luo Zheng looked curious. In a flash, he came to the side of the leaf.
On the top of the leaf, some humans were busy. They lowered their heads and constantly engraved strange magic formations on the leaf.
These people came from the Human Race's Instrument of Civilization, which was also the Human Race in the 77th Wheel of Chaos.
After Luo Zheng opened up a separate continent for them, they kept building things in Luo Zheng's Inner World.
"What is this leaf?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
One of them glanced at Luo Zheng. Of course, they knew that Luo Zheng was the master of this world.
However, these people had a natural confidence in their eyes. After all, they were not native creatures in Luo Zheng's Inner World. They would not treat Luo Zheng as an omnipotent God.
"This is the Leaf of Charm," one of them answered.
"What's the use of it?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"It can increase the speed of cultivation of all creatures in the world by several times," the man said.
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng could not help but take a few more glances. Of course, he did not expect that the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning had sent these people to repair the Inner World and build many practical things!
Standing on the leaf, he saw a small ball floating not far away. He asked again, "What are those things?"
"That is the Land of Transformation," the man answered again. He did not wait for Luo Zheng to ask and continued to explain, "Any True God can enter the Land of Transformation to cultivate, strengthen their souls, and prepare to break into the Nirvana Realm …"
"Then what are those storms further away …"
Luo Zheng did not expect that the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning and the others had built so many things while he was away!
Of course, Luo Zheng did not know that the Heavenly Primogenitor and the group of people he led had gathered most of the elites in Chaos No. 77. The most suitable cultivation method and the most convenient method were all created by them.
After confirming that Luo Zheng's Inner World was the place of origin, the Primeval Lord of Heaven was indeed very attentive.
As the most talented expert of the Human Race, Yuanshi Tianzun had investigated Luo Zheng's inner world in detail, including the settings of the forbidden areas, the composition of the races, and so on. Then, he summoned Fuxi and the others to discuss and come up with a plan to change it.
