Luo Zheng trusted these humans. He had already promised the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi that he would let them do whatever they wanted in his Inner World.
Thus, in a short period of time, the world was constantly being modified!
In addition to the modification of the world, the entire world was being deliberately guided.
Previously, the structure of Luo Zheng's world was very simple. The experts of the entire world were controlled by the Evil Deities. After the Evil Deities left, it was controlled by Xiuzhu and her family. Their purpose was not to make the humans in Luo Zheng's Inner World stronger, but to control the Inner World so that there would not be chaos.
Therefore, sometimes they would even suppress some experts.
After the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning took charge, he deliberately made changes.
They weaved the story of ancient myths and the Gate of Heaven. Only those who had reached the True God Realm could set foot on the Gate of Heaven.
In addition, they deliberately provoked a fight between several major forces. During this period of time, the cultivation atmosphere in the Inner World was extremely rich. In order to become stronger, all the creatures were madly cultivating, and this was the result that the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning wanted.
"Buzz …"
Just as Luo Zheng was learning about the situation, rays of golden light suddenly flashed in the distance. These rays of golden light soared into the sky and pierced the horizon.
"What is that?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"This … I'm not too sure," the man replied.
Luo Zheng could not find the answer, so he could only explore it himself.
His figure flashed and appeared in the place where the golden light was shining. It was actually a golden pillar.
The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning was in the center of these golden pillars. He was looking up at the golden pillars with a satisfied look on his face.
"The True Meaning of Five Years and Five Directions, the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Cloud, the True Meaning of Vanishing Void …" Luo Zheng looked at it and noticed the handwriting on it.
There were a total of fourteen golden pillars, and thirteen True Meanings of Tao were recorded on them. One of the golden pillars was blank.
"Senior Heavenly Lord of the Beginning, why did you inscribe the True Meaning of Tao here?" Luo Zheng asked.
The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning replied indifferently, "Naturally, it is for the cultivation of the thousands of creatures in the Land of Beginning. Don't forget, as the Land of Beginning, after they comprehend the True Meaning of Tao, they can also connect to the Nirvana Realm."
The creatures in his Inner World were on the Nirvana Realm with him? Luo Zheng did not consider such a problem.
"This blank golden pillar …"
As soon as Luo Zheng opened his mouth, the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning said, "It is used to inscribe the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation. However, as you said, Prince Ashoka only imparted the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation to you. Now, I will not ask Luo Zheng to inscribe it. Everything can wait until you see him."
"Then we can go now," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Luo Zheng had promised the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning before, but because of the war in the Heavenly Palace, it was delayed until now.
The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning's eyes lit up and said, "That's good!"
After Luo Zheng dispersed the incarnation in his Inner World, the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning also summoned toward the scroll.
In the blink of an eye, the spirits of Fuxi, Nvywa, Emperor Jun, Zhurong, Gonggong, and the other masters all appeared beside the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning.
They were all very interested in Prince Ashoka. They wanted to see who this Prince Ashoka was, and who could create another Nirvana Realm in the Abandoned Ashes Land!
Luo Zheng dispersed the incarnation in his Inner World, sat cross-legged, and began to silently prepare the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation.
"Buzz …"
Not long after, a mysterious True Meaning of Dao lingered around Luo Zheng, and he appeared in another space. This was the Recycle Bin where the "Mountain" had exiled him.
In the distance, a series of unique ruins were connected from end to end, forming a slender "garbage belt".
When Luo Zheng arrived in this space, the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning led Fuxi, Nvywa, and the others to appear above Luo Zheng's head. In an instant, more than thirty heads floated above his head.
Looking at the densely packed heads above his head, Luo Zheng did not know whether to laugh or cry.
The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning and the others did not care about such trifles. They opened their eyes wide and curiously looked at this strange space.
"Everything in the Abandoned Ashes Land should have been destroyed, but these things have been preserved after being exiled. How incredible …" The Heavenly Lord of the Beginning sighed.

Chapter 3355
After walking along this garbage belt for some distance, they saw the dim Ji Deleting Pillar in the distance.
The first time Luo Zheng came here, he was still confused and disoriented. In the end, he was captured by those one-eyed monsters. This time, he was familiar with the place.
"Cha cha cha …"
When Luo Zheng passed by the garbage city formed by the debris, some one-eyed monsters crawled out and stared at Luo Zheng with one of their eyes wide open.
This time, they were no longer hostile to Luo Zheng. However, when Luo Zheng passed by, one-eyed monsters kept jumping out of the ruins to watch Luo Zheng.
"The aura of these creatures is very special," Nuwa said.
After so many Chaos Epochs, all kinds of life forms were born in the Nirvana Realm. Nuwa and the others had seen many.
For example, they had seen unique creatures like the Metal Civilization and the Green Jade Civilization. Generally, no matter how strange the creatures were, they would not be surprised.
These one-eyed monsters were actually "ordinary" in appearance, but the aura they contained was very unique. It was similar to the True Meaning of the Three Spirits Construction that Luo Zheng exuded. However, it seemed to be only a part of it …
"The True Meaning of the Three Spirits Construction is composed of three kinds of energy. It can create things out of nothing at will," Luo Zheng said. "There are three kinds of monsters here. King Ashoka seems to have distributed the three kinds of energy of the True Meaning of the Three Spirits Construction to them."
"Oh?" Nuwa's eyes were full of curiosity.
Among these heroes, Nuwa was the most curious.
This was also the reason why "Nuwa" in the Mother World would tirelessly explore and search for the Nirvana Realm humans.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven was constantly looking at the surrounding environment. His eyes shone with a contemplative light …
After passing through the debris belt, the pocket-sized planet appeared in front of them.
On this pocket-sized planet stood a huge statue. It was the statue of King Ashoka.
"This statue is King Ashoka," Luo Zheng introduced.
More than thirty pairs of eyes stared at the statue of King Ashoka. They all wanted to see what kind of person King Ashoka was. Such a person could not be a nameless person in the human race.
Moreover, as Luo Zheng had said, King Ashoka was very familiar with the Path of the End of the Human Race.
This included what kind of Nirvana Realm token he had in his possession and who was the one who executed it.
Everyone looked at the statue in unison, and the atmosphere suddenly became silent.
"…"
"…"
After a long silence, Luo Zheng took the initiative to ask, "Do you recognize him?"
"I don't know him …"
"I've never seen this person before!"
They shook their heads.
"It doesn't matter. I'll make a conclusion after I see him." Luo Zheng shrugged, leaped lightly from the pocket-sized planet, and flew towards the Pillar of Deleted.
King Ashoka's palace was located between the nodes of Ji Deleting Pillars, so it was not difficult to find it.
After a short while, Luo Zheng arrived at the entrance of the palace. Yi, Nuwa, and the other phantoms floating above his head also retreated back into Luo Zheng's Inner World.
As soon as he arrived, two figures came up to him from the palace. The handsome man and the seductive woman were King Ashoka's two disciples.
King Ashoka led his disciples to explore outside the Chaos for a long time, and these two disciples were the ones who stayed behind.
They were old acquaintances with Luo Zheng.
"Brother Luo Zheng." The handsome man cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng also cupped his hands in return, and immediately asked, "May I know if your master, King Ashoka, is in the palace?"
King Ashoka had been exploring outside the Chaos for a long time, and most of the time he was not in the palace, so it was not easy to find him.
"Master happened to be meditating in the palace." The handsome man sized up Luo Zheng, and then asked, "Brother Luo Zheng, did you find out about the Nirvana Human Race?"
The two disciples of King Ashoka grew up in this world, so they only knew about the Nirvana Realm and the Chaos World in their imagination. However, they naturally knew what their master was thinking about.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
The handsome man showed a hint of joy. Master would be very happy to hear this news.
"I'll go ask Master to come out of seclusion!"
The handsome man turned and left, while the seductive woman invited Luo Zheng into the palace to take a seat.
After waiting for less than ten breaths, a figure appeared beside Luo Zheng, and at the same time, King Ashoka's hearty laughter sounded. "Hahaha, little brother Luo Zheng, you've only left for a short while, and you've already found out about the Nirvana Human Race? Could it be some groundless speculation? "
King Ashoka's face was full of anticipation, but more than that, suspicion.
King Ashoka had already learned about it through Luo Zheng, the Green Jade Spirit, and 9527.
There were always traces of the Nirvana Human Race, but in order to avoid their opponents, they hid very well. At least, civilizations like the Green Jade Civilization, which had been in the top ten, had not found any traces of them.
The possibility of Luo Zheng being able to find the Nirvana Human Race was very slim.
"It's not groundless speculation." Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
King Ashoka stared at Luo Zheng for a while, and the smile on his face gradually turned serious.
He sensed that there were many souls curled up in Luo Zheng's body!
Hum …
Nuwa, Fuxi, Houyi, Dijun, Gonggong, Zhurong …
Many former heroes of the Human Race floated above Luo Zheng's head, and at the end was the Primeval Lord of Heaven.
King Ashoka saw these people appear one by one, somewhat dumbfounded, and stood rooted to the ground.
The others did not speak, but the Primeval Lord of Heaven said, "I heard from Luo Zheng that you are extremely familiar with our Path of the End. You are definitely not a nobody in the Human Race. The three words' King Ashoka 'are not your real name. You … just who are you?"
Thump!
As soon as the Primeval Lord of Heaven finished speaking, King Ashoka fell to his knees in front of Luo Zheng.
This action gave Luo Zheng a great fright.
"Master!" After kowtowing, King Ashoka called out, his expression rather agitated.
King Ashoka's two disciples were also shocked. They had never seen King Ashoka so agitated.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven continued to stare at King Ashoka with an indifferent gaze.
Back then, he had taken in twelve personal disciples. Only these twelve personal disciples would respectfully refer to him as' Master '. Back then, in the Final Battle, some had died, while others had scattered. However, he still remembered the faces of his disciples.
Just who was this …
Seeing the Primeval Lord of Heaven's awkward expression, King Ashoka realized that he had changed his appearance countless years ago.
After thousands of chaos epochs, even his own aura had undergone tremendous changes. It was normal for his master not to recognize him.
He reached out and gently brushed his cheek, using the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction. His face began to change, finally returning to its original appearance.
"Jiang Shang?"
"Taigong Wang? Why is it this kid … "
"It's actually Jiang Ziya. You're not dead?"

Chapter 3356
In the eyes of Nuwa, Fuxi, and the others, Jiang Ziya was indeed a junior.
In the Final Battle, the entire Human Ancestor Civilization was extremely tragic. The Human Ancestor even sacrificed himself but still failed to stop him.
Jiang Ziya was quite prestigious in the Human Race at that time, but he was not the main character in the Final Battle.
In the chaos, no one noticed his movements.
Who knew that he would be deleted into the Abandoned Land of Ashes in the Mountain Space!
After more than a thousand chaos eras, Jiang Ziya was very excited to see his master again.
"Ziya …"
The Heavenly Lord of Origin was an existence that could see through everything. When he saw his beloved disciple again, he also became excited.
As for Luo Zheng, he twitched his mouth. After all this time, Prince Worriless was the disciple of the Heavenly Lord of Origin.
"How did you get exiled to the Abandoned Land of Ashes?" asked the Heavenly Lord of Origin.
Jiang Ziya's face showed a trace of contemplation. Too much time had passed, so it was very difficult for him to recall the details. After thinking for a while, he opened his mouth and told his master how he got exiled and what he encountered in this space.
Luo Zheng had heard some of it before, but this time, Jiang Ziya described it in more detail.
It turned out that although Jiang Ziya was the disciple of the Heavenly Lord of Origin, his strength was not weaker than Nuwa, Hou Yi, and the others present. In fact, in the 77th chaos, it was he who led many experts to enter the Immortal Realm and become the people present!
Among the twelve disciples of the Heavenly Lord of Origin, Jiang Ziya's position was the most important.
After Jiang Ziya finished talking about the past, including what happened in the Mountain Space, the Heavenly Lord of Origin let out a long sigh and said, "Back then, we tried to gain the advantages of our predecessors and prepared ten plans, but in the end, we failed … Since then, we have been suppressed by Genryū.
However, I didn't expect that in the end, there was still a chance that you could find this place. "
Jiang Ziya was puzzled when he heard his master's sigh. "What do you mean, master?"
The Honoured Lord of the Origin stared at Genryū and said, "For a long time, we were fighting for an exit in the Nirvana Realm. We failed to get the exit, and it is still in the hands of Genryū. That is why we were trapped in the 33 Levels of Heaven.
One of the reasons … "
"Exit? Master, are you saying that there is another exit in the 33rd Heaven?"
Jiang Ziya was also one of those who had been to the 33 Levels of Heaven.
However, when he was in the 33 Layers of Heaven, the human race did not know much about the Nirvana Realm. Therefore, he did not know that there was an exit in the 33 Layers of Heaven.
"Yes." Heavenly Lord Yuanshi's eyes were full of interest. "For so many years, I have made many speculations about the outside of the Chaos, but they are only speculations. If I don't go out in person, I'm afraid everything is just a theory on paper. Ziya, take me to have a look."
"Of course!" Jiang Ziya said.
After he got up, he seemed to have thought of something. Then, he said to Luo Zheng, "Please come with me. I want to help my master and the seniors build their bodies!"
As he spoke, Jiang Ziya stretched out his hand and instantly teleported to another place with Luo Zheng.
Jiang Ziya could control the time and space in the space of Ji Mountain. Naturally, he had the final say here.

Within Dustblood State.

Chapter 3362
This evil creature was born in one of the human tribes in the Jade Order State.

Zhou Tai was worried about the safety of the mother and the baby, so he hurriedly rushed into the delivery room.
After entering inside, the scene that Zhou Tai saw made him feel dizzy and giddy!
His wife was lying in a pool of blood, and the few midwives were decapitated. On the floor of the delivery room was a naked baby.

Judging from the face and the skin color, the baby was indeed a human being. However, there was a pair of short horns on the baby's head, which looked like the horns on the head of a demon.
This newborn baby showed an evil smile to Zhou Tai, waving his blood-stained little hands, as if he was showing off to Zhou Tai. He was telling his father that he killed his wife!

As soon as Zhou Tai realized what was going on, he pulled out the blade from his waist.
That wasn't his son, but a monster from hell!

A wild blade intent was released, chopping down towards the baby's head.

"Crack!"

Zhou Tai's Zhou Tai's heart!!!

No matter what, he had to end this Demon's life!

Zhou Tai's scream before his death attracted the guards of the City Lord's Mansion.

While the guards were still perplexed, a mysterious smile appeared on the baby's face.
"Puff puff puff puff puff …"

When the baby crawled out of the City Lord's Mansion, all of the 1,011 people in the City Lord's Mansion were dead.

"Puff puff puff puff puff …"

The originally bustling Ning An City was dead silent like a ghost town.
The innocent and romantic baby had already crawled to the city gate of Ning An City.

"Bang bang bang …"

Chapter 3365
These Greater Rats were huge, and their faces were ferocious. Pairs of soybean-sized eyes stared at them.
Seeing the unfriendly gazes of these Greater Rats, Luo Zheng asked, "Brother Zhuo Sheng, I don't see the Ear Rats?"
The Ear Rats entered the Nirvana Realm in the form of Sun Souls, and Luo Zheng had a good relationship with them.
Now that he could not see the Ear Rats, Luo Zheng felt that something was wrong.
"Most Yang Souls won't stop at the 28th Heaven. The Ear Mice will also choose to stay in Chang Rong Heaven and pass the Soul Hall's test. If it weren't for Her Highness Feng Ge and you, I naturally wouldn't have come," Zhuo Sheng replied.
"But these Greater Rats …" Luo Zheng asked hesitantly.
"Brother Luo Zheng, don't worry. These Twilight Rats are under the command of the Ear Rats, so there won't be any problems," Zhuo Sheng said. After that, he stepped into the cave first.
Feng Ge glanced at Luo Zheng, then smiled and said, "Don't be so suspicious. Let's go in too!"
Luo Zheng stood in place for a while, as if he was communicating with something. After a while, his frowning eyebrows relaxed, and he raised his leg to catch up with him. His footsteps also became firm.
The Rat Clan was good at digging underground. The Twilight Rats had lived in the 28th Heaven for several eras.
The underground world of the 28th Heaven was even more complicated than that of the Forbidden Rain City, but the Twilight Rats had obviously become a dominant force in the underground world.
After the three of them entered, one of the Twilight Rats reached out and pressed on the cave wall gently.
"Rumble …"
With a rumbling sound, a huge rock slowly rolled down, sealing the entrance tightly.
Once the entrance was sealed, the inside of the cave darkened.
"Cha!"
As soon as the cave fell into darkness, a few flames were lit.
A few Twilight Rats held torches in their hands, illuminating the surroundings.
"Prince Feng Ge, Brother Luo Zheng, please." Zhuo Sheng reached out toward the cave.
The cave near the surface of the mountain was very wide. The Twilight Rats had even tidied up the ground in the cave, so the path forward was very smooth.
However, after a few dozen miles, the cave became narrower.
Some places only allowed one person to pass.
Although the Twilight Rats were huge in size, they were still as agile as other rats. They could pass through in a flash. Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were naturally slower when they passed through.
After half a day, they had already traveled a distance of a hundred miles underground. After traveling for so long in this cramped and narrow cave, Feng Ge finally asked impatiently, "Zhuo Sheng, why haven't we reached our destination yet?"
"Your Highness Feng Ge, the Forbidden Rain City is huge to begin with. It will naturally take some time for us to traverse more than half of the city to reach the northwestern corner of the city," replied Zhuo Sheng.
"Creak! Don't worry, it won't take long for them to arrive. Why are you in such a hurry? "A Twilight Mouse said coldly.
Zhuo Sheng turned around and glared fiercely at the Twilight Rat. The Twilight Rat lowered its head and said no more.
Feng Ge blinked and quietly looked at Luo Zheng. No matter how slow she was, she could sense that something was wrong at this moment.
Luo Zheng secretly gave Feng Ge a look, indicating that he should keep quiet.
Zhuo Sheng was a member of the Greater One Heavenly Palace and was still in the Heavenly Palace. If he betrayed the Heavenly Palace, he would not be able to leave alive. Therefore, Zhuo Sheng would not do such a thing.
Deducing from this, either this Zhuo Sheng was a fake, or his Yang Soul was being controlled …
As for these Twilight Rats, they might not be related to the Eared Rats.
"Senior Yuanshi Tianzun, I'm afraid something is really wrong now," said Luo Zheng in his mind.
In fact, when they were outside the Forbidden Rain City, Luo Zheng had already sensed it. At that time, he wanted to warn Feng Ge and reject Zhuo Sheng's request. However, the Yuanshi Tianzun told Luo Zheng in his mind to bide his time.
Now that they had been traveling underground for so long, they were probably in trouble. Luo Zheng naturally hoped that the Yuanshi Tianzun would come out and solve the problem.
"Don't worry," said the Yuanshi Tianzun calmly. "This is only the 28th Layer of Heaven. I can handle it."
In the Splitting Heaven Temple, the Yuanshi Tianzun had killed the Moon Fire Slave with a single finger.
At that time, the Moon Fire Slave's strength was suppressed, but the Yuanshi Tianzun's strength was also suppressed. As long as he did not encounter the true experts of the Meta-Spirit Race, the Yuanshi Tianzun still had a way to deal with them.
"Don't listen to the nonsense of these rats. After all, they're just a bunch of animals, hehe." Zhuo Sheng explained and continued to lead the way.
After passing through the narrow section, the road ahead gradually widened.
Not long after, the three of them and the Twilight Rats arrived at a circular cave. The walls of the cave were carefully carved and did not look like a cave at all. It looked more like a magnificent palace.
After Luo Zheng and Feng Ge entered the cave, Zhuo Sheng said, "This is below the Star Ring."
"Can we enter the Star Ring if we go up?" Feng Ge asked.
"Yes." Zhuo Sheng nodded. "Unfortunately, you won't be alive to go up …"
Thump, thump, thump …
There were six tunnels around the circular cave. Green light shone from each of the six tunnels. At the same time, heavy footsteps could be heard. Soon, Moonfire Slaves covered in green flames walked out of the tunnels one after another.
Feng Ge took a step back and leaned against Luo Zheng. She frowned and stared at Zhuo Sheng. "Zhuo Sheng, what's the meaning of this?"
Zhuo Sheng chuckled. "Nothing. Someone wants to take Your Highness's life. I'm just following orders to bring Your Highness here."
As soon as he finished speaking, the Moon Fire Slaves had already entered the cave. The temperature inside the cave was rising continuously.
"Then are you Zhuo Sheng or not?" Luo Zheng asked.
Zhuo Sheng felt that Luo Zheng was very calm. He was puzzled by Luo Zheng's calmness, and even a little uneasy. He answered impatiently, "Whether I'm Zhuo Sheng or not is none of your business."
"I'm just curious. Zhuo Sheng is obviously in the Heavenly Palace. If he betrayed the Heavenly Palace, he won't be able to live. You shouldn't be him," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
"Hehehehe … So what if I'm not?"
The shape of Zhuo Sheng's Sun Soul gradually changed, and his voice became louder. Although his appearance was still unclear, it was obvious that he had changed into another person!
It was someone else's Sun Soul disguised as Zhuo Sheng.
"If you're Zhuo Sheng, I can show mercy. If you're not, then you're dead meat," Luo Zheng replied in a flat tone.
"Is that so? Hehehe … Ask yourself if you can live first! "The man retreated while pointing at Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
The Moon Fire Slaves, who had been slowly approaching, charged over from different directions.
"Luo Zheng, we can only fight!" Feng Ge said with a serious look.

Chapter 3366
At the bottom of the deep blue sea, there was a gigantic creature drifting about.
Even though most of its body was hidden in the darkness, one could still make out a huge silhouette through the dark sea. It had the shape of a human, but its limbs were tentacles that were hundreds of feet long.
The Enchanted Fishman was an extremely mysterious Nirvana race.
This race had very few people, but they were extremely powerful. They were the most powerful beings in the sea!
The outside world also knew very little about the Enchanted Fishmen. In the records, the Enchanted Fishmen had only broken one of the largest bone cavities a long time ago and launched an attack on the living creatures inside.
Most of the time, they lived in the deepest part of the sea and lived a peaceful life.
The Meta-Spirit Clan had talked to them before. The Wukong Clan had also come, and some other Nirvana races wanted to use the Enchanted Fishman's power to control Fandu Heaven.
Maybe it was a deliberate arrangement by the Creator, or maybe it was a coincidence.
Whether it was the Golden-Winged Birds in the 29th heaven or the Enchanted Fishman in the 31st heaven, they couldn't be controlled by the outside world. In the end, these races chose to give up.
However, Genryū didn't give up completely. Even though the Enchanted Fishman was a powerful existence at the top of the food chain, there were still internal disputes.
Once in a while, the Elemental Spirit Clan would help this Animatronic Fishman named Kudo to the throne, helping him eliminate his opponents and become the king of the Animatronic Fishman Clan.
Even though Quando became the king of the Enchanted Fishmen, the Enchanted Fishman still didn't care about Genryū. However, Quando made a promise to Genryū that it would help the Wukong Clan at a critical moment.
So this time, Quando chose to help.
The Celestial Spear in its hand was given to it by the Wukong Clan. This spear was surging with terrifying power. When it held the spear, it was shocked by the power within.
Quando had been waiting for a long time under the land of calamity bone. It couldn't attack the creatures inside the land of calamity bone.
However, once it left the land of calamity bone, it could attack at will.
In theory, if Luo Zheng couldn't accept the mark of calamity bone, it wouldn't be able to attack Luo Zheng in the sea.
Quando also felt strange about Genryū's order.
Why was Genryū so sure that Luo Zheng would obtain the Calamity Bone Seal?
But as Ku Duo silently watched and waited, Luo Zheng really did obtain the Calamity Bone Engraving. He even directly obtained the ninth Calamity Bone Engraving!
This human …
At this moment, Ku Duo was also shocked.
But since Ku Duo decided to kill Luo Zheng, he wouldn't hold back.
Even with the Body of Myriad Calamity and the nine Calamity Bones, it would still be difficult for him to face a full-powered attack from Ku Duo, not to mention that Ku Duo had this Celestial Spear.
It could amplify one's strength by nine times!
It could unleash a force of 15,000 Godly Jun, and when this Celestial Spear was thrust out, it would be a force of 130,000 Godly Jun!
The strength of over a hundred thousand Shen Jun, what a terrifying strength!
"Phew …"
The huge resistance of the seawater had no effect on Qudo or the Celestial Spear.
The spear in the water was like a bat shuttling through the night, heading straight for Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng, who was at the bottom of the light beam, had no time to dodge.
He attempted to swim upwards a few times, but clearly, it was too late.
'This spear is unimaginably powerful. I'm afraid I can only take it head-on!'
Just as Luo Zheng had this thought, the Celestial Spear had already reached his chest.
"Ka!"
The spear pierced through the surface of Luo Zheng's body, letting out a weird sound.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit in his body flashed with white light, and the maelstrom formed by the fusion of the Chaotic Origin Spirit rotated once …
The Chaotic Origin Spirit that was strengthened by six times had digested the strength of over 100,000 Godly Jun. The remaining strength flowed through Luo Zheng's chest and spread into his body. The Calamity Bone that had fused with Luo Zheng's fleshy body and the nine Calamity Bone Engraving helped him to take on the spear easily!
The Celestial Spear merely pierced through Luo Zheng's chest slightly, piercing through one of his ribs, causing traces of blood to spread out in the sea.
After taking on this spear that could destroy the world, Luo Zheng didn't even suffer a light injury …
When that Devil-Controlled Fishman saw this, it was so shocked that its eyeballs were about to fall out.
Honestly speaking, if this spear had pierced through its body, its tough body would have been easily pierced through. The strength contained within the spear was sufficient to shatter its body into pieces.
Such a terrifying spear …
This human ate it alive?
And he was completely unharmed?
Was this fellow really a human!
The Devil-Controlled Fishman was innately strong. It naturally understood how weak many Paramita Lifeforms were.
And in its eyes, humans were the weakest of all!
But the scene before it had completely subverted its imagination!
"Weng …"
After thrusting out this spear, the spearhead of the Celestial Spear rapidly dimmed.
The Celestial Stone was not compatible with other Paramita Keepsakes. After using it once, it would require a long time to recover. This was also one of the weaknesses of the Celestial Stone. Hence, weapons forged from this stone were usually meant to kill with a single strike.
It was the same for some experts in the Mother World after fusing with this Paramita Keepsakes. If they could not kill the other party in a single strike, they could only flee.
When Ku Duo found that his attack could not cause any harm to Luo Zheng, he fell into fear.
He took the spear and waved the tentacles on his limbs, intending to retreat.
"Crack!"
Luo Zheng grabbed the Celestial Spear with a backhand. He stared into the darkness and asked, "Who are you!"
How could Ku Duo care about Luo Zheng? He just wanted to pull the spear back.
But Luo Zheng, whose physical body had been greatly strengthened, could now exert a considerable amount of the power of the Weapon of Civilization because his physical body could bear it!
"Huff, huff, huff …"
The hand that held the Celestial Spear suddenly snatched it, and the spear completely fell into Luo Zheng's hands.
He stabbed down with a backhand. Then, he slashed!
The strength of more than 50,000 Godly Jun burst out. The faint blue water current formed a fierce tornado.
When the spear slashed down, it was like an indestructible broadsword!
"Puff!"
The tip of the spear slashed down, and the tentacles were directly cut off. Black blood rolled in the sea.
Luo Zheng wanted to chase after it, but Ku Duo was a creature that lived in the sea all year round. His escaping speed in the sea was surprisingly fast. In the blink of an eye, he had disappeared. Below him was only a black sea …
"Most likely he's from the Genryū." Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders and swam toward the light beam.
Following the light beam back to the space where the Bone of Calamity was, many Paramita creatures looked at Luo Zheng in surprise.

Chapter 3367
The human race's star ring stood at the top of a blood-colored mountain.
The ring of light in the middle of the star ring trembled, and two figures appeared near it.
After Luo Zheng's vision returned to normal, he scanned his surroundings and was slightly stunned.
At the top of the blood-colored mountain was a circular platform. There were seven or eight star rings in the center of the circular platform. Apart from the human race, there were also other races that had ascended to this place. There were dozens of Sun Souls around the circular platform.
These Sun Souls were of different shapes and sizes. There were all kinds of races, but there were no humans.
When they saw Luo Zheng and Feng Ge's physical bodies, their eyes lit up.
"These two … … have physical bodies?"
"How did they do it?"
"That human girl seems to be a Paramita lifeform?"
Feng Ge could clearly see his appearance, so he was naturally identified as a Paramita lifeform.
Luo Zheng ignored these Sun Souls. Instead, he walked to the edge of the circular platform and sized up his surroundings.
The entire Chang Rong Heaven was filled with blood-colored peaks. Each peak was separated by a considerable distance, and below the peaks was a bottomless abyss. At the bottom of the abyss was a flowing red liquid.
"Starting from the 29th Layer of Heaven, the Paramita Realm becomes extremely dangerous," Feng Ge said. "The bottom of Chang Rong Heaven is filled with 'Boiling Blood', which is the red liquid below. Any lifeform that falls into the Boiling Blood will be completely melted."
"But these shouldn't be dangerous, right?" Luo Zheng's gaze had already retracted from the bottom and landed on one side of the peaks. Long plank roads had been built between these peaks. As long as one didn't court death and jump into the Boiling Blood, they would naturally be able to pass through safely.
Moreover, Luo Zheng knew how to fly, so it was naturally easier for him.
"My human friend, the Boiling Blood at the bottom is naturally nothing, but don't forget that there are golden-winged birds in the sky!" A Sun Soul that was ten meters tall approached.
"Golden-winged birds … …"
Luo Zheng raised his head and looked.
He could vaguely see the shadows of giant birds in the sky, but those giant birds were only circling in the deep sky.
"I've checked the information." Feng Ge nodded and said, "The absolute overlords of Chang Rong Heaven are the golden-winged birds. Even the Nirvana Realm beings of the 29th Heaven are prey for those birds, not to mention Yang Souls."
The Yang Soul was much weaker than the physical body. Even if it had entered the Soul City, it would still be weak unless it completely passed the test of the Soul City, formed the Colorless Thought Body, and reached the Saint Soul Realm.
"Right, right, right!"
The ten-meter-tall Yang Soul moved closer and said, "Chang Rong Heaven is too dangerous. We've been staying here all this time in order to wait for an opportunity to go to Soul City. Since the two of you have physical bodies, can you protect us along the way?"
"I'm from the Lion Clan. If the two of you are willing to escort me through, please name your price …"
"…"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of curiosity. "Are you trapped here?"
"Yes! Those golden-winged birds are cunning. As long as we're on this round platform, we'll be safe. Once we step onto the long plank road, we'll be targeted by the golden-winged birds, "the Lion Clan member said with a nod.
Beside him, a smaller Yang Soul had already arrived at the edge of the round platform. He quietly took a step toward the plank road and said at the same time, "Friend, if you don't believe me, take a look!"
The Yang Soul had just stood on the plank road for a short while when a gust of wind and clouds surged in the sky. A huge winged shadow rushed down at an unbelievable speed and landed beside the plank road.
Before the golden-winged bird rushed down, the small Yang Soul had already retracted his foot.
"Yo!"
The golden-winged bird let out an angry cry and ferociously bit at the small Yang Soul.
However, just as its head touched the platform, blood-colored lightning sizzled. After the golden-winged bird let out a miserable cry, it hurriedly flapped its wings and retreated. Then, it rushed into the sky, and the air was filled with a burnt smell.
"That's how it is," the small Yang Soul said.
The tall Yang Soul chuckled and said, "Ugly Yan, you teased the golden-winged bird like this. It hates you. When you get on the plank road, it will attack you first."
"Ugly Yan" said indifferently, "In any case, these birds will always be guarding here. At most, I won't go to Soul City for the rest of my life …"
When Luo Zheng heard their conversation, he realized that these people had probably been trapped here for some time.
If Chang Rong Tian had always been like this, theoretically speaking, it was impossible to pass.
He thought for a moment before asking, "That's not right. If those golden-winged birds have always been here, how did the others get to Soul City?"
"We can only wait for an opportunity," Feng Ge said. "I heard that as long as there's a blood fog, the golden-winged birds are unwilling to come down. Taking advantage of the blood fog, everyone can quickly rush to Soul City and be safe!"
"How often does the blood fog come?" Luo Zheng asked.
"We've been here for a long time. In any case, we haven't seen the blood fog," the other Yang Soul replied.
The birth of the blood fog was completely random. Sometimes, the blood fog could cover more than half of Chang Rong Tian. Sometimes, even if the blood fog was born, it could only cover a small area and still could not reach Soul City.
"Then you want us to fight against those strange birds?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes!" the small Yang Soul said.
"It's too dangerous." Feng Ge rejected this suggestion. "Those golden-winged birds are the overlords of this place. If they swarm over, you won't be able to resist them!"
Although Feng Ge had a certain level of confidence in Luo Zheng, he did not want him to take risks recklessly.
When the Yang Souls heard Feng Ge's objection, they immediately lost interest and dispersed. Some even chanted illusion-breaking incantations and left the other side.
At this moment, the Primeval Lord of Heaven's voice suddenly sounded in Luo Zheng's mind. "How strange. Chang Rong Tian's blood fog incantation has been lost?"
"Blood fog incantation? What's that? "Luo Zheng asked.
"Heh, looks like Genryū has worked hard these years and washed away many things." The Primeval Lord of Heaven smiled helplessly. "The Large-Winged Golden Condors of Chang Rong Tian are indeed powerful. Even the Heavenly Master is a Large-Winged Golden Condor. This is very rare."
Generally speaking, the creatures of the Dark Domain were much stronger than the creatures of the Light Domain. However, the Large-Winged Golden Condors dominated both the Dark Domain and the Light Domain of Chang Rong Tian. This was indeed very rare.
Fortunately, the Large-Winged Golden Condors were very arrogant!
No matter how hard Genryū tried, he could not rope them in. Although this race was dangerous and cruel, they did not listen to Genryū.
This race that stood at the top of Chang Rong Tian feared the boiling blood that flowed in the Abyss the most. In reality, most of the creatures of the other side in Chang Rong Tian relied on boiling blood to survive.

Chapter 3368
The Paramita Realm was not unchanged. The Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning and the others had not returned for a long time, and some things that used to be easy had already undergone earth-shaking changes.
Luo Zheng was now somewhat hesitant. Should he listen to Feng Ge and wait for the blood fog to appear before leaving, or should he directly barge through it?
If there really was the Blood Fog Curse that the Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning had mentioned, it would be best!
"Senior Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning, I wonder if the Blood Fog Curse is still effective?" Luo Zheng asked doubtfully.
The Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning said indifferently, "Try it and you will naturally know."
This Blood Fog Curse was the same as the Illusion Breaking Curse. It was not difficult. In less than half an hour, Luo Zheng had already grasped it. Then, he walked to the edge of the round platform.
When Feng Ge and the other Yang Souls saw Luo Zheng's actions, they were somewhat confused. They thought that Luo Zheng was going to learn from the short Yang Soul and tease the golden-winged bird.
Then, they heard Luo Zheng mutter something.
As Luo Zheng chanted, the boiling blood in the abyss at the foot of the mountain boiled even more violently, as if some ferocious beast was about to emerge from the boiling blood.
When the boiling blood reached a certain level, wisps of bright red blood fog floated up!
When Feng Ge and the Yang Souls saw this scene, their pupils immediately widened.
"The blood fog has appeared so quickly?"
"No, he can summon the blood fog?"
"I've never heard of such a method …"
The Blood Fog Curse was not a complicated method, but the Meta-Spirit Race had gone through a long period of time to bury this method in history. Of course, there were still a small number of people who had mastered this method, but it was not known to outsiders.
When the Yang Souls saw the blood fog spreading around the plank road, they cheered and rushed into the plank road.
However, the blood fog seemed to be spreading with Luo Zheng as the center. If Luo Zheng did not move forward, the blood fog would not move forward, so the Yang Souls could only stop on the plank road and wait for Luo Zheng to move forward.
"Let's go," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
Feng Ge immediately asked in a low voice, "Was it that old man who did it?"
"Yes!"
Luo Zheng nodded. After he carried the scroll, he gained a lot of inexplicable abilities. As an insider, Feng Ge could naturally tell, so Luo Zheng had nothing to hide.
It was unknown who had built the long plank road, but it was connected to one lonely peak after another.
When the Large-Winged Golden Eagles saw someone walking on the plank road below, they spread their wings and swooped down. However, when they approached the blood mist, they let out disgusted cries and flapped their wings to quickly lift themselves up …
Luo Zheng's group moved on the plank road, and the blood fog moved with them.
About half an hour later, the blood mist that followed Luo Zheng all the way gradually faded. After Luo Zheng recited the Illusion Breaking Spell again, the blood mist became thicker again.
Just like that, they passed through one mountain after another. Suddenly, a huge blood-red mountain appeared in front of them. Compared to it, the surrounding blood-red mountains were just small dots.
At the top of the mountain was a huge platform, and on the platform floated a blurry city.
"That's Soul City?" Luo Zheng looked at the blurry city.
Rather than calling it a city, it was better to call it a mirage …
The entire city was not a real existence at all, but an illusion on the top of the mountain.
"It should be." Feng Ge's eyes were shining. Although she had looked up a lot of information, this was the first time she had seen Soul City.
"But this is just an illusion," Luo Zheng said with a frown.
"That's not an illusion," a Sun Soul behind said.
These Sun Souls were able to smoothly come here all thanks to Luo Zheng. They had nothing to repay Luo Zheng, so they were naturally willing to contribute the information they knew. The Sun Soul continued, "Soul City itself is made up of souls. It is said that after thousands of souls were absorbed into Soul City, it became the current scale.
That's why the city you see is similar to our current Yang Soul Body, hehe … "
"A city formed by Sun Souls is actually so huge …" Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with shock.
Not long after, Luo Zheng led Feng Ge through the tall and slender plank road and reached the top of the huge mountain. The blood mist that had followed Luo Zheng dissipated here.
The gate of Soul City was grayish-brown and extremely huge.
Two huge square pillars sat on both sides. When Luo Zheng walked to the side of the square pillar and looked at it, he heard a "wuwu" sound coming from the pillar.
The square pillar itself was formed by Sun Souls, and the dark brown soul matter inside was constantly floating. At the same time, all kinds of strange faces floated on the surface of the square pillar.
"These Sun Souls are still alive?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
"Of course they are alive, but they will always be a part of the entire Soul City and will never be reincarnated, hehe …" The voice of the Primeval Lord of Heaven floated out.
"It seems that I thought too simply of Soul City," Luo Zheng muttered.
Soul City tested the Sun Soul itself, and only Sun Souls that were at the peak of the Indigo Soul Body were qualified to enter. Soul City opened once every sixty-two hours, and most Sun Souls would leave the other side after arriving here, waiting for the gate to open before entering.
As for the Sun Souls that stayed at the gate of Soul City, most of them were Sun Souls that were still some distance away from the Indigo Soul Body. They either sat cross-legged at the gate of Soul City to cultivate, or thought of ways to collect soul crystals.
"Feng Ge, wait for me here," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Zheng's physical body had come, but his Sun Soul Body was still in the lower heaven. If he wanted to enter Soul City, he naturally had to enter with his Sun Soul Body. Feng Ge had a natural advantage on this point. After all, his physical body was only equivalent to an "armor" in the other side, so he didn't need to go through as much trouble as Luo Zheng. Now, he sent Feng Ge over.
Now that he had arrived, it was time to get his Sun Soul up.
After Feng Ge nodded, he stayed where he was and waited.
Luo Zheng's Sun Soul also took some time to come from the lower heaven. Without the protection of his physical body, even an Indigo Soul Body was relatively weak, not to mention that he still had to pass through the twenty-eighth heaven.
Fortunately, Genryū placed most of his attention on his physical body, so he wasn't strict with the inspection of his Sun Soul. Moreover, it wasn't easy for them to identify Luo Zheng's Sun Soul.
After passing through the twenty-eighth heaven without any mishap, he was once again teleported to the twenty-ninth heaven of Chang Rong Heaven. Using the Blood Mist Curse, Luo Zheng traveled without stopping. It took him more than half a day to finally appear at the gate of Soul City.
The two of them continued to wait in front of the gate of Soul City. After about twenty hours, Sun Souls gradually appeared.
The Liyuan Clan, the Ashwolf Clan, the Minyue Clan, the Human Clan …
There were Sun Souls from all the clans.
The square in front of Soul City, which was originally quiet, also became noisy.

Chapter 3369
Luo Zheng smiled when he heard the Ear Mouse's voice.
Generally, the market price was a million soul elixirs for a soul crystal. These Ear Mice were offering three million soul elixirs, which was already very black-hearted.
However, the Sun Souls who had already arrived at the entrance of Soul City were eager to pass the trial. If they were really a little lacking, they would have no choice but to pay three million soul elixirs. Moreover, the status of the experts who had cultivated to this level was definitely not low, so they could afford to pay so many soul elixirs.
After the bustling at the entrance of Soul City for a while, the gate of Soul City made a "kacha" sound and slowly opened to the side.
At the same time that the door opened, faces appeared on the door formed by the Yang Soul Body, constantly letting out wails of anguish.
When the entire Soul City opened, a vast soul aura was released from within!
Luo Zheng had seen the Yang Soul aura of experts in the Immortal Realm, such as Emperor Donghuang and Emperor Jun. However, even the experts in the Immortal Realm couldn't be compared with the vast aura in front of him.
This Soul City was formed by trillions of souls, and naturally could not be compared.
"The city is open …"
"I hope that this time, I can go one step further!"
"I've been stuck at that step for many years, and I've failed every time …"
As soon as the door opened, many people strode in.
"Let's go in too," Luo Zheng said.
Then, he and Feng Ge followed the other Sun Souls and stepped into Soul City.
After entering Soul City, the omnipresent soul aura wrapped around Luo Zheng. Generally speaking, soul aura could be seen as the release of "consciousness". For example, when someone's killing intent was strong, their soul aura would become very sharp, like a sharp knife.
Weak souls would feel very uncomfortable after feeling the aura that was rich in killing intent. If the soul was too weak, it would even be directly obliterated!
If it was a gentle aura, it could comfort the other party and even have the effect of repairing the soul …
Countless souls were mixed in Soul City, and the aura contained within was incomparably complex. It also had a very complicated effect on Luo Zheng.
It was as if he could hear the emotions of millions of people at the same time. Crying, sadness, rejection, anger, excitement, happiness …
Luo Zheng was like this, and so was Feng Ge.
This kind of 'listening' and not hearing was something that couldn't be discarded. Being in Soul City, one could only accept it. In fact, the test had already begun when the two of them took their first step into Soul City.
It was also because Luo Zheng and Feng Ge had long stepped into the Extreme Indigo Soul Stage. Their Yang Souls were very stable.
Not far away, a Sun Soul was walking through Soul City with ease at first. Soon, that Sun Soul began to stagger. The pressure from the complex spiritual aura gradually increased, causing that Sun Soul to be unable to bear the burden. It tried to cover its ears to avoid this aura, but this action …
It must be futile.
When it reached the limit of its endurance, it let out a shrill scream, as if it had used up the last of its strength to rush out of the Soul City. When its soul power was exhausted, it finally reached the outside of the Soul City and lay on the ground, dying.
Many people in the city noticed this scene, and most of their eyes were filled with pity.
"That guy sure has guts …"
"He hasn't even reached the Extreme Indigo Soul Stage, yet he dares to barge into Soul City. He's simply courting death!"
"Fortunately, he escaped when he couldn't hold on any longer. Otherwise, he would have become a part of Soul City!"
After entering Soul City for a certain distance, pillars appeared in front of them. The pillars were only half the height of a person, and one could climb up with a leap. The Sun Souls walking at the front all rushed to climb up the pillars and sat cross-legged on them.
While Luo Zheng was still sizing them up, Feng Ge said, "These are the Soul Pillars. Let's go up too!"
"Soul Pillars?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Yes, the test in Soul City is completed on the Soul Pillars. As long as you pass this test, your soul will be cleansed of all dust, become a colorless Thought Body, and enter the Divine Soul Stage," Feng Ge said.
However, Feng Ge only understood this through reading up on information. In fact, she didn't know much about what exactly one would experience on the Soul Pillars.
Luo Zheng nodded. After finding a Soul Pillar, he gently leaped onto it and sat down cross-legged. Feng Ge found a Soul Pillar beside Luo Zheng and sat down.
When they sat down, a number appeared on the Soul Pillars. Feng Ge and Luo Zheng both showed "1", while the Sun Soul on Luo Zheng's right showed 63. On the Soul Pillar below a Sun Soul further away, a number of more than 1,300 appeared.
Luo Zheng was still looking around when Feng Ge said, "This number should be the number of times we entered Soul City. This is our first time entering, so it's naturally one time."
"That person's number is more than 1,300. Doesn't that mean he's entered Soul City more than a thousand times?" Luo Zheng asked.
"That's right." Feng Ge nodded.
After Luo Zheng sat on the Soul Pillar, there was no movement from the Soul Pillar. However, more and more Sun Souls entered Soul City, and some humans also entered in groups.
Among them, six or seven people entered the range of the Soul Pillar and walked towards Feng Ge.
"Your Highness Feng Ge!" Someone called out.
Feng Ge had a physical body in Soul City, just like the other Nirvana Realm creatures, so he was very eye-catching. The disciples of the Heavenly Palace naturally saw him at a glance.
Feng Ge looked at the Sun Soul and asked, "You are?"
"I'm Liu Hen," that person replied.
"I'm Qin Hua," another person also introduced himself.
"I'm Li Bianxue," another woman also said.
These people were all disciples of the Third Floor, and they were quite famous in Tai Yi Heavenly Palace. Feng Ge had naturally seen them before, but he wasn't that familiar with them. After introducing themselves, Feng Ge naturally understood.
Feng Ge smiled and said, "That's a coincidence, I'll break through to the Saint Soul Realm with you all."
Entering Soul City to break through to the Saint Soul Realm was never an easy thing.
Every attempt was an extremely painful test for the soul. Some people would even give up on their own initiative after several attempts, unwilling to take this step for the rest of their lives. Those who could endure hundreds or even thousands of attempts were also extremely tenacious, which was why they were able to persevere.
For people like Feng Ge who entered Soul City for the first time, he was a standard newcomer in the eyes of others.
"Your Highness Feng Ge also needs to work hard," Liu Hen encouraged.
After Li Bianxue introduced herself, she looked at the other soul pillar and asked, "Your Highness Fengge, may I know who this is?"
In fact, Li Bianxue was asking the obvious. After the battle in the Heavenly Palace, Li Bianxue knew that Feng Ge and Luo Zheng had a close relationship. Stepping into the 29th Layer of Heaven together, this person was most likely the abnormally strong Luo Zheng.
"This is Luo Zheng," Feng Ge replied briefly.
After the battle with the Golden Crow, Luo Zheng didn't return to the Land of Sword Grief, but his name was earth-shattering in the Land of Sword Grief.
Before Ru Hechi went to the Dragon Cliff, he only said one sentence in the Land of Sword Grief, "If not for Luo Zheng in my Heavenly Palace, the Human Clan of the Center God Province would have been wiped out."

Chapter 3370
Liu Hen and the other disciples of the Third Floor also looked at Luo Zheng's Sun Soul.
In terms of Yang Soul alone, there was indeed nothing special about Luo Zheng.
After all, those who could enter Soul City were all at the Extreme Indigo Soul level. Liu Hen and Qin Hua had been stuck at the Extreme Indigo Soul level for many years.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge, who had just climbed to the 29th level of Changrong Heaven, were absolute newcomers.
However, Luo Zheng was too famous in the Heavenly Palace.
In the entire Land of Sword Grief, Li Bianxue was the only one who participated in the Jinwu Dialogue. Li Bianxue was probably just a bystander in the Taiyi Sect.
For example, Liu Hen and Qin Hua could only choose to enter the 12th level and become Divine Generals.
"So it's Sir Luo Zheng!"
"Nice to meet you, nice to meet you!"
Liu Hen, Qin Hua, and a few other disciples of the Third Floor came forward and greeted Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly at them and said, "This is the first time Feng Ge and I have entered Soul City, so we are very unfamiliar with this place. I hope that seniors can guide us."
"Heh, Sir Luo Zheng is joking. With Sir Luo Zheng's strength, it is not difficult for him to pass the trial," Liu Hen said.
"Not at all …" Luo Zheng shook his head.
While they were talking, the Soul Pillar below Luo Zheng suddenly flickered and began to vibrate slightly.
When Qin Hua saw this, he said, "The Soul Pillar is about to rise. Let's go up too!"
There were many Soul Pillars in Soul City. There were many Soul Pillars behind Luo Zheng that were empty. Qin Hua, Liu Hen, and the others were familiar with this process. With a light leap, they jumped onto a Soul Pillar and sat down cross-legged.
The number on Li Bianxue's Soul Pillar was 17. This meant that this was her seventeenth time in Soul City. Qin Hua's Soul Pillar was 198, and Liu Hen's was 230. The two of them had stayed in Soul City for quite some time.
"Ai, Brother Liu Hen, with that Luo Zheng's strength, wouldn't it be easy for him to pass through Soul City?" Qin Hua secretly asked from the side.
Liu Hen quietly snorted, "As far as I know, the reason why that kid was able to shine in the battle with the Golden Crow was not because of his own strength, but because he had an expert guiding him from behind!"
Liu Hen and Qin Hua, as disciples of the Third Floor of the Sword of Grief, were extremely proud. In the entire Sword of Grief, they only obeyed Li Bianxue.
Now that Luo Zheng had suddenly appeared, it was easy to imagine the difference in their hearts.
Liu Hen was the son of the Lord of Mount Taixiu. His father's death was a huge blow to him. Naturally, he would try his best to find out what had happened to his father. Naturally, he would investigate Luo Zheng's deeds more clearly than the average person.
"I knew it. How could a kid who didn't even pass the Soul City challenge be able to fight against those strong people from Jinwu? The Heavenly Palace has almost made him into a god-like figure. That's too much," Qin Hua suddenly realized.
"Heh, it's his first time on the Soul Pillar. It seems that he doesn't even know what kind of test he's about to face. Let's watch him make a fool of himself later," Liu Hen sneered.
At this moment, the Soul Pillar below everyone shook again, and then began to slowly rise from the ground.
The Soul Pillar that Luo Zheng was sitting on also rose. One zhang, two zhang, three zhang …
Just as the Soul Pillar rose, a brown cloud above the Soul City pressed down. That cloud seemed to be a mixture of many souls. Countless distorted faces struggled and howled in the cloud.
The soul cloud went down, and the Soul Pillar went up. As Luo Zheng continued to get closer to the cloud, the pressure of the soul became stronger and stronger!
"Is this the Soul City's test?" Luo Zheng said with a frown.
If it was simply to deal with the pressure of the soul, then it would be enough to strengthen one's own Yang Soul.
Since Luo Zheng began to cultivate his soul, there had been no changes until now.
However, the reality was not so. After reaching the Ultimate Indigo Soul, everyone's soul had really reached a limit. Even if they replenished their soul crystals, they could not improve their Yang Soul.
Therefore, Luo Zheng did not believe that the Soul City's test was to deal with the pressure of the soul.
Five zhang …
Ten zhang …
Twenty zhang …
When the Soul Pillar rose to twenty zhang, it stopped. The Soul Cloud above also stopped descending, hovering above all the Yang Souls. It was only a few zhang away.
At this distance, Luo Zheng could observe the Soul Cloud more clearly.
There were all kinds of strange faces inside the Soul Cloud. There were also some sharp and small crystals. It was because of these small crystals that the Soul Cloud reflected all kinds of light.
Now, they were directly entering the Soul Cloud?
Just as Luo Zheng was guessing, a sound suddenly came from inside the Soul Cloud.
It was still wailing, but now the wailing had become very strange.
The tone and pronunciation of the wailing sound became very rhythmic. Under the fluctuating tone and rhythm, it formed a pleasant song!
"Singing …" Luo Zheng stared at the Soul Cloud with a face full of surprise.
This situation gave Luo Zheng a very strange feeling. It was as if the Soul Cloud itself had turned into a singing girl. However, this girl did not have any vocal organs and could not sing. Therefore, it used the mouths of the souls attached to the Soul Cloud to form a …
A song!
At this time, the voice of the Heavenly Lord of the Origin came again. "Listen carefully, Luo Zheng. This song contains the comprehension to the Colorless Thought Body."
"The comprehension to the Colorless Thought Body is in the song?" Luo Zheng was shocked. "But I can't understand this voice at all …"
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin smiled and said, "Then can you understand Sanskrit?"
Luo Zheng could not understand Sanskrit. He did not have Luo Nian's heaven-defying deciphering ability. If he wanted to decipher Sanskrit systematically, he would need to practice for a very long time …
Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment and immediately reacted. "Senior Heavenly Lord of the Origin, you mean, this song …"
"Yes, it's their voice," the Heavenly Lord of the Origin answered.
Sanskrit could not be read in the Chaos.
If one had to read and understand it, it could only be done through deciphering.
Just like Luo Nian translated a Sanskrit into the corresponding language, he did not "read" the Sanskrit himself.
But in fact, in addition to the language, Sanskrit also corresponded to a special pronunciation, which could be read. However, there was no one in the Chaos World who could read Sanskrit directly …
"So those Sanskrit words can be read with such a strange pronunciation," Luo Zheng listened attentively.
The voice sometimes rose and fell, sometimes meandered, like a poem, like a song, or like people scolding each other in the marketplace. It seemed that it could only be understood from the spiritual level and could not be described with words.

Chapter 3371
Every time the blood-colored lightning struck on Luo Zheng's body, the True Dao Intent would surge out from Luo Zheng's limbs and bones like a tide.
This True Dao Intent would flow around his body, reviving the weakening Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Dozens of blood-colored lightning struck on Luo Zheng's body consecutively, making him look extremely dangerous. But after that, the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise's eyes widened.
'He is unharmed? How is this possible? '
The Blood Spirit Black Tortoise's eyes were filled with disbelief.
Cultivators who had just entered the Myriad Calamity Body could only withstand a force of 80,000 to 90,000 Divine Jun. After entering the Undying Painting and reaching the Undying Realm, it would be strengthened further.
Even if that round of blood-colored lightning did not kill this brat, it should still tear his body apart. But now, Luo Zheng seemed unharmed?
Actually, the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise had seen such a bizarre phenomenon before.
Those Sacred Soul Realm experts who had fused with the Chaotic Origin Spirit would have such a reaction when defending against ordinary attacks. All the energy that struck them would be absorbed by the Chaotic Origin Spirit, preventing their bodies from suffering any injuries.
But the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise had always disdained those 'Sacred Soul Realm' experts. These Sacred Soul Realm experts had lost the qualification to enter the Calamity Bone Ground. They were all half-finished products.
Even though one could use the Chaotic Origin Spirit to make up for the shortcomings of the fleshy body and even create a realm called the 'Chaotic Origin Realm', this was merely a method of adding gold to one's own flaws.
Even those 'Late Stage Chaotic Origin Experts' could only withstand a force of 10,000 to 20,000 Divine Jun. It was not worth mentioning …
'Impossible, it is impossible for him to have a Chaotic Origin Spirit!'
The upper limit of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was very low. It was impossible for it to withstand a force of 70,000 to 80,000 Divine Jun.
But Luo Zheng could withstand the blood-colored lightning without suffering any injuries. How could this be explained?
"You can try again." Luo Zheng said with a smile.
He could see that the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise was getting impatient. Hence, he deliberately provoked it.
As long as the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise's attack did not exceed 100,000 Divine Jun, it would not cause any substantial damage to him.
Even if the force exceeded 100,000 Godly Jun, his newly cultivated Myriad Tribulation Body could withstand it. Moreover, his Myriad Tribulation Body had nine saint soul imprints, which was far stronger than an ordinary Myriad Tribulation Body.
However, Luo Zheng didn't forget that he was trapped on the other side of the river.
The Blood Spirit Black Tortoise might not be able to do anything to him, but he might not be able to kill it either.
His greatest opportunity was the Celestial Spear in his hand. This spear could amplify its power by three to four times, but it could only stab out once.
There was only one chance. Luo Zheng wanted to be absolutely safe!
The Blood Spirit Black Tortoise had a cautious look in its eyes. It did not believe that Luo Zheng could withstand its lightning without being injured.
This brat must have some kind of special life-saving method, but no matter how miraculous it was, there was still a limit to it. He couldn't block it indefinitely.
"Die!"
"Rumble …"
An even thicker blood-red lightning bolt shot straight at Luo Zheng.
Under the cover of this blood-red lightning, the area around Luo Zheng within a thousand feet radius was like purgatory.
No matter how strong an existence was, it would be hard for him to survive in this region.
But Luo Zheng could …
Above the Heaven Palace.
Gan Gaohan, Ning Xu Yuan, and the others were not much different from the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise. Their eyes were wide open.
"Is this the strength of the Myriad Tribulation Body?" Gan Gaohan asked in astonishment.
Even though Gan Gaohan and the others didn't have the chance to cultivate the Myriad Tribulation Body, they had stayed in the 32 Levels of Heaven for a long time. They were clear of how powerful the Myriad Tribulation Body was.
The Myriad Tribulation Body strengthened the fleshy body. It wasn't like the Spirit of Chaotic Origin which made the fleshy body invincible.
In other words, a Late Stage Chaotic Origin Expert would not be injured at all when the force of 10,000 Divine Catchments landed on him. But when the force of 10,000 Divine Catchments landed on the Myriad Calamity Body, this Myriad Calamity Body might be slightly injured …
"Impossible!" Ning Xu Yuan shook his head with certainty: "It's impossible for the Myriad Tribulation Body to be completely unscathed. It seems like he is relying on the Spirit of Chaotic Origin to absorb the force of 10,000 Jun …"
"Absorbed by the Spirit of Chaotic Origin?"
Gan Gaohan, Qiu Yinhe, and the other Heaven Palace experts were stunned.
"Indeed." Lin Zhanting said: "Luo Zheng seems to have absorbed all the energy from the Spirit of Chaotic Origin …"
"The Blood Spirit Black Tortoise is one of the Ten Great Primal Chaos Ferocious Beasts. A casual strike from it would be at least 70,000 Jun. Are you saying that Luo Zheng's Spirit of Chaotic Origin is that strong?" Gan Gaohan retorted.
Many Sacred Soul Realm experts present had cultivated the Spirit of Chaotic Origin to the limit.
Together with other protective magical treasures, cultivation techniques, and so on, he could at most withstand 10,000 to 20,000 Godly Jun of strength. 25,000 Godly Jun of strength was already the limit.
Hence, the meaning behind Gan Gaohan's question was simple. Even if Luo Zheng was heaven-defying, it was impossible for him to violate this common sense.
Lin Zhanting merely shrugged, "I don't know what the exact reason is. But from his performance, it seems so."
In any case, the strange things that had happened to Luo Zheng were not just one or two.
Hence, it wasn't strange for something weird to happen to Luo Zheng.
"Elder Gan, no matter what, Luo Zheng is still able to resist against the attack of that Blood Spirit Black Tortoise. It is ultimately beneficial for us." Li Bianxue suddenly reminded.
Because that scene was too weird, everyone didn't pay attention to the most crucial issue.
Luo Zheng swayed back and forth amidst those blood-colored lightning bolts. Ultimately, he remained unharmed.
"That's right. Even though the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise can't do anything to Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng can't do anything to it either. I wonder if he can break free from it." Ning Xu Yuan said.
The moment Ning Xu Yuan finished speaking, Luo Zheng was struck by a blood-colored lightning bolt. His entire body bounced back.
"Opportunity?"
The moment Luo Zheng bounced back, he accelerated and charged towards the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise like a meteor.
"Puny human, you dare to counterattack? Scram! "
The Blood Spirit Black Tortoise sneered. It opened its mouth and spat out another blood-colored lightning bolt.
The bucket-sized blood-colored lightning bolt whipped Luo Zheng a few more times and sent him flying back another thousand feet.
After Luo Zheng stabilized himself, his eyes narrowed slightly. He immediately retorted, "You're just a cowardly tortoise. Your strength is only so-so, yet you dare to spout such arrogant words. How laughable!"
In the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise's eyes, Luo Zheng was just a small fry that Genryū wanted to get rid of.
It was infuriated to be mocked by an insignificant little fellow like Luo Zheng. It spat out lightning in an attempt to exhaust Luo Zheng's unknown protective divine ability.
However, its series of attacks were completely ineffective. Instead, it was mocked. How could it tolerate that?
In its anger, it lost all of its patience. It suddenly opened its mouth and revealed a mouthful of fangs. Countless blood-colored lightning bolts coiled around its fangs.
"You really think I can't do anything to you? Watch me devour you! "

Chapter 3372
The last time Li Bianxue entered the Soul City, she had already reached a height of 620 feet.
It was only her twelfth time entering the Soul City, but she was able to achieve such a result. It was already very amazing in the Soul City. At least in the Mother World, there were very few people like Li Bianxue. Therefore, Li Bianxue attracted a lot of attention in the Soul City.
Li Bianxue's speed was not as fast as "Ah Ku". She was very careful and rose at a constant speed. In the time it took for an incense stick to burn, she had only risen a few dozen feet.
After a few incense sticks, Li Bianxue had already risen to a height of 600 feet.
Liu Hen, Qin Hua, and the rest were very concerned about Li Bianxue. At this moment, they were also staring at the Soul Pillar nervously.
"This time, she will probably exceed 700 feet!"
"Maybe Sister Bianxue can really break our Heavenly Palace's record!"
"Look, she has already surpassed the previous record!"
After crossing a height of 620 feet, Li Bianxue continued to climb. Although she had slowed down, she still rose steadily and at a constant speed.
When the Soul Pillar's height reached 700 feet, she finally stopped.
"Whoosh!"
A person's sword shadow quickly descended from the Soul Cloud and landed steadily on the ground.
Just like what Li Bianxue had said to Luo Zheng, she never forced herself in the Soul Cloud. Naturally, she did not land as pathetically as "Ah Ku".
"Reaching a height of 700 feet on the thirteenth time, Sister Bianxue is really our Heavenly Palace's beloved girl!" Liu Hen praised.
Li Bianxue looked at Liu Hen and said indifferently, "You flatter me."
She did not place much importance on these records. Otherwise, with her ability, she would be able to go further.
Just as the few people in the Land of Sword Grief were praising her, an indifferent voice came from not far away. "It's only 700 feet, and the Heavenly Palace is already treating it as a treasure?
Everyone looked towards the source of the voice. A human Sun Soul stood on the Soul Pillar, and the number on the Soul Pillar was 11.
This meant that this person had entered the Soul City for the eleventh time.
"It's Ji Huandao from the You Xiong Clan!"
"He is known as the strongest prodigy in the history of the You Xiong Clan. His talent surpasses Ji Xuanyuan's!"
"It's said that when he entered the Soul City for the first time, he had already climbed to a height of 790 feet...... Now, he has already climbed to a height of 950 feet …"
No matter what race they belonged to, as long as they were geniuses, they would always receive attention.
After Ji Huandao spoke, he immediately attracted the attention of many Yang Souls. Whether it was the Human Race or the other races of the Mother World, they were all talking about him.
"What's so great about that? You haven't even reached a height of one thousand feet. Even if you reach ninety-nine feet, what's the difference between ten feet and ninety-nine feet? If you want to be arrogant, you have to go through Soul City first! "Liu Hen rebuked coldly.
Ji Huandao glanced at Liu Hen indifferently. A person like Liu Hen who had entered the Soul City hundreds of times was naturally not worthy of Ji Huandao's attention.
"A hundred zhang?" Ji Huandao revealed a faint smile, "It's just a piece of cake to form a colorless thought body. What I'm pursuing is not on the same level as what you're pursuing. You don't understand it at all!"
Hearing this, Liu Hen, Qin Hua, and the other Outsiders all revealed looks of confusion.
Those who entered the Soul City were all pursuing the Colorless Thought Body and achieving the Holy Soul Realm. Why was Ji Huan acting so mysterious? What kind of pursuit were they not on the same level?
"Watch carefully, everyone, and the proud daughter of heaven from the Heavenly Palace," Ji Huandao smiled gently, and the soul pillar under his feet began to rise.
The soul pillar rose at an extremely fast speed, and the height displayed on the soul pillar was also rapidly increasing.
Ten feet …
Twenty feet …
Thirty feet …
In less than ten breaths of time, Ji Huandao had risen to a height of more than eighty feet, and was going straight for ninety feet.
"What a fast speed!"
"That soul cloud doesn't seem to have any hindrance to Ji Huandao!"
"It seems that he will be able to pass through Soul City this time …"
Before they finished speaking, the number on the soul pillar under Ji Huandao's feet had already shown the number one hundred.
"Buzz …"
When this number appeared, the entire thick soul cloud suddenly bloomed with a faint light, which meant that Ji Huandao had passed.
"I knew that this kid would be able to pass through in a short period of time!"
"I really envy him. He can safely pass through Soul City and go to the higher shore …"
"Eh? Wait, look … "
Generally speaking, as long as they broke through the height of one hundred feet, most people would breathe a sigh of relief and jump down from the soul pillar. After all, after crossing the finish line, there was not much point in continuing to go up, and it was meaningless to persist at a height of one hundred feet.
But under everyone's gaze, Ji Huandao did not leave, but continued to go up, and the number on the soul pillar continued to increase!
"What does Ji Huandao want to do?"
"Does he want to leave his name in Soul City?"
"No, I heard that there are stronger levels above the Holy Soul Realm, as long as he has the ability to continue to go up …"
On the soul cloud, a light pillar shot into the sky, and Ji Huandao, who was sitting in the soul pillar, rushed out of the soul cloud, and allowed the light pillar to continue to rise.
"Senior Yuanshi Tianzun, isn't the upper limit of Soul City 300 feet?" Luo Zheng stared at the scene and asked.
Yuanshi Tianzun smiled and said, "For most people, it is 300 feet. Crossing this height means that you have achieved the Colorless Thought Body and stepped into the Holy Soul Realm. But do you remember that I told you that the Holy Soul Realm is not the end?"
Luo Zheng nodded. When he first met Yuanshi Tianzun, Yuanshi Tianzun had mentioned it.
"When you become a Colorless Thought Body, everyone has a chance to challenge a higher limit. This opportunity only comes once, and you can't miss it again. But most people choose to give up, because it is very difficult to go up." Yuanshi Tianzun also stared at Ji Huandao, who was above the soul cloud.
Huan Dao said, "This person is also a person with excellent comprehension, so he should be able to get a Holy Soul Engraving."
"Holy Soul Engraving?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
Yuanshi Tianzun nodded, "One should be his limit …"
As the saying went, to go one step further, because one had already climbed one hundred feet, the next step would be very difficult.
This was the current situation.
Although Ji Huandao was extraordinary, it was not easy to move up in the light pillar, and the speed of his ascent was extremely slow.
After half an hour, he had climbed up 80 feet, and the soul pillar showed that he was 800 feet high!
"Buzz!"
A golden engraving circled around Ji Huandao and was immediately imprinted on the surface of Ji Huandao's soul. Then Ji Huandao jumped down from the soul pillar and landed heavily on the ground.
Even though the crowd could not see Ji Huandao's appearance, they could feel his great joy.

Chapter 3373
After escaping from the other shore, Luo Zheng knew that the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise had projected the illusion of his battle onto the Center Divine Province.
After Luo Zheng woke up, Gan Gaohan and the others gathered around, some asking Luo Zheng about the situation, some showing concern for his injuries...
Before, when these experts of the Heavenly Palace faced Luo Zheng, they still had the attitude of a senior looking at a junior, but now when facing Luo Zheng, there was a trace of reverence, the kind of reverence that would only be shown when facing an Immortal expert.
Many disciples of the Sword of Grief had already flown to Greater One Mountain.
For example, Mo Yijian, Chou Xinghe and the others were watching from afar.
The people surrounding Luo Zheng were all core figures of the Heavenly Palace, and the disciples of the Sword of Grief were not qualified to approach them.
"Brother Luo Zheng hasn't left the Fourteenth Heaven for long..." Chou Xinghe sighed.
The corner of Mo Yijian's mouth twitched slightly.
His gaze inadvertently fell on Li Bianxue. After Li Bianxue woke Luo Zheng up from the other shore, she naturally retreated.
Even a genius like Li Bianxue could only step aside at this time.
Her back was facing Mo Yijian, her fine straight hair draped over her thin shoulders, looking a little sparse.
Although Mo Yijian was a later generation, his path was very similar to Li Bianxue's.
If the Heavenly Palace continued to operate stably, after Li Bianxue left the Sword of Grief, Mo Yijian would very likely replace Li Bianxue and become the rising star of the Sword of Grief.
But the light on their bodies was not worth mentioning in front of Luo Zheng, like fireflies competing with the sun and moon. So Mo Yijian could understand Li Bianxue's feelings at this moment.
… …
Southeast of the Dragon Cliff.
The endless mountain forest was shrouded in poisonous miasma.
Ordinary people, and even Nirvana experts, had to take protective measures when they entered.
Naturally, the wild races that lived here were able to blend in with the poisonous miasma. In other words, most of them were poisonous creatures to begin with.
So the people of the Center Divine Province did not migrate to the southeast, and only guarded the Dragon Cliff to prevent the invasion of the savage foreign races.
However, not long ago, a huge monster came here.
Those savage alien races who were known for their ferocity stopped fighting each other and set their sights on this colossus.
The most ferocious of them all was the first to launch an attack on the colossus.
However, the colossal being only needed to stomp a single foot to crush this elite army into dust …
None of the other foreign races had expected that such a powerful being would descend upon this accursed place, and they all scattered in all directions.
During this period of time, they were also secretly observing the movements of the behemoth.
