Earlier in the day, they had seen two threads extending into the sky.
After a period of time, the head of this colossus suddenly stretched out and smashed into the ground with a bang. That rich and long lasting soul aura started to disperse.
He had actually died?
Such a powerful existence had fallen just like that?
The Blood Spirit Clan, who lived on the turtle's back, was even more shocked than the Savage Wasteland outsiders who were secretly observing the situation.
This Blood Spirit Clan had been relying on the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise for generations, and the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise was also their religion.
They had never thought that there would be a day when the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise would fall.
When the soul aura of the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise dissipated, the Blood Spirit Clan members were initially filled with disbelief. Following that, they started crying and howling …
But just as they were grieving, those savage foreign races hiding in the forest started moving.
The Blood Spirit Black Tortoise was such a powerful existence, and its entire body was filled with treasures.
Now that the aura of the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise had disappeared, it was clear that its soul had been extinguished. How could those savage foreign races miss this opportunity?
Within the poisonous miasma, countless creatures of other races swarmed in from all directions. The Blood Spirit Clan living on the tortoise's back had no other choice but to fight a bloody battle. Although their clan was powerful, in the end, they couldn't withstand the tide of other races from the Desolate Wilderness …
…
This time, the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise suddenly acted against Luo Zheng, causing the entire Great Yi Heaven Palace to feel nervous.
At the same time, some new suspicions arose.
Now it seemed that the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit that could allow the Paramita lifeforms to descend was indeed very important to Genryū.
Given Genryū's demand, it was impossible for them to give up on the chance to descend.
They could easily send experts from the Dihong Clan and Lie Shan Clan to act. If these Sempiternal realm experts worked together, the Heaven Palace would not be able to resist …
But now, they merely sent the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise over. Furthermore, the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise was using the Jade Dragon Silk to take away Luo Zheng's life.
From all these, it seemed that the Lie Shan Clan and Dihong Clan did not have much resources they could use. Or rather, they had more important things to do!
Of course, these were all guesses of Gan Gaohan and the others.
Gan Gaohan and the others were also within the 32 Levels of Heaven. They were relaying the matter of the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise to the Sacred Soul experts of Li Mountain. These Sacred Soul experts would then relay the information to Nuwa and Fuxi …
Other than collecting information, Gan Gaohan and the others personally went to the star ring of the 32 Levels of Peace to welcome Luo Zheng.
After clearing out the obstacles in the bone cave, Luo Zheng returned to Fandu Heaven.
The Li Yuan clansmen naturally wouldn't wait for Luo Zheng. Once the obstacles in the passage were removed, they carefully crossed the Jade Dragon Silk and left for the top of the bone cave.
Li Bianxue followed closely behind Luo Zheng and carefully took out the spear. "Here, take this!"
Li Bianxue had already heard from the Heaven Palace that Luo Zheng had been able to kill the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise mainly because of this Celestial Spear.
Amplifying his strength by four times meant that he had the strength of five to six hundred thousand jun.
To Li Bianxue, this was an astronomical number!
This meant that Luo Zheng himself had to release a strength of over a hundred thousand jun. What was his body made of to withstand such terrifying power?
"We'll be flying through the star ring in a bit. Help me put it away first," Luo Zheng said.
Li Bianxue nodded obediently and put the Celestial Spear back into Sumeru Space.
Before she entered Fandu Heaven, she had been repeatedly told to help Luo Zheng as much as possible.
In the depths of her heart, Li Bianxue felt that it was unfair.
After all, she was once the pride of the Heaven Palace, and after passing through Soul City, she became a Sacred Soul expert. But now, she had completely become Luo Zheng's follower, and had to serve him first.
But on second thought, if it wasn't for Luo Zheng, she probably wouldn't even have been able to pass through Yu Sheng-tian, let alone Fandu Heaven.
After climbing to the top of the bone cave, she arrived at the exit.

Chapter 3374
In the Soul Cloud, Luo Zheng was still immersed in confusion.
At this time, even the Heavenly Lord of the Origin began to feel confused.
In fact, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin did plan to help Luo Zheng pass the Soul City.
And not only did he want to help Luo Zheng pass the Soul City, but he also wanted to help Luo Zheng get the nine Holy Soul Engravings!
Even though more than a thousand chaos epochs had passed, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin still remembered clearly that when he got the seven Holy Soul Engravings, he had vaguely seen something at the top of the light pillar. It seemed that there was something else.
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin didn't know what that thing was, but he speculated that it might be related to the original "Instant" civilization.
Any news about the Instant civilization could arouse the Heavenly Lord of the Origin's great interest.
But how difficult was it to get the nine Holy Soul Engravings?
For so many divine epochs, the highest record was only eight Holy Soul Engravings!
However, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin thought that since it was the first time Luo Zheng entered the Soul Cloud, he should let him have a process of adaptation. When he came out, he would teach him the experience.
But after Luo Zheng entered the Soul Cloud, he didn't move at all. Even the Heavenly Lord of the Origin was confused.
"Luo Zheng, what's wrong with you?" the Heavenly Lord of the Origin asked.
"These things in the Soul Cloud seem to have no effect on me. I seem to be unable to drive the Soul Pillar to rise," Luo Zheng said.
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin's pupils shrank slightly. "Impossible …"
Forming the Colorless Thought Body was a process of repeatedly replacing the Sun Soul.
"Colorless" was the world of matter. A person's soul was born with seven emotions and six desires. Through the swallowing of the "faces" in the Soul Cloud, the seven emotions and six desires would be constantly replaced.
In this process, Luo Zheng would constantly forget and constantly gain.
When completely forgotten, the Sun Soul would be reborn and become a real Colorless Thought Body. At this time, the Sun Soul would become a Holy Soul and naturally undergo a rebirth.
In the eyes of ordinary people, the Soul Cloud seemed to be a chaotic mass. In fact, it was layered, and each layer had a different range of replacement for the Sun Soul.
No matter how low Luo Zheng's talent was, it was impossible for this to happen.
"Could it be that you are repelled by Colorless Thoughts …" Heavenly Primogenitor's voice became cautious.
If that was the case, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to pass through Soul City, let alone the people in the scroll.
"Repel …"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hands towards those "faces", but those faces were still madly biting at Luo Zheng.
The rhythmic singing continued to reverberate through the entire soul cloud.
He still couldn't understand the meaning behind it, but there was a surging feeling in the depths of his soul.
"They … they want to bestow it on me?" Luo Zheng suddenly blurted out.
Luo Zheng saw the soul cloud as a whole, but the "faces" in the cloud were individual individuals. But no matter what, this feeling emerged in his heart with incomparable accuracy.
"Buzz …"
As the faces continued to bite, the purple light within Luo Zheng's Yang Soul was rapidly stripped away. It was as if his soul was being eaten and replaced by a pure, transparent soul!
When Heavenly Primogenitor noticed this scene, he was also stunned. "Are … are they all being replaced now?"
The entire soul cloud was surging. Even Ah Ku, Li Bianxue, Ji Huandao, and the others below could clearly see that all the faces were converging in one direction, and the place where they were converging was where Luo Zheng's Soul Pillar was!
Many Sun Souls entered the soul cloud at the same time as Luo Zheng. The speed at which these Sun Souls rose was either fast or slow, but when those "faces" rushed towards Luo Zheng, there were no more faces to help them replace the Sun Souls. Their Soul Pillars also stopped, and they were all dumbfounded.
"What happened?"
"All the ghost faces are converging at Luo Zheng's place?"
"I've never heard of such a situation …"
Ji Huandao stared at this strange scene, his face full of vigilance.
The 29th Heaven could be regarded as the starting point of another level. The experts on this level would probably face direct competition in the future. Such an abnormal scene was definitely not what Ji Huandao wanted to see.
"It seems a little different," Feng Ge said with a smile. Although Feng Ge did not know what had happened, she had great confidence in Luo Zheng.
After those faces gathered for a while, they dispersed. Luo Zheng's Sun Soul had turned pure and transparent.
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng looked at his hands in confusion. He was really afraid that he would disappear.
"Colorless Thought Body, you have already formed it," Heavenly Primogenitor said briefly.
"Don't I have to climb up?" Luo Zheng asked.
"It seems that there's no need. Those 'soul faces' came down from above, haha …" Heavenly Primogenitor was also very speechless. These faces kept in the soul cloud did like to devour the seven emotions and six desires, but no one had ever received the same treatment as Luo Zheng.
"But if I form the Colorless Thought Body like this, I can't get the Holy Soul Engraving, right?" Luo Zheng asked again.
At the mention of the Holy Soul Engraving, Heavenly Primogenitor was also shocked.
After becoming a Colorless Thought Body, there was only one chance. If it was missed, it would not come again. Heavenly Primogenitor did not expect that Luo Zheng would step into the Holy Soul Realm in this way. For a moment, his mind was blank.
Soon, Heavenly Primogenitor said, "Don't leave the soul cloud, rush up!"
This was completely out of options, because Luo Zheng was not sure whether he could drive the soul pillar. At least, the soul pillar did not respond when he drove it like that just now.
After hearing Heavenly Primogenitor's words, Luo Zheng changed his mind and drove the soul pillar up again.
This time, the soul pillar finally moved.
"Phew …"
In the eyes of everyone, those "faces" swarmed toward Luo Zheng and left like a swarm of bees. They were completely confused.
But then the soul pillar began to rise at a rapid speed.
100 feet …
300 feet …
900 feet …
1000 feet …
"Buzz!"
As the light pillar at the top of the soul cloud rose, Luo Zheng's figure appeared in it.
He stepped on the soul pillar and was still climbing up!
When he crossed the distance of 800 feet, a golden Holy Soul Engraving had hit Luo Zheng's chest.
But just as the golden Holy Soul Engraving hit him, Luo Zheng's figure continued to climb up.
1,160 feet, the second Holy Soul Engraving!
1,240 feet …
100 feet …
Holy Soul Engraving hit the surface of Luo Zheng's body one after another.
Below the soul cloud, Feng Ge reached out to cover his small cherry mouth. He was so surprised that he could not say a word.
Not only Feng Ge, but also the others. It was just that the others could not see the expression on their faces.
Especially Ji Huandao. Every time a Holy Soul Engraving fell on the surface of Luo Zheng's body, Ji Huandao's heart trembled.

Chapter 3375
Completing the colorless Thought Body was a difficult hurdle for a Sun Soul that had reached the 28th Heaven.
But with time, there was still a possibility of crossing it.
But if he wanted to obtain the Saint Soul Engraving, he would have to go through a lot of trouble.
Every moment in the light pillar was like lying in the magma of hell.
Ji Huandao had already risked his life to be able to hold on until the first engraving appeared. But Luo Zheng obtained these engraving so easily and naturally. How could he feel balanced?
Ji Huandao was not the only one who felt unbalanced in his heart. None of the people present could keep calm about it.
For example, Liu Hen had instigated Luo Zheng to suffer. Who would have thought that Luo Zheng had crossed the soul cloud in an instant and entered the light pillar above.
"Blazing Seal!"
"All Living Seal!"
"Lifeless Seal!"
"Cluster Ghost Seal!"
"Group Dao Seal!"
"No Three Seal!"
"Earth Seal!"
"Heaven Returning Seal!"
The first eight Saint Soul Engraving hit the surface of Luo Zheng's Saint Soul one by one.
One on his chest, one on his dantian, two on his shoulders, one on his forehead, and two on his waist.
When Luo Zheng obtained the eight Saint Soul Engraving, the rapidly rising soul pillar suddenly stopped.
Luo Zheng still had a puzzled look on his face. He asked, "What's going on …"
"I'm not sure either." Yuanshi Tianzun shook his head.
With Yuanshi Tianzun's understanding of the Nirvana Realm, not many people knew what he did not know.
However, the problem that Luo Zheng was facing now had already exceeded the scope of the Heavenly Lord of Primordial Beginning's understanding.
During this process, Yuanshi Tianzun was also constantly trying to figure out what the problem was.
Yuanshi Tianzun also knew Luo Zheng's origin very well. After all, the Six-striped Blood Tree was also created by him. The only variable in Luo Zheng's growth was probably his special physical body and the Chaos Secret Skill. But these two should not have much to do with Soul City.
Right …
Now that Yuanshi Tianzun also did not know what the problem was, Luo Zheng was too lazy to think about it.
Since the Soul Pillar wasn't going up or down, he observed his surroundings.
Standing at this height, he could already see the entire Soul City.
The interior of the Soul City was actually a circular area, and the Soul Pillars were evenly distributed in this circular area. Further away from the Soul Pillars was a grayish-brown building formed by souls.
The buildings and houses in Soul City could not be entered. There had once been a Sun Soul who had tried, but the Sun Soul that had entered never came out. It was said that it had also become a part of Soul City. Since then, no one dared to explore the houses in Soul City.
Everyone had come and gone in a hurry just to reach the Saint Soul Realm.
However, the room at the top of Soul City still attracted the attention of many Sun Souls.
The room at the top was a small round house with a pointed roof. There was a small window beside the house. Every time the door of Soul City opened, the small window would light up with a pale yellow candle flame.
Because of the candlelight that lit up on time, many people concluded that there must be living creatures in the small house.
But no matter what, no one had entered the small house.
Even the Meta-Spirit Clan, who had dominated the Paramita Realm for the longest, had never set foot in the small house!
In fact, everyone was so curious because the Meta-Spirit Clan had tried to enter the house in the second and third chaos eras.
In order to take a peek at what was inside the small house with a pointed roof, the Yuanling Clan had once invited 'Gui Chentou' from the thirty-three layers of Sanqing Heaven to descend. Gui Chentou was the Nirvana Realm creature with the strongest soul invited by the Yuanling Clan.
But even Gui Suntou had completely disappeared after entering the house …
At that time, all the Sun Souls in Soul City had been devoured by the soul cloud.
From then on, Genryū had never had any ideas about the small house with a pointed roof again.
Luo Zheng's Soul Pillar stayed there for the time it took to burn a stick of incense. The light within the pillar became denser, and all the golden light seemed to condense into a solid body.
Looking from afar, the pillar of light continued to condense, almost turning into a solid golden pillar that tightly wrapped Luo Zheng within.
Originally, the entire Soul City and the faces in the soul cloud were constantly making all kinds of sounds.
But after the bronze pillar appeared, the entire Soul City quieted down.
The singing that had been constantly ringing out also stopped.
The strange scene and the quiet atmosphere caused many people to become vigilant.
The Sun Souls that were still in the soul cloud immediately chose to give up and jump down from the Soul Pillars. Then, they stared dumbfoundedly at the scene above.
"Crack …"
The Soul Pillar beneath Luo Zheng's feet trembled and slowly began to rise again.
Luo Zheng, who had been feeling nothing all this time, finally began to feel a lot of pain at this moment.
After the golden pillar formed, it had already completely wrapped Luo Zheng within.
He couldn't move or see anything. He had already become a part of the golden pillar.
"Don't ask. Just wait and see!"
Luo Zheng was just about to ask the Primeval Lord of Heaven, but the Primeval Lord of Heaven had already flatly refused.
Although the Primeval Lord of Heaven didn't know what this was, he was extremely excited. He realized that Luo Zheng had a chance to reach the top. Although all of this had happened very suddenly and was difficult to understand, it was indeed proceeding in the direction he had thought …
Not long after, a number appeared on Luo Zheng's Soul Pillar: one hundred and seventy-two zhang.
At this time, the solid golden pillar that wrapped Luo Zheng suddenly shattered. The substance on the golden pillar continuously shattered and peeled off, falling into the soul cloud from above.
When the "faces" saw the countless fragments falling, they swarmed over like a school of fish in a pond, desperately fighting over the fragments.
After the golden pillar continuously peeled off, Luo Zheng's Sun Soul form appeared, and the ninth Saint Soul Seal had already landed on top of his head.
"Leading Seal!"
Luo Zheng stood on the surface of the Soul Pillar. His back was straight, and he was actually breathing shallowly!
The nine Saint Soul Seals inside his soul actually outlined golden veins, and the location of these veins was actually exactly the same as the distribution of the Eight Extraordinary Meridians of a human.
As Luo Zheng exhaled, the golden veins turned from bright to dark, and then from dark to bright.
Although after reaching the Saint Soul Realm, the soul was strong enough to contend with the physical body, it still couldn't escape the scope of the soul.
But at this moment, Luo Zheng had really undergone a qualitative change.
The nine Saint Soul Seals formed a large circle of their own, turning his soul into a different kind of life!
"What is this?"
In front of Luo Zheng floated a small bronze ring.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven had seen this bronze ring when he obtained the seventh Saint Soul Seal.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently pulled on the bronze ring. The entire Soul City suddenly trembled.
A huge gear on the right side of the Soul City slowly rotated, and the chains formed by souls were constantly loosened.

Chapter 3376
Candlelight was still on the window of the small house with a pointy roof.
He could not see what was inside the window with his eyes alone.
As the iron chain bridge was lowered from the opposite side of the Soul City, he finally had the chance to explore this secret after thousands of chaos eras.
"Go, child," said the Heavenly Lord of Origin. "Perhaps you have a chance to face them directly."
Luo Zheng looked at the iron chain bridge in front of him with a hint of hesitation on his face.
Frankly speaking, his purpose of exploring the Soul City was to achieve the Divine Soul Realm. Now that he had achieved his goal, he could leave directly.
However, from another perspective, he had inherited the scroll of the human race, which meant he had become the hope of the human race.
He had no right to give up on such an exploration.
"Who are they?" Luo Zheng asked.
"The civilization that built the Soul City," replied the Heavenly Lord of Origin.
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. "Is it that civilization that doesn't exist?"
"Doesn't exist? Who told you that? "The Heavenly Lord of Origin was slightly stunned.
"Goddess Nuwa, uh, Goddess Nuwa's avatar," replied Luo Zheng.
The Heavenly Lord of Origin smiled faintly. "Nuwa thought herself clever. The Soul City, the Land of Calamity Bones, and the Immortal Scroll are proof of the existence of the Instant Civilization. No matter how powerful Genryū is, it is impossible for him to create these things. With their tolerance, it is too late for them to destroy them!"
If the Soul City and the Land of Calamity Bones were completely destroyed, no one would be able to reach the Immortal Realm. As the forerunner, Genryū would be able to put an end to the pursuers once and for all. They were obviously very happy to do so.
It was a pity that the Yuanling Clan had no way to destroy the Soul City and the land of Calamity Bones. They had paid a hefty price for several experiments, and could only allow the Nirvana Realm creatures and Sun Souls to enter and leave the Soul City …
"That … that house should have the Instant Civilization?" Luo Zheng's interest was slowly being piqued.
"We have to go in to see for ourselves," replied the Heavenly Lord of Origin.
Luo Zheng nodded and took a step forward.
With this step, he stepped onto the iron chain bridge.
Because his Yang Soul had been changed, his soul's sense of touch had also undergone an earth-shattering change. This new colorless Thought Body was like a cub that had just broken out of its shell. Any object it touched felt new and soft.
Just as Luo Zheng stepped onto the Iron Chain Bridge, something suddenly rushed in from the entrance of Soul City.
The object was sparkling and translucent, as if it was formed from ice crystals. It seemed to have a physical form, but it also seemed to be able to take on a myriad of forms. At this moment, the object had already transformed into a pair of wings and was flying towards the soul cloud.
"Sou!"
Its speed was astonishingly fast. From the moment Luo Zheng saw it to the moment it rushed onto the iron chain bridge, only a split second had passed!
After this thing rushed onto the iron chain bridge, it kept moving forward along the bridge, heading straight for the building on the other side of the bridge. But this time, just as it rushed out for a certain distance, little dots of orange light appeared on the iron chain bridge.
After these dots of orange light appeared, they stuck to the surface of its body one by one. Each dot of orange light seemed to be a hand, pulling it forcefully.
This thing kept struggling and moving forward, but it couldn't move a single step on the iron chain bridge.
After a short while, this thing turned into a pool of blue liquid and sprinkled on the iron chain bridge …
"What is this?" Luo Zheng looked at the pool of blue liquid and asked.
The Heavenly Lord of Origin sneered, "It's Ning Bingnu."
"Genryū's?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Yes, it's even stronger than the Moon Fire Slaves." The Heavenly Lord of Origin nodded.
Over the years, the Heavenly Lord of Origin had killed the most Moon Fire Slaves, followed by Ning Bingnu.
However, Ning Bingnu's body was ever-changing, and its strength was much stronger than the Moon Fire Slaves.
Soul City was such an important place, so the Genryū would naturally monitor it closely. After Luo Zheng caused the anomaly, it quickly attracted the Genryū's attention.
Now that the iron chain bridge had been lowered, the Genryū naturally wanted to fight with all their might. They also wanted to know what was hidden in the small house with a pointed roof.
But this fight failed in the end …
Luo Zheng stepped forward lightly. After he took a few steps forward, the orange dots of light appeared on the iron chain bridge again.
These dots of light were not in a regular shape. Some were circular, some were diamond-shaped, and they looked like floating orange petals.
Seeing these dots of light floating towards him, Luo Zheng's face showed a cautious expression.
Ning Bingnu had just died at the hands of these orange dots of light. Naturally, he didn't want to repeat the same mistake.
But judging from the density of these orange dots of light, Luo Zheng couldn't safely pass through them.
"Clack …"
Just as Luo Zheng was hesitating, an orange petal floated onto his arm. Luo Zheng quickly retracted his arm, but the petal had already stuck to his arm and was drilling deeper into his arm.
Strangely, the orange petal didn't cause any harm to Luo Zheng. After it entered his arm and touched the golden meridians, the orange petal automatically merged with them and became a part of Luo Zheng's Yang Soul …
At the same time, Luo Zheng felt a trace of warmth spreading from his arm.
"This thing won't hurt me?" Luo Zheng asked in a daze.
"Of course," the Heavenly Lord of Origin said with a smile. "After all, you opened this iron chain bridge with nine Soul Seals. How could the Instant civilization make such a mistake?"
In the eyes of the Heavenly Lord of Origin, the Instant civilization was the chief designer of this world.
Time, space, truth, Shinto, and any other laws were designed by the Instant civilization. It was a magical race that basically didn't make mistakes.
Luo Zheng nodded. Only then did he feel at ease and move forward on the iron chain bridge.
Those orange petals not only didn't harm Luo Zheng, but also nourished his soul. When Luo Zheng passed by, he touched them intentionally or unintentionally.
The iron chain bridge was only two to three hundred feet long. In a short while, Luo Zheng had reached the end of the iron chain bridge and stepped on the stairs on the side of Soul City.
The buildings of Soul City were built very strangely. The entrance was very narrow. If the creatures of Instant had a body, they should be very slim.
But after climbing up from the entrance, the stairs suddenly became wide.
The carved reliefs on both sides of the stairs were not strange beasts or scenery, but some very abstract things, such as triangles, squares, circles, and so on.
Around these shapes, there seemed to be rich markings. They were fist-sized Sanskrit characters, which seemed to explain the relationship between these shapes.
Luo Zheng looked at them. He couldn't understand Sanskrit, but the Heavenly Lord of Origin said, "Luo Zheng, wait …"

Chapter 3377
The bronze ground in Pingyi Tian was very vast.
Gan Gaohan led Luo Zheng and Li Bianxue to the temple of the Heavenly Palace. They had only seen a corner of Pingyi Tian.
After returning to the bronze ground, they saw a vast ocean engraved on the bronze ground after walking for a short distance. There were various small islands in the ocean, and they could also see some huge creatures swimming in it.
"This is the Sea of Tianyi. It's very big."
Perhaps Gan Gaohan was worried about Lin Zhanting and the others, so he only briefly introduced them to Luo Zheng.
Could such a big area like the Sea of Tianyi be divided into pieces like the temple of the Heavenly Palace?
Just when Luo Zheng had some doubts, the white light beside him suddenly flashed.
Murlocs with frog feet crawled out from the bronze ground. These murlocs curiously sized up Luo Zheng and the others. They stretched out their hands and gently clapped, releasing a white whirlpool. Then, they jumped into the whirlpool and jumped into the ocean …
"It's really divided …" Luo Zheng's mouth slightly curved.
These murlocs had reached the end of the ocean. If they wanted to continue forward, they could only crawl out and use light stones to cross to the other side.
On the way north, they encountered many Nirvana Realm creatures.
It was impossible to fight in the space of the bronze ground. These Nirvana Realm creatures were quite friendly. Some of them even greeted Luo Zheng and the others as if they had met an old acquaintance.
Such etiquette surprised Luo Zheng.
After all, the Thirty-two Levels of Heaven should be the highest level below the Immortal Realm. The competition in this level of heaven should be very fierce. However, most of the Nirvana Realm creatures seemed to be getting along well after being separated by the immortal picture scroll.
Just when Luo Zheng felt that the atmosphere was harmonious, two Nirvana Realm creatures appeared not far away. One was like a bear with a huge body, and the other was like a bull with a human body. These two burly Nirvana Realm creatures were cursing at each other.
On the bronze ground, no one could do anything to the other. They could only provoke each other with words and at the same time, ask each other to enter their own picture scroll space.
However, these Nirvana Realm creatures had been operating in their own territory for a long time. There were many magic circles and mechanisms inside. Without enough strength, it was basically a dead end to enter the other party's temple …
"It's not good for them to keep arguing like this, right? Is there no way to resolve conflicts in the 32 Heavens? "
After passing by those Nirvana Realm races, Luo Zheng finally couldn't help but ask.
"Yes," Ning Xuyuan said with some disdain. "In fact, there is a public area where they can fight. They can even kill each other. However, the Voodoo Bear Tribe and the Human Ox Tribe are timid. They have been fighting like this for a long time. You will get used to it."
After traveling for another five hundred miles to the north, the relief carvings on the ground began to reveal stone-like patterns. Most of these stones were slender and long, looking like thick trees that were growing into the sky.
"We've arrived at the Stone Forest!" Gan Gaohan stopped and looked around.
Whether it was the light stone, the heavy stone, or the scroll sealing stone, they were all found in the stone forest.
The Stone Forest could be seen as a giant mine that all races could enter. Many creatures of different races would search the Stone Forest all year round.
Light stones were very common in the Stone Forest, so they were the most valuable. Heavy stones were much rarer.
However, there weren't many who had the strength to enter the Immortal Picture Scroll. Those Nirvana Realm creatures that were born in the 32 Heavens didn't cultivate the Saint Soul Realm or the Body of Eternity. If they wanted to enter the first scroll of the Immortal Picture Scroll, they still had to return to the 29 Heavens and 31 Heavens.
Therefore, although heavy stones were rare, the demand wasn't high and it wasn't difficult to obtain.
The most difficult thing to obtain was the "scroll sealing stone". As the name suggested, the scroll sealing stone could completely seal an area for a period of time. During the sealing process, creatures inside and outside the area couldn't enter or leave.
This way, they could protect themselves and also prevent others from leaving.
However, the scroll sealing stone was too rare. Donghuang had been operating for so long and had sent many Saint Souls to search the Stone Forest, but he had only obtained two scroll sealing stones.
In fact, this time when the Tai Yi Heaven Palace fought against the You Xiong Clan and the Divine Farmer Clan, they should have known that Genryū wouldn't let it go.
At such a sensitive time, they had to be careful when searching for heavy stones.
If they had used the scroll sealing stone, they wouldn't have been trapped by those ferocious creatures.
When Gan Gaohan was still trying to determine which area Lin Zhanting was trapped in, Ning Xuyuan suddenly patted his shoulder and said, "Brother Gan, to the left!"
Everyone looked to the left and saw a person standing not far away. It was a powerhouse of the Saint Soul Stage. On the surface of his Saint Soul, there were traces of green flames moving around. It seemed that he was similar to the people of Jinwu, as they both had a fire-type bloodline.
Behind this powerhouse of the Saint Soul Stage, there was a large group of ferocious Nirvana Realm creatures.
"Brother Gan, long time no see." The powerhouse of the Saint Soul Stage sneered. His voice was dragged out, and there was a hint of mockery in it.
"Fire Envoy," Gan Gaohan stared coldly at the Saint Soul and said, "You came to the Stone Forest with such fanfare. Are you here to dig for light stones?"
"Yes or no, brother Gan can't control that, right?" The Fire Envoy laughed and said, "However, I'm afraid that some fellows who are digging for heavy stones will never come out again, heh heh heh …"
As he spoke, the Fire Envoy pointed at the ground in front of him.
Inside the Stone Forest on the ground, one could see that there were 11 people retreating to a corner. These 11 people were naturally Lin Zhanting and his group, who were helping Luo Zheng to find heavy stones. On the screen, Lin Zhanting and his group were guarding a corner, and in front of them, there were many huge ghosts floating.
Cicadas.
Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and the others all looked over. When they saw that Lin Zhanting was still guarding, their worries were slightly relieved. At least they were still alive …
However, at this moment, the layer of white light in the distance swept over again.
When the white light swept across the bronze ground, the reliefs on the ground were refreshed again!
In the Stone Forest, Lin Zhanting and his group retreated again. Now, there were only six people left …
In one hour, five people had already died!
Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan's faces twitched violently.
In the battle between the Heavenly Palace and the Golden Crow clan, they had lost quite a few Saint Soul experts. All the mountain lords of the seven mountains had died, so every single Saint Soul expert that died was an unacceptable loss to the Heavenly Palace.
"Splitting Crane Race, listen to my orders! Ice Spirit Race, listen to my orders! Green Spirit Race, listen to my orders! Just kill all those Saint Souls! "

Chapter 3378
The Nirvana Realm creatures brought by Huo Xing weren't in the Immortal Realm, not even the Body of Eternity.
However, as creatures from Pingyi Heaven, they weren't weak. At least, they could put a lot of pressure on the Holy Soul Realm.

"Brother Gan of the Heavenly Palace, did this person really only use three years?"
"This speed is comparable to a miracle …"
"It seems that an extraordinary figure has appeared in the Heavenly Palace this time!"
The other Three-eyed Tribesmen behind Clan Leader Jiutian said.
Although the Three-eyed Tribesmen couldn't be compared to monsters like Yuan Ling and Wu Kong, their status in the 32 Layered Heavens was much higher than the Heavenly Palace, and their strength was also much stronger.
The Heavenly Palace was able to safely exist in the 32 Layered Heavens because of the protection mechanism in Ping Yi Heaven. When the next Chaos Era came, just the Eastern Sovereign and Old Ying, two Immortal Realm experts, wouldn't be able to protect the Heavenly Palace's temple.
The experts of the Three-eyed Tribe didn't expect that such an extraordinary junior would appear in such a weak Heavenly Palace.
This kid's growth speed was much faster than the Eastern Sovereign's, right?
While the Three-eyed Tribesmen were still in shock, Fei Yu suddenly said, "Grandfather, it wasn't three years. I only met Luo Zheng two years after entering the 13th Layer of Heaven, and I was injured about a year ago."
The Three-eyed Tribesmen suddenly fell silent. They couldn't help but look at Luo Zheng again.
One year …
Where did this monster come from?
Although the Three-eyed Tribesmen maintained their silence, Gan Gaohan and the others were overjoyed.
One must know that the Heavenly Palace knew Luo Zheng very well. Luo Zheng's terrifying growth speed wasn't the only thing that was terrifying.
"It seems that Tai Yi Heavenly Palace has poured a lot of effort and resources into you, right?" Jiutian Clan Leader smiled.
Gan Gaohan wanted to deny it. After all, those resources were fought for by Luo Zheng himself, but Luo Zheng nodded and said, "Yes, I was able to reach Ping Yi Heaven so quickly because of the resources and support of the Heavenly Palace."
If it was to celebrate the strength of the entire tribe and give Luo Zheng a large amount of soul realm and Qi Dipper Jade, it was naturally possible.
After Luo Zheng was so frank, many experts of the Three-eyed Tribe also revealed an expression of "as expected". Such an explanation at least made them feel better.
"You disciples of the Heavenly Palace don't have a physical body, only a soul. It's not easy for someone to enter the Nirvana Realm with a physical body, so of course we'll do our best," Jiutian Clan Leader said with a smile. "My grandson obtained four Saint Soul Engravings a long time ago and five Tribulation Bone Engravings.
The person with the most Saint Soul Engravings will get a good ranking in the Immortal Painting Scroll. "
He changed the topic to his own family and forgot to ask how many Saint Soul Engravings Luo Zheng had obtained.
Fei Yu's child-like face was full of confidence. "Grandfather, this time I won't let you down."
Jiutian Clan Leader nodded. "You owe this little brother from Tai Yi Heavenly Palace a favor. After he enters the painting scroll, Fei Yu, you have to guide him."
"Of course!" Fei Yu smiled and nodded at Luo Zheng.
"Then thank you, Jiutian Clan Leader." Gan Gaohan calmly cupped his hands and thanked him.
Jiutian Clan Leader looked at Gan Gaohan strangely.
If it was in the past, the people of Tai Yi Heavenly Palace would be overjoyed to be taken care of by the Three-eyed Tribe.
But although Gan Gaohan expressed his gratitude, his tone was calm, as if he didn't need his grandson's help?
He must be thinking too much …
How could he know that although he was an extremely talented person with four Saint Soul Engravings and five Tribulation Bone Engravings, he was nothing compared to Luo Zheng. They weren't on the same level at all.
Next, Fei Yu and the other two members of the Three-eyed Tribe each found a piece of Sanqing Stone. After releasing light stones, they hung their Yang Soul and physical body inside.
For the creatures in the Nirvana Realm, their souls and physical bodies were one.
When they came out, two "Anchoring Bodies" appeared on both sides of each Sanqing Stone. One was naturally the physical body, and the other was the soul. If they could reach the Immortal Realm, a third "Anchoring Body" would appear.

Chapter 3383
After the Nirvana Realm races of the 32 Levels of Heaven showed themselves, Luo Zheng realized that there were so many races here.

They were also the most solid of the human race.
An ally. "The Primeval Lord of Heaven's thoughts drifted back to the past.

After the failure of the Human Race,

Thousands of chaos epochs. Too many things had happened in the long river of time.

Before the Purple Cloud Race, several Nirvana Realm experts had already hung their souls and bodies inside, including Luo Zheng, Li Bianxue, and a few from the Three-Eyed Race.

The Purple Cloud Race's Immortal Realm expert smiled sinisterly with his hands behind his back. "It doesn't matter which Three-Clarities Stone is used to enter the Immortal Painting. However, the Purple Cloud Race's Immortal Realm experts are anchored around this area. Outsiders can't enter!"
The Three-Clarities Stones on the ground and the "sculptures" around them were indeed slender and long bodies. They were undoubtedly from the Purple Cloud Race.
Even so, the Purple Cloud Race's actions were still unreasonable.
Even large clans like the Yuan Ling Clan and the Wu Kong Clan would casually choose a Three Clarities Stone. However, the Purple Cloud Clan was overbearing. Others had already hung their souls and bodies on it, but they actually wanted others to take it down.

The Three-Eyed Boy Fei Yu and the other members of the Three-Eyed Race were also furious.

The Three-Eyed Race had the ability to protect themselves in Flat Sky. However, if the Purple Cloud Race targeted them, the Three-Eyed Race's days would be very difficult.
Hearing the Purple Cloud Race's blatant threat, the Three-Eyed Race's members were filled with grief and indignation. However, Fei Yu was very magnanimous. Instead, he persuaded, "Grandfather, it's fine if we withdraw. Just exchange it for a Three-Clarities Stone. We won't lose a piece of flesh!"
Under Fei Yu's persuasion, Clan Leader Jiutian had no choice but to agree. Fei Yu and the other two members of the Three-Eyed Race returned to the space inside the second half of the painting and withdrew their bodies.
The Three-Eyed Race chose to withdraw. The other small races couldn't afford to offend the Purple Cloud Race, so they also withdrew obediently. In the end, only Luo Zheng and Li Bianxue of the Human Race were left. One of them had his soul and the other had his body.

Gan Gaohan and Ning Xuyuan were still thinking of a solution. Donghuang wasn't here, and they didn't want to offend these Nirvana races.

The Purple Cloud Race's members didn't think that the weak Human Race would refuse. Especially the Human Race of this Round of Chaos. They couldn't even enter the Nirvana Realm with their bodies. They could only hang their souls there to gain some benefits. They were insignificant characters.

Chapter 3384

Once?
Had anyone passed through Soul City in one go?
It seemed that it wasn't just this round of chaos. It had never existed before …

Among the many old ministers of Donghuang Taiyi, only Ning Xuyuan had one Saint Soul Seal. Because of this Saint Soul Seal, his soul was much stronger than other Saint Soul Realm experts!
Luo Zheng had nine Saint Soul Seals. How strong would he be?

Lady Nuwa, who had been calm, also lost her composure. Then she said, "Zheng 'er, try releasing your soul pressure …"
She believed Luo Zheng, but she still wanted to see how strong the soul of a person with nine Saint Soul Seals was!
Luo Zheng had also just left the Paramita Realm. He felt an unprecedented sense of energy, but he didn't know how strong his Sun Soul was.
"Okay!"
Luo Zheng closed his eyes slightly and took a deep breath.
"Phew!"

The nine Saint Soul Seals connected into a whole, constantly flowing inside his soul.
The soul aura quickly formed a terrifying pressure, filling the entire Lineage Dragon Palace!
Apart from Fuxi and Nuwa, all the experts of the Celestial Palace who were present felt the oppressive aura and found it hard to breathe.

He had just thought that putting aside strength, Luo Zheng's cultivation was now on the same level as his. Now it seemed that he had thought too simply …
Outside the Lineage Dragon Palace, there were newly deployed guards, and their cultivation wasn't low.

This soul pressure only lasted for a few breaths of time. Luo Zheng closed his eyes slightly and withdrew all his aura. When he opened his eyes again, they had returned to normal.

Fuxi couldn't accept this reality!

Chapter 3385

Six of them landed in the hands of Shennong, three landed in the hands of Black Ship, and the other three landed in the hands of Emperor Xi. It's just that … "

After Nuwa finished speaking, the eyes of Nuwa's true body, Fuxi's true body, and Heavenly Lord of Primordial Origin seemed to be inlaid with starlight.

Through the memories of Lei Xiao, he saw the scene of the Moon Fire Slaves descending.

After that, there was once when Heavenly Lord of Primordial Origin found a loophole and no longer attempted to "descend". Instead, he spread the seeds of the Human Race. That was how Li Mountain, Heavenly Palace, You Xiong, and the Divine Farmer Clan came to be …
"The reason why they were erased by the Truth should be due to the lack of the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit in Luo Zheng's hands." Fuxi added: "If the Ring of Descent was used together with the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit, it should be able to avoid being erased by the Truth and complete the true descent."
Luo Zheng waved his hand lightly, causing a scarlet-gold Chaotic Origin Spirit to appear in his hand. He said with astonishment: "I have never thought that these Chaotic Origin Spirits have such an effect …"

Chapter 3386
The Three-Eyed Race was also shocked when they saw the blood-red ball flying towards them.
Other than the Nirvana Realm races that submitted to the Wu Kong Clan, the other Nirvana Realm races didn't want to have anything to do with the Wu Kong Clan.

Now that the Wu Kong Clan suddenly appeared, they actually came for Luo Zheng?

Fairy Yun Xiao's cold voice was transmitted from the blood-red ball at the front. A graceful silhouette could be faintly seen in the ball.

… "
"Hehehe …"
The Evil God let out a series of deep laughter.
He gently stroked his body with both hands and looked satisfied. "I don't just have a physical body. I have completely become a Nirvana Realm creature!"
"What?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
Luo Zheng thought that Evil Deities' situation was like Feng Ge's, where he obtained a new body by chance. However, this body was like armor and couldn't leave the Nirvana Realm. It had no connection with the body outside the chaos.
However, Evil Deities' words were once again out of Luo Zheng's expectations.
"Since you have become a Nirvana Realm creature, what about your physical body in the Mother World?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
After becoming a pure Nirvana Realm creature, there wouldn't be another him in the Mother World. Logically speaking, this must be the case.
If both of them existed, then when Evil Deities from the Nirvana Realm arrived at the Nirvana Realm, wouldn't there be two of him alive and kicking?
"It's burned."
Evil Deities answered calmly. "The me you gave me, the me you created, has been burned by me. Now, I am completely reborn!"
A few months ago …
Under the lead of Fairy Qiong Xiao, Evil Deities went to the Death Bone Land that was unique to the Wu Kong Clan.
It was a very unique area. Before Evil Deities entered, Fairy Qiong Xiao had told him that once he entered the Death Bone Land, he would actually transform into another living being. At the same time, he would not be able to return to the Mother World.
In this way, not only would Evil Deities be able to break free from the shackles of the Dao Refinement Body, but he would also have an extremely powerful body.
The disadvantage was obvious. It would be very difficult for him to return to the Mother World unless the Wu Kong Clan obtained the method to descend.
Evil Deities agreed without any hesitation.
He would never be controlled by anyone and was willing to do anything to get rid of it.
Before entering the Death Bone Land, Evil Deities returned to the Mother World for the last time.
He dug a small tomb for himself. After entering the tomb, he sat upright. He had really dug a hole and buried himself.
Not long after Evil Deities entered the Nirvana Realm, a violent energy fluctuation was set off in Qi Bei Province.
If the Qi Bei Prefecture was the same as before, it would definitely alarm many people.
However, after the Nine Suns Apocalypse bloomed in Qi Bei Province, the entire Qi Bei Province was destroyed. Such a terrifying energy of truth did not attract anyone's attention.
"Whoosh!"
The energy fluctuation went straight to the tomb that Evil Deities had dug. After a while, a wisp of green flames rose from Evil Deities' body and quickly burned his body clean. However, the tomb was intact.
The situation of Evil Deities becoming a Nirvana Realm living being was completely different from Feng Ge. Feng Ge had put on a layer of armor for his Sun Soul under special circumstances, so he did not trigger the punishment of truth. On the other hand, the Wu Kong Clan's Death Bone Land disobeyed the truth, so it was naturally not allowed.
Xu would also attract the punishment of truth.
Luo Zheng stared at Evil Deities for a long time and suddenly shook his head. "You didn't become another person. You're still that Evil Deities."
"What do you mean?" Evil Deities frowned.
"There's no change in the world in my body," Luo Zheng replied briefly.
Evil Deities was the embodiment of negative emotions. If he really left him and his body disappeared, then the negative emotions would return to Luo Zheng's body.
"So what?" Evil Deities shrugged. "At least I left you!"
"For now," Luo Zheng said.
"You … Nonsense!"
The veins on Evil Deities' forehead were exposed, as if they were about to jump up.
Evil Deities' memories of the past had a deep impact on him. He had failed every time he faced Luo Zheng. Evil Deities who was proud and aloof was completely unacceptable.
However, he noticed that he had lost his composure and quickly adjusted himself. He said calmly, "No one knows what will happen in the future. You and I should have a way to coexist!"
Luo Zheng's eyes showed surprise again.
Evil Deities' temperament seemed to have changed greatly during this period of time. At least it was not like before, when he wanted to devour him whenever he saw him.
"Tell me, why are you looking for me? You can't just be telling me about your situation, right? "Luo Zheng asked.
"On behalf of the Wu Kong Clan, I would like to invite you to join the Wu Kong Clan," Evil Deities said.
When Evil Deities said this request, there was a sudden uproar around them.
On one hand, these Nirvana Realm races were afraid of the Wu Kong Clan. On the other hand, they were envious of the Nirvana Realm creatures who were chosen by the Wu Kong Clan.

Chapter 3387
Before the birth of the human civilization, the Elemental Spirit civilization was relatively open-minded.
They would even "help" other civilizations that had just entered the Nirvana Realm.
However, the premise of this "help" was that those civilizations could not threaten the existence of the Elemental Spirit civilization. After all, the Elemental Spirit civilization had a first-mover advantage and was much stronger than the other civilizations.
In fact, the idea of "the end" had been proposed in the first few chaos epochs, but there was a consensus within the Elemental Spirit civilization that they thought ending the end was a very ridiculous idea.
The entire Elemental Spirit civilization used a playful attitude to observe the struggles of other civilizations in the Nirvana Realm over and over again.
Until the birth of the human civilization, the Elemental Spirit civilization had an unprecedented sense of crisis.
For example, the fact that most civilizations could set foot in the Thirty-Three Heavens was enough to prove their strength. Even the "Primordial Gods of Chaos" and the Wing King would not be easy to enter.
So far, only seven or eight races had entered the innermost Jade Pure Land, and these races were very ancient and almost all of them were legendary races.
This was also the reason why the Celestial Hound of Trigram was shocked.
"When I obtained the unique information through the Six-Stripe Blood Tree, I believed that the human race was not ordinary, but I really didn't expect that the seniors would go to the Jade Pure Land …" Nuwa's eyes flashed.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven's expression was calm. He shook his head and said, "In fact, it's not that the later generations did not surpass the human civilization, it's just that the Elemental Spirit civilization did not give them this opportunity."
Nuwa nodded firmly. "If the Path of Order is really completed as they set it up, then the entire Chaos will have no chance to speak of."
The Primeval Lord of Heaven smiled faintly. "Actually, that's not necessarily true. The degree of chaos in the entire Chaos is always increasing, it can't be reduced. According to their Path of Order plan, it's just greatly delaying the chaos …"
All activities in the entire world were constantly moving towards chaos, and since the beginning of the Chaos, the degree of chaos was constantly accelerating.
If the Path of Order of the Elemental Spirit civilization could only survive ten thousand creatures, the degree of chaos would naturally become extremely slow, but as long as one creature was alive, the degree of chaos would increase …
Perhaps after thousands of chaos epochs, it would finally come again …
Therefore, in the eyes of the Heavenly Lord of Yuanshi, the "Path of Order" of the Elemental Spirit civilization was not a solution, but a way to delay the inevitable.
Hearing Yuanshi Tianzun's words, Nuwa, Fuxi, Luo Zheng, Gan Gaohan, and the others were enlightened. Although these people stood firmly on the Path of the End, there were still some who agreed with the Path of Order. They believed that the Path of Order was another way to save Chaos.
This part was just too cruel …
With the analysis of the Primeval Lord of Heaven, they naturally understood that Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno had chosen the wrong path from the beginning, and this path was destined to fail.
"It's a pity that Lie Shan and Dihong are still so stubborn." Gan Gaohan shook his head.
Fuxi said expressionlessly, "Some people are just like that. It's impossible to make them wake up on their own. Such people can only be awakened by others!"
Hearing Fuxi say that, Gan Gaohan revealed a joyous look, "Unless Li Shan is planning to act?"
After the Great Yi Heavenly Palace and the You Xiong Clan had shed all pretense of cordiality, they had been fighting on a small scale. Due to the rules, Li Shan had never made a direct move. Because of this, the Golden Crow launched a surprise attack, which almost killed them.
It would be a lie if one said that the Heaven Palace did not have any resentment.
But under the situation where Donghuang was trapped, they had no other choice but to endure.
But if Li Shan were to act, they would naturally be happy to see it …
"If Li Shan blindly tolerates it, they will naturally bully him. In fact, this time, the Golden Crows have already declared war. Most importantly, we can no longer tolerate their scheme this time," Nuwa said.
"Scheme? I wonder what scheme does the You Xiong Clan have? "Gan Gaohan asked with a solemn expression.
"They are using an extremely unique flame to burn across the various great prefectures in the Mother World. This flame is unable to burn Chaotic Origin Experts, but most living beings at the Paramita Realm and below could not resist it at all. They are already releasing their flames across the 300 great prefectures one after another.
"Nuwa continued.
"What? They actually did such an inhumane thing? "
"Why is that? Just to clean up these great prefectures? "
"Has the Di Hong Clan gone mad?"
The experts from the Heaven Palace revealed indignant expressions.
Even if they were strong, they could not do such a meaningless massacre …
Just as everyone was puzzled, Elder Hai suddenly said from the leftmost seat of the Lineage Dragon Palace Hall, "Ghost Green, this should be their motive."
Nuwa nodded at Elder Hai, indicating that he had guessed correctly.
After Elder Hai reminded them, the experts from the Heaven Palace immediately reacted.
"They are stirring up the negative emotions of the Mother World!"
"They have gotten Ghost Green?"
"The Di Hong Clan's motive is to control the entire Mother World through Ghost Green …"
The sense of danger in everyone's heart rose rapidly.
The current Heaven Palace and Li Shan could still ally with other superpowers to fight against You Xiong and Shennong. It was hard to predict the outcome since this was a battle on the same level.
However, if the Di Hong Clan controlled the Mother World, it would be equivalent to directly starting a war from a higher level, and an attack from that level would be impossible to resist.
"Although Ghost Green is the embodiment of the negative emotions of the Mother World, it is only an embodiment. Whether it can really shake the will of the Mother World is only a guess. Whether it can be realized or not is still unknown. Everyone, please calm down." Nuwa raised her hand and made a calming gesture.
As the birthplace of this round of chaos, the Mother World's will was high and unfathomable.
However, since the birth of Ghost Green, many knowledgeable people had hypothesized that through the negative emotions of the Mother World, they might be able to control the Mother World itself …
Previously, everyone was just hypothesizing, but now the Di Hong Clan had begun to try.
"It looks like the situation is not optimistic." Someone sighed.
Many people's faces were a little gloomy.
Luo Zheng, who had been silent at the side, suddenly laughed. "The situation may not be as bad as everyone thinks. Now that we have the Ring of Descent, we can even send experts from the Nirvana Realm!"
"Experts from the Nirvana Realm?" Nuwa stared at Luo Zheng with a questioning look in her beautiful eyes.

Chapter 3388
Previously at Li Mountain, Luo Zheng had already imparted the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation to Nuwa.
Nuwa and Fuxi's comprehension of the True Meaning of Dao was not slow either. But they still required some time …
Luo Zheng had already found the Human Race of the Paramita World. After making another trip to Ji Deleted Dimension, Nuwa had yet to cultivate the complete True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation. Hence, Nuwa naturally did not have the chance to meet King Ashoka.
Jiang Ziya was once an Undying Realm expert. In addition to staying in Ji Deleted Dimension for more than a thousand Primal Chaos epochs, his strength was unquestionable.
If he were to descend, he would naturally become an expert of his Mother World!
"That King Ashoka can descend …" Nuwa was still muttering.
Fuxi's eyes brightened, "That expert trapped in Ji Deleted Dimension?"
Given Fuxi's personality, he would be jealous of anyone stronger than him. But he was ultimately righteous and kind. Furthermore, he stood firmly on the side of the Human Race.

Fuxi extended his finger and lightly flicked it. A Ring of Descent floated in front of Luo Zheng.
Under the gaze of everyone, Luo Zheng sat down cross-legged. He placed a golden Chaotic Origin Spirit into the ring. After that, he began chanting the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation. Very soon, he returned to Ji Deleted Dimension.
During this period, Jiang Ziya had been accompanying Luo Nian in exploring the Profound World.
Luo Nian's performance the first time he entered the Profound World had already astonished Jiang Ziya. Ever since he entered the Profound World with Luo Nian, he was constantly in shock.
Previously, when Jiang Ziya was exploring that "mountain", he was like a blind man entering the forest. Every step he took was extremely difficult.
Jiang Ziya could only rely on his astonishing memory and analytical ability to make up for this flaw.
This kind of exploration was extremely time-consuming and exhausting. That was why he was unable to cross the mountain or cross the river for so many years ….
But everything changed with the appearance of Luo Nian.
Luo Nian's perception was completely different from Jiang Ziya's. His eyes were born to distinguish the paths of the Xuan Liang World.
In a few days, Luo Nian had already investigated the situation of the entire "mountain" clearly. Jiang Ziya's puzzles and mysteries were always solved by Luo Nian …
The only regrettable thing was that Luo Nian was right. That "mountain" didn't have many resources to begin with, so Luo Nian wasn't very interested in it.
Under the guidance of Luo Nian, Jiang Ziya crossed the river for the first time and began to explore further.
Jiang Ziya himself was still in a semi-ignorant state about the Xuanliang World. He almost relied on Luo Nian to lead the way …
After several explorations, Jiang Ziya's understanding and conjecture about the Mysterious World increased day by day. During the explorations, Jiang Ziya was accompanied by a very contradictory mentality.
He wanted to interact with the living beings of the Xuan Liang World and understand the civilization of the Xuan Liang World.
But the Xuan Liang World wasn't as safe as it seemed. There were several times when Luo Nian discovered the danger in advance and hurriedly brought Jiang Ziya back to the Jixue Space.
Although it was dangerous, the Xuan Liang World was still fascinating. Jiang Ziya even regarded Luo Nian as a unique treasure.
He would try his best to satisfy any request that Luo Nian made as long as it was within his ability.
When Luo Zheng returned to the Jixue Space, he saw that on the other side of the Jixue Pillar, another more magnificent and exquisite palace had been built. This palace was naturally the one given to Luo Nian.
Although Jiang Ziya was very resourceful and it wasn't difficult for him to build a palace out of thin air with the Gist of Trueness of Three Spirits Construction, it would still take a lot of effort.
Luo Zheng waited in front of the palace for a while. With a spatial fluctuation, Jiang Ziya appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
In front of Luo Zheng, Jiang Ziya had always acted as if he was aloof from worldly affairs. But this time, it was completely different. He had a smile on his face, as if he was getting along with Luo Zheng as an equal.
Luo Zheng was still a little puzzled, but he still asked respectfully, "Senior Jiang Ziya, where is Nian 'er?"
"He's still in the Xuan Liang World. That boy has been staying there for the past few days," Jiang Ziya replied.
"Is he in danger alone?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Luo Zheng was definitely worried about handing Luo Nian over to Jiang Ziya.
Jiang Ziya shook his head and said, "During this period, he has encountered several dangers, but that boy's escaping speed in the Xuan Liang World is simply amazing. He shouldn't be in any danger …"
The "space" in the Xuan Liang World existed in a very special form. Jiang Ziya still couldn't understand the way Luo Nian moved.
Looking at Jiang Ziya's convinced face, Luo Zheng was slightly relieved.
After all, Jiang Ziya had been in the Xuan Liang World for so many years. Nian 'er was like a fish in water in the Xuan Liang World and could protect himself.
"I didn't enter the Jixue Space to visit Nian 'er this time," Luo Zheng said.
"What's the matter?" Jiang Ziya was surprised. He thought Luo Zheng returned here because he missed Luo Nian.
"I want to invite Senior Jiang Ziya to descend to the mother world," Luo Zheng replied.
Jiang Ziya's eyes widened, "Descend? That's too dangerous! "
In fact, with Jiang Ziya's control of the Jixue Space, he could go to the chaos independently.
However, many Primal Chaos epochs ago, he had attempted to throw things from the world in his body into the Primal Chaos. However, the moment he threw them in, the Truth was triggered, and those things were wiped out. From then on, this thought was severed.
Luo Zheng saw Jiang Ziya's hesitation and told him about the Nirvana Realm creatures descending. At the same time, he took out the "Ring of Descent".
When Jiang Ziya was still hesitating, Heavenly Lord Yuanshi appeared again and smiled, "This disciple has always been cautious. But it's better to be safe than sorry. After all, the Jixue Space is somewhat different from the Nirvana Realm …"
Therefore, Jiang Ziya reached out his hand and grabbed out a green toad that was completely dark green from his inner world.
In the Lineage Dragon Palace, everyone was silently waiting for the powerhouse descending from the Jixue Space.
As Luo Zheng opened his eyes, a toad appeared under the Ring of Descent. Everyone's expression was quite interesting at this moment. The entire Lineage Dragon Palace became even quieter. With the Celestial Hound of the 33 Layers of Heaven in front of them, many people really thought this toad was that big shot.

Chapter 3389
Luo Zheng smirked and said, "What's the result of your observation after playing so many tricks?"
"The result is obvious." The smile on Ah Huo's face faded a little. "Joining Genryū is the only right path for you. I'll give you the authority of the Ten Giants and above. There are only six people in Genryū with this level of authority."
As soon as Ah Huo said this, the Nirvana Realm creatures present were shocked again.
Genryū had always been mysterious. Its internal structure and division of authority were not known to outsiders.
For example, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin had led the human race to fight against Genryū for so long. He only knew that Genryū's leader was Mu Ling.
However, the creatures of the Thirty-Two Levels of Heaven were well aware of Genryū's Ten Giants. After all, the Ten Giants were in charge of everything in Pingyi Heaven.
If Luo Zheng joined Genryū, would his position be above the Ten Giants?
The Nirvana Realm creatures could not hide their shock. It was directly shown on their faces.
Some of the weaker Nirvana Realm creatures, even if they joined Genryū, rarely had the chance to meet one of the Ten Giants.
Generally speaking, only Immortal Realm powerhouses were qualified to meet the Blood Red Parliament. One could imagine the status of the Ten Giants …
This Elemental Spirit Clan wouldn't be talking nonsense, right?
What right did that human kid have?
He was most likely joking …
These Nirvana Realm creatures all had the same thought.
Although they doubted Ah Huo, they only dared to think about it. They did not dare to say it out loud.
"After the Order Plan is fully implemented, I can give you 300 more places. If you think it's not enough, you can add more," Ah Huo added.
According to Genryū's plan, they planned to delay the arrival of this round of Chaos indefinitely.
However, the only way to delay it was to descend the level of Chaos in the entire Chaos.
Simply put, it was to eliminate almost all the creatures in the Chaos, leaving only 10,000 creatures.
These 10,000 creatures were equivalent to 10,000 places.
If the Order Plan could be implemented, the value of these 300 places could be imagined.
Ah Huo promised three hundred places at once. If it was not enough, he could add more. It was indeed very respectful.
"Three hundred places …" Luo Zheng pondered for a moment and shook his head. "That's too little."
"How much do you want?" Ah Huo widened his eyes and asked.
"At least ten billion?" Luo Zheng shrugged.
"Tens of billions is impossible. That kind of chaos cannot be contained." Huo actually thought according to Luo Zheng's idea. However, she reacted in an instant. This guy was just talking nonsense. She immediately became angry. "You!"
"I won't join either of you. I don't agree with the path you have chosen." Looking at Ah-Huo's angry face, Luo Zheng ignored him. "When I first entered the mother world, senior Qiu Yinhe told me about the future.
There are three paths to the debate! "
His voice suddenly became louder. "All the Order Plan Plan is just a cowardly appeasement method. What will descend will eventually descend. What kind of logic is that to kill everyone else in order to survive?"
"The so-called Voidless Path wants to return everything to zero. It's just the ravings of a madman. These guys just want to drag everyone into the abyss." Luo Zheng continued. "I don't know why the Chaos wants that needle to descend, but since that needle exists,
But as long as it exists, we have no reason to stop! I know that there are many sovereign civilizations present. You have also fought for the Path of the End. I also believe that this is the only right path. However, you are all walking … the evil path! "
"Shameless boasting! A kid who might not even reach the Immortal Realm dares to talk nonsense here. What a joke! "Elder Hu from the Purple Cloud Race berated loudly.
Elder Hu was also surprised that Ah Huo would offer such a price. He did not expect Luo Zheng to reject it.
Jumping out at this time would naturally attract the attention of "Ah Huo." How could he let go of this opportunity?
"Well said! If it weren't for Genryū's obstruction all these years, I'm afraid we would have succeeded long ago. Genryū is the main culprit for the Path of the End's repeated failures! "
Elder Hu had just jumped out when another Nirvana Realm race stepped out.
The one who spoke was a skinny Nirvana Realm creature from the Yan Sheng Race. The Yan Sheng Race was similar to the Three-Eyed Race. Although they were not very powerful, they had enough power to protect themselves.
In the 32 Levels of Heaven, there were actually many Nirvana Realm creatures like them. They were all failures of the Path of the End.
"Young man, you have great courage. Perhaps you can complete the Path of the End?" Clan Leader Jiutian of the Three-Eyed Race also stepped out.
"As long as there's a chance, I won't give up …"
"…"
The Nirvana Realm creatures who had once supported the Path of the End chose to step out at this time. They directly supported Luo Zheng in front of Ah Huo and the Tongtian Sect Leader.
Ah Huo and the Tongtian Sect Leader's expressions naturally did not look good.
It should be known that these Nirvana Realm creatures had fallen along with the fall of the human civilization. Independent Nirvana Realm creatures like the Three-Eyed Race and the Yan Sheng Race were very low-key. After all, they did not have the power to compete with the Wu Kong Clan and Genryū.
Opposing!
However, if they were to gather together, they would actually be a powerful force!
Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and the others did not expect this to happen. Luo Zheng's words had attracted the support of many Nirvana Realm creatures. This was simply unimaginable to them.
"Shut up, all of you!"
Ah Huo suddenly let out a voice.
His voice was so loud that it almost caused the aura in the entire Ping Yi Heaven to flow. The power contained in the aura spread in all directions as if it was solid, like surging water.
"What a terrifying energy flow …"
After Luo Zheng saw the surging energy, his expression changed slightly.
If there was no protection in Ping Yi Heaven, just this roar alone would probably be able to crush most of the Nirvana Realm creatures present into powder.
The other Nirvana Realm creatures were also shocked by this voice. The scene suddenly became silent. On the other hand, the Tongtian Sect Leader looked calm and collected.
Ah Huo's anger only lasted for a moment. When she faced Luo Zheng, a smile appeared on her face again.
She would definitely carry out the things she had set her eyes on. Moreover, she had already communicated her thoughts with Mu Ling.
Luo Zheng's rejection made her lose face, but she still would not give up.

Chapter 3390
"Feng!"
Ah Huo's slender body suddenly burst into flames. The terrifying flames burned her completely.
The scarlet flames floating in the white ball were her true body.

"If it's Mu Ling, she will fight you to the death," Ah Huo's voice added.
All these years, Genryū and the Wu Kong Clan had been fighting fiercely. Not only did the Wu Kong Clan not decline, they became stronger step by step. But overall, Genryū still had the absolute advantage.
If Genryū really wanted to fight the Wu Kong Clan to the death, the situation would be the same as the human race.

God.

No one would do such a stupid thing.
The current situation was similar to the past. The Wu Kong Clan's sudden rise had already threatened Genryū's position. If Genryū risked being heavily injured, he could also eliminate the Wu Kong Clan.
And now, there was no third party. Those Nirvana Realm clans who still held on to the Path of the End were nothing …
From this point, Genryū had a psychological advantage against the Wu Kong Clan.

He was the man who personally led the Wu Kong Clan to become stronger.

His calm and simple answer revealed his strong determination …
Ah Huo didn't say anything. As the flames in the white ball trembled slightly, she floated away.

He was also an extremely intelligent being. Luo Zheng's words had already made it very clear that he believed in the Path of the End, and there was absolutely no possibility of him changing his tune.
Since that was the case, there was no point in him staying here and persuading them. He could only think of another way.
