The three blood-red spheres also flew in another direction.
Both Ah-Huo of the Genryū and Tongtian of the Wu Kong Clan had left, but the shock they left behind had not disappeared.
Especially the final exchange between the experts of the two clans, it caused the numerous Paramita beings present to be extremely shocked.
In order to fight over Luo Zheng, the Wu Kong Clan and Genryū would fight to the death?
One had to know that the two largest races in the Nirvana Realm had been fighting openly and covertly for almost four hundred divine eras. However, both sides were quite fearful of each other and had never escalated to a situation where they would fight to the death.
Now, for a human kid who had yet to step into the Nirvana Realm?
What did this mean?
It meant that Luo Zheng had a reason that was worthy of them fighting to the death!
Then, what was this reason?
Almost all of the Paramita beings had developed a strong interest in Luo Zheng at this moment.
Even Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and the others were at a complete loss.
Luo Zheng was very important to the current Taiyi Heavenly Palace, but it was not to the extent that the Wu Kong Clan and Genryū would fight over him, right?
"Senior Yuanshi Tianzun, why is this?" Luo Zheng said somewhat helplessly.
The attitude of the two clans towards him was also beyond Luo Zheng's expectations. Luo Zheng wanted to find an answer from Yuanshi Tianzun.
From the beginning, Yuanshi Tianzun did not say anything. He seemed to be avoiding Tongtian.
Now that Tongtian had left, Yuanshi Tianzun came to life and said leisurely, "Perhaps it's because of the eighteen engravings on your body?"
The Nirvana Realm beings in the Thirty-two Layers of Heaven did not know that Luo Zheng had obtained the eighteen engravings, but Tongtian and Huo must have known.
"Because I have these engravings …" Luo Zheng was somewhat uncertain. This reason was not very tenable.
"According to my understanding, the more engravings one has, the more comfortable they are in the immortal picture scroll. However, whether the eighteen engravings will cause other problems was not determined at that time. Now that so many years have passed, many things that I don't know about have happened.
Things I don't know. "
Although Yuanshi Tianzun was a know-it-all, he had been trapped in the Thirty-three Heavens. Naturally, he did not know about the things that had happened in so many chaos eras. However, according to Yuanshi Tianzun's judgment, the problem was most likely still with the eighteen engravings.
Just when everyone was still guessing, the edge of the picture scroll that was unfolded in the sky was suddenly plated with a layer of gold.
The picture scroll that was hanging above Ping Yitian showed a variety of colors. These colors slowly took shape and turned into the shape of silhouettes. Those were the silhouettes of various Nirvana Realm beings!
"Buzz …"
Not long after, a circle appeared in the immortal picture scroll. This circle fluctuated and turned into a huge whirlpool, filling the entire immortal picture scroll.
"Whirlpool …"
Recently, whenever Luo Zheng saw a whirlpool, he could not help but associate it with the Xuanliang World.
"Yes, this is connected to somewhere in the Xuanliang World," Yuanshi Tianzun confirmed for Luo Zheng.
As the whirlpool continued to fluctuate, the silhouettes that were originally arranged in a disorderly manner also lined up neatly along with the fluctuations of the whirlpool.
The entire whirlpool was divided into hundreds of circles of different sizes. The silhouette of the Nirvana Realm beings that were arranged in the innermost layer was a round ball …
This round ball could be the Wu Kong Clan, or it could be Genryū.
However, Yuanshi Tianzun once mentioned that the first in the immortal picture scroll was Genryū's "Eye Spirit". Presumably, this round ball should be that Eye Spirit.
Ranked second was a "spider-like" creature. Yuanshi Tianzun explained to Luo Zheng that it was the spider spirit of the "Star Spider Civilization" in the 96th round of chaos. Although its silhouette had always been left in the immortal picture scroll, its body had already been destroyed.
Its original body had already fallen in the Thirty-three Heavens.
"The second-ranked existence will fall in the Thirty-three Heavens?" Luo Zheng was very surprised when he heard this.

Chapter 3391
After stepping into the door, before she could get used to the darkness, a sweet smell of rust entered her nose.
Ling Shuang frowned, her eyes filled with confusion.
Suddenly, he realized with a start that he could smell something.
She was just a Yang Soul Body …
But when she looked at herself, she was shocked.
Her Soul Body was filled with red threads, which gave off a ruby-like luster.
These red threads outlined the veins in her arms, and at the same time, the five viscera, the six bowels, the cervical spine, and even the eyes, ears, mouth, and nose of her head were roughly outlined.
"I … What's wrong with me?"
Ling Shuang took a few steps back, she wanted to leave the door.
But when she thought of Granny, the fear in her heart melted like snowflakes in June …
No matter what fate awaited her, she would accept it without hesitation.
If Ling Shuang had been to Spirit City, she would have realised that everything that happened to her Yang Soul was very similar to stepping into the Saint Soul Realm.
The difference was that she did not need to enter the Soul Cloud, she only needed to take a step towards the door.
When her soul had the five senses, Ling Shuang became more sensitive to the surrounding illusions, even if it was a pitch black tunnel, everything big or small would appear in her eyes.
"These two sides are prisons?"
On both sides of the tunnel, there were rooms separated by fences.
The fences closest to the door were broken, it looked like they were broken by brute force, the rooms were naturally empty.
Ling Shuang continued to walk along the tunnel, her gaze constantly sweeping across the partitions on both sides.
"They're all empty …"
"The things locked inside, have they all escaped?"
"But I've clearly seen those monsters …"
In Ling Shuang's heart, those monsters represented fear.
When she was young, she would often wake up from nightmares, it was because of these monsters …
But monsters also represented power.
She needed power, and she needed these monsters, no matter what she had to pay, she would not hesitate.
After walking a few thousand feet forward, the fences of the rooms on both sides of the passageway were all broken. Ling Shuang felt a little disappointed. She had mustered up so much courage, but she had barged into an empty door. This was not what she wanted to see.
Just as she passed by another empty house, there was a rustling sound in front of her.
Hearing this sound, Ling Shuang's eyes flashed, and she quickly walked forward.
Just as Ling Shuang approached the metal fence, a furry hand grabbed onto the fence. "Bang!"
All the fences started to shake violently …
"About five pounds of muscle …"
"Awoo …"
When the monsters in the room heard that, more of them rushed toward the bars.
They beat, shook, and made all kinds of strange cries.
The entire passageway seemed to come alive at this moment. The darkness in the distance transformed into the mouth of a monster, and the surging aura forced Ling Shuang to take a few steps back.
The excited cries of the monsters in the room startled something.
In the darkness of the tunnel, two red lights suddenly appeared. The red lights approached from afar, quickly closing the distance between them and Ling Shuang.
When it got closer, Ling Shuang finally saw its face clearly.
It looked like a huge monkey. Its hairy hands hung on the ground, and its hands were covered with sharp nails. As it walked, its nails dragged on the ground, making ear-piercing sounds.
Ling Shuang's eyes widened. That was the monster that had been observing her from the door countless times!
"You … I'm coming in," said Ling Shuang.
She wanted to ask who it was.
But in his heart, he felt that he was familiar with it.
It had observed her many times, and she had observed it many times. It shouldn't be appropriate to ask that question.
The monster stretched out its long arms, and its sharp nails extended toward Ling Shuang.
Even in the dark, the surface of its nails shone with a cold light. Ling Shuang didn't doubt that this monster could easily tear her to pieces.
But when its nails were in front of Ling Shuang, it knelt on one knee in front of her with a plop.
"I, I, we … have waited for so many years. It's too painful. Wu wu, please fulfill your promise and help us!"
The voice was hoarse and rough, like two pieces of iron rubbing against the ground.
But that didn't stop it from begging.
"Let you go?"
Ling Shuang's eyes were filled with confusion.
"The curse on us has been there for too long. My brothers couldn't take it and ended their lives. I dragged them out of the prison and buried them …" it said again.
Now, Ling Shuang understood a little.
These monsters were also in the fences, but they were cursed and had been locked up here, waiting for her to come.
But did she come too late? Some of the monsters couldn't take it and chose to commit suicide?
The big holes in the fences should have been made by this monster. It smashed those holes just to bury them.
"I don't know how to remove your curse," Ling Shuang said honestly.
Granny knew that she had this door. She even knew that she could command the monsters inside …
But what this door meant, and what relationship Ling Shuang had with these monsters, Granny didn't mention.
Granny probably didn't know either …
Just as Ling Shuang finished speaking, the monsters on both sides of the tunnel suddenly roared.
Her answer didn't satisfy them.
Whoosh!
The claw that was extended in front of her suddenly wrapped around her body and caught her in mid-air.
"Impossible! You can't not know! " The monster's voice was filled with unwillingness. "The promise wasn't like this. You were supposed to save us!"
The monster's strength was unimaginable.
Ling Shuang felt that she was like a fragile porcelain in its hands. If it exerted a little force, she would shatter into pieces.
"Ah wu wu, ah wu wu, ah wu wu …"
The monsters on both sides of the tunnel started to speak in unison.
"Did you hear that?" It said to Ling Shuang, "They said that if you can't do it, they will kill you … My brothers are already in despair. They don't care about crushing their last hope!"
This was a threat, but it was also the truth.
Ling Shuang, who grew up in a carefree environment, didn't expect that she would be the last hope of these monsters.
When she finally entered the door, Ling Shuang didn't know anything.
Their feelings could be imagined …
Gu wu wu —
Just as everyone was shouting, a sharp voice suddenly came from afar.
The owner of the voice seemed to be very authoritative. When this voice came, the tunnel instantly returned to silence.
Ling Shuang bit her lip. "But I really don't know …"
Gu lu wu wu …
The voice in the distance continued to speak.

Chapter 3392
There was naturally no such thing as absolute fairness in this world.
Every living being had its own unique form. In a specific environment, it would naturally gain an advantage. For example, a fish could swim freely in the water, but it would die on land.
Clearly, his human form did not have an advantage in the Immortal Painting.
Now that he had no choice, Luo Zheng could only use "Crab Steps" to make do.
From the outside, the Immortal Painting looked like a single page, but in fact, the Immortal Painting had three pages.
And be it the first, second, or third page, they were not as blank as they looked. There were still quite a number of "curly beasts" on them.
There were many different theories regarding the origin of the curly beasts. Some said that the curly beasts were actually some kind of living being in the Xuanliang world, but they were naturally suited to live in the Immortal Painting. There were also some who said that the curly beasts were created by the Instant civilization.
However, they were all guesses.
Not long after Luo Zheng entered the Immortal Painting, he encountered a curly beast.
The curly beast looked similar to the silhouette of a Nirvana life form. It was not big and looked like a wolf cub that did not even reach his knees.
When Luo Zheng saw the curly beast, it had already pounced at him.
Whether it was the other Nirvana Realm creatures or Luo Zheng himself, there was still a certain limit to their movements in the immortal painting scroll. It was as if they were really embedded in the painting and the movements they could make were very limited. However, this beast in the scroll was much more flexible.
However, the curly beast was very agile.
Seeing the curly beast pounce at him, Luo Zheng chose to counterattack.
The battle process was more boring and simple than Luo Zheng imagined.
The moment the curly beast approached, Luo Zheng's arm grabbed it from top to bottom. Although it felt awkward, he still accurately held the curly beast in his hand. At the same time, his other hand turned and tore. The black curly beast was torn apart.
It was torn in half.
The entire process was silent. It was as though continuous images were presented in front of Luo Zheng's eyes.
The torn curly beast turned into wisps of black smoke and rapidly gathered towards Luo Zheng's body, completely fusing with his body.
When the smoke entered Luo Zheng's body, Luo Zheng's whole body trembled violently.
At the same time, this wisp of black smoke shot out from the Immortal Picture Scroll. It descended from the sky and shot straight into Luo Zheng's Three Clarities Stone.
The complicated patterns on the surface of the Three Clarities Stone were smeared with black.
"Senior Heavenly Primogenitor, how many beasts do we have to kill to get to the second page?" Luo Zheng asked.
There was a requirement to go from the first page to the second page, and that was to absorb enough curly beasts.
"No," the Heavenly Lord of the Origin said, "The Curly Beasts on the first page are not very strong, so you won't be given too much black smoke. If you only kill Curly Beasts of this size, you will need about four to five thousand …"
Every time the Immortal Scroll was opened, the number of beasts in the scroll was limited.
The reason why those Nirvana Realm beings were in a hurry was because they wanted to kill as many rolling beasts as possible in the shortest amount of time.
"So many …"
Luo Zheng felt somewhat helpless.
It was not difficult to kill Curly Beasts, but the number of Curly Beasts in the Immortal Scroll seemed to be too little.
If the number of Curly Beasts killed was not enough, one would not be able to enter the second page. When the Immortal Picture Scroll was closed, those who could not enter the second page would naturally be expelled. That would be considered a failure …
The price of failure in the Immortal Picture Scroll was not high. After leaving the Immortal Picture Scroll, the "heavy stone" would be wasted.
However, it was not difficult for even the Taiyi Heavenly Palace to get a few heavy stones, let alone these Nirvana Realm beings.
Therefore, almost all the Nirvana Realm beings who had achieved the Divine Soul Realm and the Body of Innumerable Tribulations would enter the Immortal Picture Scroll. This intensified the competition in the Immortal Picture Scroll.
However, the Immortal Picture Scroll was not a safe place. Every time the Immortal Picture Scroll was opened, many people died in it.
Some were killed by the Curly Beasts, but more of them killed each other.
After killing the Curly Beast, Luo Zheng ran rampant in the Immortal Picture Scroll again.
Due to the limitation of his vision, he could not even determine his own position. He only moved in one direction.
However, Luo Zheng's luck was really bad. The Curly Beasts he met along the way were sparse, and most of them were very thin and small.
In the spirit of "even mosquito legs are meat", Luo Zheng naturally did not let any of them go.
In more than two hours, Luo Zheng only met eight Curly Beasts. Among them, a snake-shaped Curly Beast escaped directly. Luo Zheng chased after it for a long time but could not catch up. He could only choose to give up.
…
On the bronze ground, many Nirvana Realm beings stared at the Three-Clarities Stone on the ground.
After the Immortal Picture Scroll was opened, it showed the silhouettes of the strong men in the Immortal Realm. That was the picture on the third page. Luo Zheng and others were still on the first page, so their every move could not be observed.
However, every time the Nirvana Realm beings killed a Curly Beast, a black ink stain would descend from the picture scroll and smear on the Three-Clarities Stone.
Every time the bronze ground refreshed, by observing the Three-Clarities Stone, they could see the person's progress in the Immortal Picture Scroll.
Most of the Nirvana Realm beings only cared about the Three-Clarities Stone of their own people. However, this time, many Nirvana Realm beings intentionally or unintentionally looked at Luo Zheng, whether it was the Nirvana Realm beings who submitted to Genryū or the neutral Three-Eyed Race.
The Nirvana Realm beings were all silently observing.
Logically speaking, a person who could be roped in by Tongtian and Huo should have a stunning performance in the Immortal Picture Scroll. At least, it should not be difficult for him to reach the Immortal Realm in one go.
However, after more than two hours, the number of ink stains on Luo Zheng's Three-Clarities Stone could be counted with one's fingers.
"That human kid seems to have no ability."
"More than two hours to kill Curly Beasts in single digits?"
"If this continues, he will be eliminated soon …"
Although those voices were deliberately lowered, Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, and others could still hear them clearly.
"What is Luo Zheng doing?" Gan Gaohan asked.
Ning Xuyuan shook his head. "Maybe he's just unlucky. I heard that those Curly Beasts appear randomly."
The people of the Heavenly Palace present were only in the Saint Soul Realm. None of them could enter the Immortal Picture Scroll, so naturally, they could not say anything.
"Hiss hiss hiss …"
At this moment, wisps of black smoke continuously drilled out of the Immortal Picture Scroll and rapidly descended toward them.
When Gan Gaohan and others saw these ink stains, their eyes lit up. "He's here?"
Ning Xuyuan also revealed a trace of a smile. When had Luo Zheng ever disappointed them?
However, as the black ink continued to descend, it actually drilled into a Three-Clarities Stone at the side. This Three-Clarities Stone belonged to the Purple Cloud Race's Wudang.

Chapter 3393
Not far away, the Three-eyed Clan and the Yan Sheng Clan naturally saw Luo Zheng's Sanqing Stone.
The expressions on their faces also became subtle.
Originally, they thought that Luo Zheng would amaze the world with a single brilliant feat in the Immortal Picture Scroll.
However, they did not expect that Luo Zheng's performance was not only not outstanding, but also had no achievements.
For example, there were a total of three people from the Three-eyed Clan who had entered the Immortal Picture Scroll this time. Not to mention the Three-eyed Boy Fei Yu's performance, even the Three-eyed Clan member who had the worst performance was much better than Luo Zheng …
However, they did not believe that the Sect Leader would be wrong. The reason for Luo Zheng's poor performance was probably because it was his first time entering the Immortal Picture Scroll.
In fact, even if he did not reach the Immortal Realm this time, it was not a big deal.
Most of the Nirvana Realm creatures in the Thirty-two Levels of Heaven had failed several times. Those who had reached the Immortal Realm had also entered the Immortal Picture Scroll dozens or even hundreds of times.
However, the Sect Leader and Ah Huo's previous statements made everyone have high expectations for him. They believed that he could pass in one try, but they did not expect his performance to be so clumsy.
In the picture scroll, Luo Zheng was still moving forward at a moderate pace.
Although he did not know the progress of others, the number of curly beasts he had killed in more than two hours was too little. He knew in his heart.
However, this could not be blamed on Luo Zheng, because the number of curly beasts that appeared around Luo Zheng was too little.
"Eh, in front …"
Luo Zheng suddenly saw a pitch-black thing rolling not far away. As Luo Zheng approached, he realized that it was a curly beast that was not small in size!
Seeing this curly beast, Luo Zheng was slightly happy. It turned out that his luck was not so bad that it was hopeless.
However, just as Luo Zheng was approaching in a hurry, three Nirvana Realm creatures rushed over from another direction.
These three Nirvana Realm creatures were aggressive. Two of them fixed the curly beast on the left and right, while the other one directly pierced through it.
The curly beast that was killed turned into wisps of black smoke and quickly gathered on the body of the Nirvana Realm creature.
After killing the curly beast, the three Nirvana Realm creatures noticed Luo Zheng who was not far away.
"That brat from the Human Clan?"
"Hehe, I don't seem to have gained anything …"
"I thought he was some amazing character, but he didn't even have the right to enter the second page. He's not worthy of my attention."
Although everyone had turned into flat paintings, their voices were still transmitted over. Because of the space, they could hear everything clearly.
The more curly beasts they killed, the more black smoke would appear on their bodies.
The bodies of the three Nirvana Realm creatures were surrounded by thick black smoke. On the other hand, Luo Zheng was naked with only a faint black smoke surrounding him.
Previously, Luo Zheng had been invited by the Wu Kong Clan and Genryū at the same time. It was impossible for these Nirvana Realm creatures to not be jealous, especially since Luo Zheng was not a Nirvana Realm creature. He came from the chaos and could freely enter and exit the Nirvana Realm.
After saying these words, they hurriedly left.
Right now, everyone was making the best use of their time to hunt the curly beasts. No one was willing to waste any more time.
Luo Zheng watched them leave and rolled his eyes helplessly. Even the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning sighed quietly. Luo Zheng's luck seemed to be really bad.
In fact, it was not difficult to pass the first page of the Immortal Painting Scroll. The position of the curly beasts was random. Even if one's luck was bad, there should be a lot to gain …
Luo Zheng and the Heavenly Lord of the Beginning did not think that it was not due to luck that he could not encounter the curly beasts.
Every time there were curly beasts in the direction he moved, those curly beasts would mysteriously disappear or directly move to other places.
For more than two hours, this kind of thing kept repeating.
No matter how fast Luo Zheng moved, most of the curly beasts would be able to avoid him in the first place.
However, Luo Zheng's perspective could not see this scene at all. Therefore, he could only attribute it to his bad luck.
At this moment, above the Immortal Painting Scroll, there was a huge smiling face floating.
This face was also a painting, but this painting had left the Immortal Painting Scroll and was floating in a special space.
"It seems to be a little impatient?"
The smiling face made a soft voice.
When this voice sounded, the smiling face instantly turned into a crying face. At the same time, the voice became rough, "Then, shall we start the guidance now?"
"Let's wait a little longer. This time, there are too many ants rushing into the scroll. I want to kill some of them," the smiling face laughed, and the voice became soft again.
"Will they find out?" the crying face asked.
"Do you think they don't know of our existence?" The smiling face snorted coldly, "This group of cunning guys is not capable enough, so they want to use our hands. Wishful thinking."
"Then let's do it!"
The crying face no longer worried about anything.
In that special space, a big black hand appeared. The big hand was holding a slender ink pen.
Under the light outline of the ink pen on the Immortal Painting Scroll, a lifelike curly beast had already been formed.
However, this curly beast was much bigger than before. It had a fierce face and sharp teeth, and its body was covered with sharp thorns. Obviously, it was not the curly beasts that were at the mercy of others.
This kind of curly beast was called a tapir. It was very rare even on the second page. The Nirvana Realm creatures would avoid it if they encountered it.
However, as the slender ink pen continued to outline, tapirs appeared on the scroll one by one.
In a short while, the face had drawn hundreds of tapirs!
"Go, at least eliminate half of them," the crying face ordered.
Upon receiving the order, the tapirs that had been "created" spread out in all directions like blooming ink, searching for their prey.
The competition between the Nirvana Realm creatures on the first page was very fierce. Every time the Immortal Painting Scroll opened, only one-third of the creatures could enter the second page. Every curly beast would be besieged.
On the upper left corner of the Immortal Painting Scroll, four Nirvana Realm creatures worked together to kill a group of curly beasts. Just when they were still unsatisfied, a black shadow suddenly appeared not far away.
When the four Nirvana Realm creatures saw the black shadow, they were delighted. They really wanted to surround it. However, one of the Nirvana Realm creatures realized that something was wrong. When he saw the full appearance of the black shadow, his heart skipped a beat.
"It's a tapir! Run! "
This Nirvana Realm creature had been to the second page before and had seen the powerful tapir with his own eyes.
What he could not figure out was, this was the first page. How did the tapir appear?
He did not have the chance to know the answer …
After the tapir locked on to its prey, its figure became erratic.
Its figure appeared and disappeared from time to time.
This was because compared to the Nirvana Realm creatures who became paintings, although the tapir was also a painting, it could leave the scroll for a short period of time.
The moment it jumped out of the scroll, from the perspective of the scroll, the tapir naturally disappeared …
When the tapir fell back into the scroll, its figure appeared again.

Chapter 3394
The moment the four Nirvana Realm creatures were killed, four faint blood-colored dots appeared in the immortal painting.
After the blood-colored dots appeared, the Nirvana Realm creatures present became cautious.
The appearance of the blood-colored dots meant that someone had died. It all depended on which clan they belonged to.
The immortal painting had only been open for less than four hours. It was basically impossible for anyone to die on the first page. It was very unusual for such a situation to occur.
Under everyone's gaze, four blood-colored dots suddenly shot out four blood-colored lights!
Swish! Swish! Swish!
Swish! Swish! Four blood-colored lights shot out from the painting and went straight to the north, hitting the four Sanqing Stones.
Beside the four Sanqing Stones stood the Withered Blood Clan!
The Withered Blood Clan was also a well-known clan in the 32 Levels of Heaven. After seeing this scene, the Withered Blood Clan members' faces turned extremely ugly.
The four of them were geniuses that the Withered Blood Clan had carefully nurtured. They had only been in the immortal painting for less than four hours, and they had all died?
"What happened in the immortal painting?!"
"They should be on the first page. Why?!"
"…"
The Withered Blood Clan members were extremely angry and puzzled. However, the other Nirvana Realm creatures heaved a sigh of relief. No matter what happened in the immortal painting, it was fine as long as it wasn't their clan that died.
However, the Worm Tapir released by the immortal painting was overwhelming. How could the other Nirvana Realm creatures be spared?
Just after the four Withered Blood Clan members died, two blood-colored dots appeared in the immortal painting.
When they saw the two blood-colored dots, their hearts were in suspense again.
"What's going on?"
"Since when was the first page of the immortal painting so dangerous?"
"Two more died …"
The two blood-colored lights fell into another Nirvana Realm creature. The Nirvana Realm creatures' faces turned extremely ugly.
"It might be an accident!"
"Although it's rare for people to die on the first page, there have been cases in history …"
"They can only blame it on their bad luck!"
In history, there weren't many who died on the first page of the immortal painting. Occasionally, one or two people would die. However, the opening of the immortal painting this time was indeed a little strange.
Just when the Nirvana Realm creatures thought it was an accident, dozens of blood-colored dots suddenly appeared on the first page of the immortal painting!
The moment these blood-red dots appeared, the entire Bronze Plains fell silent.
The Nirvana Realm creatures who were looking up at the sky widened their eyes and opened their mouths wide. They could not utter a single word. The blood-red dots were like a shocking pattern, telling every Nirvana Realm creature the bad news that happened on the first page.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
Dozens of bloody lights intersected and shot down from the immortal scroll. Every time they hit a Sanqing Stone, it meant that the Nirvana Realm creature in the Sanqing Stone had died.
"This, this, this … how could this be!" Gan Gaohan's voice was somewhat anxious.
"Will Luo Zheng be alright?" Ning Xuyuan was much calmer.
"There's a bloody light coming!" Li Bianxue suddenly pointed.
A beam of blood-red light from the Immortal Picture Scroll shot toward them. When they saw this blood-red light, the hearts of the people from the Heavenly Palace suddenly tightened.
Swish!
In the end, the beam of blood-red light still deviated slightly and landed on a nearby Sanqing Stone. Hanging on that Sanqing Stone was a member of the Purple Cloud Clan!
The people from the Heavenly Palace finally heaved a sigh of relief when they saw the beam of blood-red light landing on the ground.
Fortunately, it was not Luo Zheng …
The people from the Heavenly Palace were relieved, but the people from the Purple Cloud Clan were depressed.
This time, there were a total of five people from the Purple Cloud Clan who entered the Immortal Picture Scroll. Other than the leader "Wu Dang," the one who was most likely to reach the Immortal Realm was the one who died. How could they not feel distressed?
After dozens of blood-red lights landed, the Nirvana Realm creatures still looked up at the Immortal Picture Scroll.
So many people died on the first page in one breath. The remaining people might not be able to survive alone.
As expected …
After a short while, hundreds of blood-red dots appeared on the Immortal Picture Scroll again. Hundreds of people died again!
After the blood-red light landed, a white flash flashed across the Bronze Plains in the distance.
The relief sculpture on the Bronze Plains refreshed again. The Nirvana Realm creatures once again looked at the Sanqing Stones of those who died.
The anchoring bodies around the Sanqing Stones had been torn into pieces and scattered on the ground …
…
Inside the Immortal Picture Scroll, there was a bloody storm, but the Nirvana Realm creatures in the picture scroll were very calm.
The area of the Immortal Picture Scroll was quite large. Moreover, because it had turned into a picture, the Nirvana Realm creatures' vision was very limited. They could not see areas that were slightly further away. Naturally, they did not know that so many people had died.
Luo Zheng's position was relatively remote, so he managed to avoid these deadly tapirs.
But when he looked in one direction for a curly-up beast, he suddenly found a black area spread out on the ground.
Luo Zheng thought it was a curly-up curly-up beast. Just when he rushed forward excitedly, he found that it was a torn "picture" and the black area spread out on the ground was blood!
"This is a Nirvana Realm creature, not a curly-up beast. How could it be torn into pieces?"
Luo Zheng was a little surprised, "Senior Heavenly Primogenitor, didn't you say that the first page was not dangerous?"
The Heavenly Primogenitor did not expect such a thing to happen. He thought for a while and said, "Maybe it's an individual phenomenon. Some Nirvana Realm creatures are too weak."
"After all, they have achieved the Body of Eternity. What kind of Nirvana Realm creature would lose to a curly-up beast?" Luo Zheng felt that the Heavenly Primogenitor's analysis was untenable.
The Heavenly Primogenitor also felt a little strange and added, "Maybe some curly-up beasts that should not have appeared on the first page appeared. This possibility is very small, but it still exists …"
If so, Luo Zheng naturally had to be careful.
Just when the Heavenly Primogenitor and Luo Zheng were analyzing, three Nirvana Realm creatures suddenly rushed over from afar. They were the three Nirvana Realm creatures who killed the curly-up beast before Luo Zheng did!
It seemed that something was chasing them from behind. The three Nirvana Realm creatures were running at an incredible speed, and they were still calling for help while running!
Luo Zheng did not react, but the Heavenly Primogenitor had already noticed.
"That's a tapir! A tapir appeared on the first page? Luo Zheng, run! "The Heavenly Primogenitor quickly warned.
"A tapir?"
Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment. The Heavenly Primogenitor's urgent voice revealed extreme danger!
He saw a black silhouette that appeared and disappeared from time to time chasing after the three Nirvana Realm creatures.
The black silhouette that disappeared and appeared again was extremely fast. In less than a breath, it had already caught up with the three Nirvana Realm creatures.

Chapter 3395
"Hurry up and leave! Why are you in a daze! "
Seeing Luo Zheng standing still, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin urged him.
The blooming black 'ink' was mixed with the body, and the appearance of the Nirvana Realm creature could no longer be seen clearly. However, the three Nirvana Realm creatures were most likely dead.
Luo Zheng, who came back to his senses, waved his hands and feet fiercely and began to run wildly in the other direction.
Although it was difficult to coordinate running like a crab, at the moment of life and death, his speed was surprisingly fast …
After killing the three Nirvana Realm creatures, the Insect Tapir naturally noticed Luo Zheng. It stood up gently from the scroll, and the silhouette projected on the scroll quickly 'disappeared', leaving only four round black spots. The four black spots were the Insect Tapir's four legs.
They were the projections that stepped on the immortal scroll.
As the Insect Tapir leaped up, the four black spots disappeared as well!
The Insect Tapir was running in a higher level of space. In Luo Zheng's eyes, it disappeared, reappeared, disappeared again, and reappeared again …
Every time it reappeared, the Insect Tapir was a little closer to him!
"That thing is so fast!" Luo Zheng's face was full of nervousness.
"That's why I urged you to leave as soon as possible. You're still watching the show," the Heavenly Lord of the Origin said hurriedly.
Ever since Luo Zheng carried the Human Tribe's weapon of civilization, he had encountered a few crises. However, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin always seemed to have a perfect plan. He had never been so anxious before.
After all, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin always had a backup plan when Luo Zheng encountered trouble before, or he knew that Luo Zheng could handle it.
However, this time Luo Zheng was in the immortal scroll, and he was a picture in the scroll.
It was very inconvenient for him to move in the form in the scroll, let alone use other means. The most terrible thing was that he could not get out of the immortal scroll voluntarily.
Every time the immortal scroll was opened, he could only enter but not leave.
Even if he was trapped in the first page because he could not kill enough beasts in the scroll, he had to wait until the immortal scroll was closed before he could be ejected.
Under such circumstances, there was basically no possibility of escaping if he encountered a Insect Tapir alone!
"What should we do now?" Luo Zheng asked hurriedly.
In a short period of time, the Insect Tapir was less than three meters away from him. If this continued, he would definitely die in a few breaths of time.
"What can we do …"
The Primeval Lord of Heaven was also thinking quickly.
With such a huge limitation, no method would work.
He really wanted to tell Luo Zheng that he had no other choice but to resign himself to fate, but when the gloominess in his stomach gathered at the tip of his tongue, it all turned into one word: "Run."
When Luo Zheng rushed forward for a distance, the Insect Tapir was less than a foot away from Luo Zheng.
When the tapir disappeared and reappeared, it would probably pounce on him!
The tapir disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight …
The moment the Insect Tapir disappeared, Luo Zheng suddenly stopped and ran in the opposite direction.
The Worm Tapir did not expect that Luo Zheng would be in such a situation, so it would definitely pounce in the wrong direction.
Although Luo Zheng knew that he couldn't get rid of them in the end, he could still fight for a little bit of time.
Luo Zheng's strategy was indeed effective.
The Insect Tapir on the immortal picture scroll had already jumped up high and pounced on Luo Zheng in the picture. When Luo Zheng turned back, the Insect Tapir was about to pounce on nothing.
However, when the Insect Tapir jumped up, a big black hand appeared out of nowhere and grabbed the Insect Tapir in the air with a gentle lift. At the same time, the "crying face" suspended in the air said gloomily, "I can kill other people, but he can't.
No way … "
After the ferocious Insect Tapir was lifted up by the big hand, it curled up like a well-behaved kitten.
The big hand tossed the Insect Tapir gently, drawing a parabola, it fell into another part of the immortal picture scroll again, and there were two Nirvana Realm creatures in this place!
"Woo …"
The black Insect Tapir showed its fangs again.
When Luo Zheng turned back, he ran in another direction.
He had fooled the Insect Tapir …

Chapter 3396

The Underworld.

Chapter 3398

Chapter 3399
After the other Nirvana creatures entered the labyrinth without hesitation, Luo Zheng, Fei Yu, and Kuai Jian also entered.

Land.

Inside.

On both sides of the thin line, there was something in the shape of an arc. It was unknown what it was, but it was stuck to the wall.

This was the dangerous part of this path. As long as they passed through this section, the rest of the path would be easier.
"Understood." Luo Zheng nodded.
After the three of them prepared at the intersection, they suddenly sped along the crooked path.
Luo Zheng changed his way of moving forward. His speed wasn't slower than Fei Yu and Kuai Jian's. The three of them seemed to be swimming in a river, sprinting upwards.
Just as they entered the winding tunnel, the arc-shaped objects on both sides of the tunnel suddenly broke apart. Insects as thick as an arm crawled out from the cracks. These insects seemed to have a pair of wings, and they flapped their wings as they charged straight at them.
It turned out that the circular objects were eggs, and these disgusting insects were hidden in the eggs …
As Luo Zheng followed closely behind, he didn't forget to observe the beasts in the rolls.
"Brother Luo Zheng, follow closely. Don't fight with the poisonous leeches. They're almost endless!" Fei Yu didn't forget to remind Luo Zheng as he rushed to the front.
The poisonous leeches themselves weren't very strong, but there were many of them and they were extremely poisonous. Therefore, most Nirvana creatures weren't willing to take this path.
But at this moment, Kuai Jian suddenly exclaimed, "Fei Yu, there's something wrong with the eggs in front of us!"
Fei Yu's expression changed when he saw what was in front of him.
There was a faint layer of black on the surface of the eggs in front of them.
These white eggs were ordinary poisonous leeches. The black layer of poisonous leeches on the surface was called Cold Poison Leeches. These Cold Poison Leeches were much stronger than ordinary poisonous leeches.
In fact, there had always been Cold Poison Leeches on this path, but there were only one or two at the end of the path.
Although the Cold Poison Leeches were difficult to deal with, it wasn't a big problem as long as they ran fast enough.
But now, they had only traveled half the way and encountered so many Cold Poison Leeches. This was something Fei Yu didn't expect.
"We can only rush over now!" Fei Yu clenched his teeth and said.
Behind them, the poisonous leeches that were flapping their wings blotted out the sky and covered the earth. They had practically blocked off all paths of retreat. It was impossible to retreat even if they wanted to.
Now, they had no other choice but to grit their teeth and rush out.
When the black eggs cracked, they were accompanied by a cold chill.
Luo Zheng, Fei Yu, and the others felt the temperature around them drop a lot. From the eggs, black Cold Poison Leeches flew out and surrounded them from both sides.
Fei Yu and Kuai Jian walked at the front and suddenly stretched out their bodies, dodging this batch of Cold Poison Leeches.
Luo Zheng brought up the rear and was blocked by these Cold Poison Leeches.

Chapter 3400
Luo Zheng had long heard of the Ji Chou Civilization.
This civilization advocated using a different method to complete the Dao of Finality.
They seemed to have completely abandoned the idea of relying on the Paramita. Instead, they used their understanding of space, time, and energy to complete the Dao of Finality.
It was said that this civilization had once almost completed the Dao of Finality. But the majority of civilizations did not believe it. Furthermore, the Ji Chou Civilization did not leave many things behind in the long river of history. That rumor of almost completing the Dao of Finality was not widely spread.
Even so, it was caught by Heavenly Venerable of Primeval Origin. At the same time, he stuffed the information regarding the energy axis into the Six Vein Blood Steadying Tree. In the end, Li Shan chose to let Luo Yan nurture the energy axis.
Hence, Luo Yan had once used the energy axis in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
But that was merely the embryonic form of the energy axis. The true energy axis could complete the transformation of time, space, and energy. Its power was unimaginable!
Phoenix and Li Bianxue did not have much of a reaction towards the green figure.
Li Bianxue had never heard of the "Ji Chou Civilization" and the Tian Zhi Civilization. Phoenix had heard of them before, but she did not feel that this civilization was very formidable. She was only curious. How could this unknown civilization survive two Primal Chaos eras? How could they construct their temple here?
"A civilization can only survive one Primal Chaos Epoch. How could you survive two?" Luo Zheng asked.
This question was asked by Heavenly Venerable of Primeval Origin to Luo Zheng.
Heavenly Venerable of Primeval Origin had paid a huge price to spread the Human Race. He only succeeded when the Paramita was crossing Primal Chaos Epochs.
This Tian Zhi Civilization actually became a Ruler civilization in two Primal Chaos Epochs. This had never happened before!
"Because we found the method of inheritance." The green figure answered, "Our Ji Chou Civilization is different from all your civilizations …"
As the green figure spoke, many insects hatched on the wall in front of Luo Zheng.
The abdomen of these insects would emit light like fireflies. Furthermore, the light emitted could be controlled freely. Hence, it was always flickering.
"The main body of our Ji Chou Civilization is them." The green figure said.
Luo Zheng nodded. His expression did not change.
A civilization formed by insects. This was very common.
At the very least, the green jade civilization and the metal civilization were rarer than the insect civilization.
"In our civilization, the males can release blue light, and the females can release green light …"
Countless neatly arranged bugs appeared on the screen.
A female bug and a male bug formed a pair, and they had red and green lights. Countless bugs formed pairs, and they continuously flashed with red and blue lights.
"What the hell is this?" Luo Zheng looked at the red and green lights with a puzzled expression.
Phoenix and Li Bianxue were more confused than Luo Zheng.
"This is a method of calculation." The green figure explained, "You humans have ten fingers, so when you calculate, you go from ten to one. However, we use red and green to calculate, so we go from two to one …"
Luo Zheng was a little confused, but the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi had already appeared above Luo Zheng's head. His eyes flashed as he asked, "What are the benefits of doing so?"
"Benefits? The advantage is that we have unparalleled computing power … "
As the green figure spoke, the image on the wall began to rise.
Thousands, millions, hundreds of millions …
Countless red and blue bugs were arranged together, and the lights of the two colors were constantly flashing. They converged the results of their calculations through a unique communication method …
"This method of calculation is very special. How powerful is it?" Yuanshi Tianzun asked excitedly. He looked like a student who was diligent in his studies.
"It can easily decipher Sanskrit," the green figure replied.
Hearing this, the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi's expression changed slightly.
For so many years, Luo Nian was the only one who could "decipher" Sanskrit. Luo Nian's ability to decipher Sanskrit seemed to be innate, or rather, he could naturally understand Sanskrit instead of decipher it.
However, the decipher method of the Ji-Chou civilization was completely different. They could rely on their terrifying computing power to decipher Sanskrit. Therefore, the secret of Sanskrit was not a secret to them at all.
Overall, the entire civilization relied on the deciphered Sanskrit to develop at a very fast speed!
"If that's the case, you can't be so unknown," the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi said.
Such a dominating civilization would definitely leave a mark on the Nirvana Realm, and not those illusory rumors.
Luo Zheng also nodded, agreeing with the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi's point of view.
"Haha …"
The green figure let out a faint laugh before saying, "We are too small. We are even smaller than the smallest mosquito in your world. It's very difficult for us to improve our individual strength."
Smaller than a mosquito, then it was really like a grain of sand …
It was very difficult for such a small individual to give birth to a powerful existence.
"Even if we are small, we still have the means of cultivation. Although our individual strength is not enough, our overall strength is not weak …"
Countless bugs appeared on the wall again.
These bugs combined together in a special way to form a whole body to fight against the ferocious beasts of the Chaos Era.
And their cultivation method was indeed unusual. They maintained a whole body to cultivate instead of being independent individuals …
This method was a bit like the situation of "9527" in the mind of the Chaos Ancient God. Every divided soul had its own division of labor, but when everyone combined together, they were a complete whole.
"Soon, our civilization became the dominating civilization of the entire Chaos. Like most dominating civilizations, we wanted to complete the Path of the End, so we moved step by step toward the upper levels of the Nirvana Realm until we saw the starry sky …"
Luo Zheng nodded subconsciously.
He and the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi both knew that it must be referring to the Mysterious World.
"What starry sky?" Li Bianxue asked quickly after seeing Luo Zheng nod. She felt more and more confused as she listened.
"It should be the starry sky of the 33 Levels of Heaven. It leads to the world outside the Chaos," Luo Zheng answered simply.
"You've been there? How do you know? You … "Li Bianxue threw out a series of questions.
Luo Zheng showed a helpless expression. "I'll tell you these questions later …"
"You can also understand the things in the starry sky through calculations?" the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi asked.

Chapter 3401
The Primeval Lord of Heaven looked surprised. "You've already done it?"
"Hum …"
Light and shadow appeared on the wall again. The scene constructed by the light and shadow seemed to be an extremely complicated process.
"Although the individuals of our civilization are small, they are not small enough," the green figure continued to explain. "After we process the sand, we can expand our computing power. It took us more than half of the Chaos Era to discover this method …"
The complicated process slowly turned into black boxes. These boxes were very similar to the Instruments of Civilization of the Tianzhi Civilization that Luo Zheng had fused with.
"Such a powerful thing is actually made of sand …" The Primeval Lord of Heaven exclaimed.
The green figure did not care about the Primeval Lord of Heaven's exclamation. It knew that their civilization was unique in terms of craftsmanship.
As the scene on the wall suddenly changed, it showed a world of ice and snow.
"This is the Yu Sheng-tian," Li Bianxue finally interjected.
After all, she had just walked all the way to the Yu Sheng-tian, so she knew everyone.
"Yes, if you came from the upper layer of the Ice Field, you should have seen those light spots. The light spots are distributed in the Cold Prison," the green figure said.
Luo Zheng nodded. "That should be something from the Xuanliang World …"
The scene on the wall quickly moved to the side of the "Cold Prison". Then, the black boxes were presented. After the green light on the boxes flashed, the green light outlined an illusory shape.
The light spots in the Cold Prison were determined through extremely fast calculations.
During the process of the scene on the wall, the green figure also introduced some strange words, such as algorithms and the like. This was probably a word unique to the Jiu Civilization.
Luo Zheng and the Primeval Lord of Heaven did not understand, but they roughly understood that the Jiu Civilization had indeed restored the things in the Xuanliang World through crazy calculations.
A strangely shaped monster was curled up in the Cold Prison.
The monster was imprisoned in the Cold Prison, but it did not seem to be completely dead. The scene showed that it was still blinking its eyes …
"The monsters in the Cold Prison, are they alive?" Phoenix blurted out.
She could not help but feel a trace of fear when she thought of being stared at by these strangely shaped monsters when she passed by the Cold Prison.
"Yes, most of them are alive. Only a small number of them are dead," the green figure replied.
"Are the monsters in the Cold Prison part of the Instant Civilization?" Yuanshi Tianzun asked.
"No."
The green figure sighed. They were like the humans and other ambitious sovereign civilizations. They were tirelessly trying to find the original Instant Civilization. They wanted to find the meaning of their existence from the Instant Civilization.
"Since you can see through the world outside the Chaos, haven't you found the true way to the Path of Finality?" asked Yuanshi Tianzun.
"Of course we have. We have considered many methods, such as using those powerful Nirvana tokens," said the green figure.
At the mention of the Nirvana Realm token, the Heavenly Lord of the Primordial Beginning's eyes lit up. "We humans have collected the Nirvana Realm tokens from the 33 heavens, such as …"
The green figure listened to Honoured Lord of the Origin patiently. When he finished, the green figure smiled and said, "You must have failed, right?"
"Of course we failed. Otherwise, we wouldn't be standing here today," said Phoenix.
The green figure shook his head. "I didn't make this deduction. We have already calculated the power needed to resist that needle."
Honoured Lord of the Origin's eyes widened. "Do you know how much power is needed?"
"Yes," continued the green figure. "It needs about 161 million Godly Jun's power …"
Hearing this answer, Luo Zheng, Li Bianxue, Phoenix, and Honoured Lord of the Origin all gasped.
The power of Phoenix and Luo Zheng was still hovering around tens of thousands of Godly Jun's power …
If hundreds of thousands of Godly Jun's power were released, it would be an extremely shocking number.
More than 161 million Godly Jun's power …
What kind of terrifying power was that …
It was hard to imagine even by association.
"Impossible." The Primeval Lord of Heaven shook his head like a rattle-drum. "The Human Ancestor sacrificed himself using the strongest Path of the End to release the power of more than nine million Godly Jun, but he was just a little bit away from completing the End …"
Luo Zheng was also inclined to believe Honoured Lord of the Origin.
After all, from the time he met Prince Ashoka to the Nirvana Realm Human Clan, all he knew was that the Human Clan had almost completed the Path of the End.
This 9 million Godly Jun's power was indeed terrifying, but compared to 160 million Godly Jun's power, it was still an order of magnitude lower. This number was really too exaggerated.
The green figure didn't care about Honoured Lord of the Origin's reaction. Instead, he continued, "We collected a lot of information about the 33 Heavens and arranged all the possible tokens of the Nirvana Realm. We calculated that the greatest power that could be obtained was 9.73 million Godly Jun's power.
Is there any difference between the power that your Human Ancestor released and our calculation? "
"…"
Honoured Lord of the Origin was stunned.
That pair of wise and farsighted eyes that seemed to know everything flashed with a trace of dullness.
Frankly speaking, when they knew that the Ji Chou Civilization was a bunch of bugs, Honoured Lord of the Origin and Luo Zheng more or less looked down on them. This kind of disdain came from human nature. What could a tiny bug do?
But now, Honoured Lord of the Origin understood that these bugs had indeed developed a powerful civilization on their own.
There was nothing that they didn't calculate and master!
Honoured Lord of the Origin still remembered the moment before the Human Clan sacrificed themselves, their faint voice spread throughout the entire galaxy.
"Today, I will use all my power and burn all the tokens. I will offer a terrifying power of 9.73 million Godly Jun's power …"
The Ji Chou Civilization had never obtained those tokens of the Nirvana Realm, but they had calculated so accurately. This indeed shocked Honoured Lord of the Origin.
"The things left behind in the Nirvana Realm seem mysterious, but they are actually illusory traps. They let the civilizations fall into them one after another, thinking that they can complete the Path of the End," said the green figure indifferently. "But no matter how you arrange and combine them, the optimal solution is none.
Complete the Path of the End! "
Honoured Lord of the Origin's face also showed that he was convinced. But when he turned his face, there was hope in his eyes. "Then what means can be used to break it?"
"The only way to break the Path of the End is the Energy Axis. The Energy Axis can transform into endless power," answered the Ji Chou Civilization.

Chapter 3402
After the Ground Shaker Clan member was swallowed by the black snake, there was an uproar in the tunnel, and the few Nirvana Realm creatures at the front stepped back.
"It's Otaki Otaki!"
"It can't be …"
"I knew it wouldn't be so simple this time, but why would Otaki Otaki appear?"
After the Nirvana Realm creatures saw the black snake, their faces were filled with despair.
After so many Divine Epochs, many Nirvana Realm creatures had studied the Unbroken Labyrinth and the Immortal Painting Scroll more thoroughly.
Most of the Nirvana Realm creatures knew which path in the Unbroken Labyrinth would lead to what kind of creatures, their strength, and how to deal with them. For example, the path chosen by the Three-Eyed Clan had a large number of poisonous leeches, and the Purple Cloud Clan.
The path chosen by the Purple Cloud Clan had a large number of Demon Shadow Butterflies.
While …
At the end of the Unbroken Labyrinth, there were usually stronger creatures guarding it.
Although there weren't many of them, they were extremely powerful.
For example, the creature "Insect Tapir" that appeared on the first page would often appear at the end of the Unbroken Labyrinth.
Although the Insect Tapir was powerful and its whereabouts were unpredictable, there was usually only one Insect Tapir guarding the end of the Unbroken Labyrinth. Naturally, the tragedy on the first page wouldn't happen.
Other than the Insect Tapir, there were also creatures of different sizes such as "Poisonous Pincer Beast", "Scaled Ghost", "Evil Slayer", and other creatures that often appeared.
These creatures were extremely powerful, but there were ways to deal with them.
For example, due to the Insect Tapir's form, it couldn't enter the Hexagonal Star Formation room. The Poisonous Pincer Beast's attack was extremely terrifying, but its movement speed was slow …
But other than these common creatures, there were also some less common ones, such as the Ghost in the Wall, the Monkey on the Ground, and so on. When encountering these rare creatures, one could only pray for good luck.
The Ghost in the Wall and the Monkey on the Ground weren't the most powerful creatures. The most powerful and rare creature in the Immortal Painting Scroll was undoubtedly the Otaki Otaki in front of them.
In the recorded history, the number of times Oda Orochi appeared was very few, only five times.
And in these five records, none of them had reached the Immortal Realm.
When Otaki Otaki first appeared, the Nirvana Realm creatures that entered the Immortal Painting Scroll didn't know how powerful it was, and almost all of them were killed.
The second time, it was much better. Those Nirvana Realm creatures knew that they could not resist this thing, so they chose to give up. When the time was up, they would be kicked out of the immortal picture scroll.
The next few times were the same as the second time. Even if he was unlucky, he could choose to give up, at least he would be able to keep his life.
Therefore, when the living beings of the Nirvana Realm saw Xie Qi, they all sighed. Since they had met with something that they could not fight against, they had no other choice but to give up.
"Looks like Brother Luo Zheng's luck isn't good. It's his first time in the Immortal Picture Scroll and he already encountered this thing," Fei Yu said with a wry smile.
"Yes," Fei Yu replied with certainty. "None of those who tried survived."
"We can only wait here obediently until the next Immortal Painting opens," Kuai Jian said.
Not only the two Three-Eyed Race members, but the other Nirvana Realm creatures had also given up.
They had time to wait for the next opportunity. After all, they had met Xie Qi, and returning empty-handed was not something to be ashamed of.
Luo Zheng was not the kind of person who would back down when faced with a little difficulty. Although Fei Yu had said that the Orochi Xie Qi was undefeatable, Luo Zheng still asked Yuanshi Tianzun in his mind.
"The first time that the Three-Eyed Race kid mentioned actually happened in Chaos Era 83, which was the fifth Chaos Era after we humans entered Pingyi Heaven," Yuanshi Tianzun said. "Nine of us humans entered the Immortal Picture Scroll, and none of us died.
If I was in the Immortal Picture Scroll at that time, I'm afraid I wouldn't be able to return either. "
The humans were quite powerful in Pingyi Heaven at that time, so they naturally sent a lot of people into the Immortal Picture Scroll each time.
Even Yuanshi Tianzun had no chance of winning against Xie Qi. Luo Zheng also quickly made a choice. He gave up on the third page and waited for the Immortal Picture Scroll's time to end …
But just as the Nirvana Realm creatures chose to give up, a black shadow flashed under everyone's eyes.
Everyone was stunned for a moment before they reacted. Was that another big snake?
Because after turning into a picture, the angle of view was always looking down, so the end of the maze and the exit were above, but that big snake just flashed by from below.
"That's … another big snake?"
"Two, two big snakes?"
"That big snake won't come in, right …"
Someone's voice was already trembling.
Encountering the Orochi Xie Qi was already an extremely unlucky thing.
The Immortal Picture Scroll had opened and closed countless times, but Xie Qi had only appeared five times, and there was no record of two big snakes appearing at the same time!
Just as everyone was discussing in a trembling voice, the Xie Qi swept past from below while the other Xie Qi swept past from above. With one above and one below, all of the Nirvana Realm beings were blocked in the tunnel.
Fortunately, the two Xie Qi flashed by in an instant and disappeared quickly …
Just the shadow of the snake in an instant was enough to scare everyone.
"Do you think Xie Qi can enter this passage?" One of the Nirvana Realm creatures asked.
As soon as this question was asked, the pressure in everyone's hearts increased.
No one said that there was a safe zone in the Unending Maze, but those who were unwilling to rush out would stay in this passage and wait.
But was it really safe inside the passage?
"It, it should be safe …" Another Nirvana Realm creature answered.
"Yes, normally the Nirvana Realm creatures at the tail would not enter."
Kuai Jian affirmed.
"But that Xie Qi did not come from the tail," Fei Yu frowned and said.
Since there were so many anomalies in the Immortal Picture Scroll this time, even the most impossible things could happen. Moreover, who said that this passage was definitely safe?
Hearing Fei Yu's words, everyone's hearts were shrouded in a layer of ominous clouds. Wu Dang was even more dissatisfied and scolded, "You jinx!"
Fei Yu was also unhappy with the Purple Cloud Clan, and replied coldly, "We said it ourselves, it's none of your business!"
"You!"
Just as Wu Dang was about to retort, the Xie Qi below once again swept across horizontally. This time, when it was halfway across, it suddenly dived into the tunnel. The thing that the Nirvana Realm beings were most worried about had happened.
These Nirvana Realm creatures were originally very vigilant, and the moment they saw the Xie Qi enter the passage, they all rushed upwards.

Chapter 3403
The first to rush out were the three Nirvana Realm creatures from the Fenglan Clan.
The Fenglan Clan was also an ancient Nirvana Realm tribe. Although they had submitted to the Elemental Spirit Civilization, their status was much higher than the Purple Cloud Clan. The Immortal Realm experts under the clan leader had a certain level of influence within the Elemental Spirit Civilization.
This time, the Fenglan Clan was luckier. They sent four people into the Immortal Scroll, but only one of them died on the first page.
When the second snake, Xie Qi, appeared, the three Fenglan Clan members predicted that Xie Qi would rush up through the tunnel. The three Fenglan Clan members quietly moved along the tunnel.
Therefore, when the Xie Qi below them made a move, the three Fenglan Tribe members immediately rushed out.
"Rush out as fast as you can!"
"As long as we get out of the Endless Labyrinth, all the dangers will vanish!"
"…"
The three Fenglan Clan members had a strong desire to survive.
They did not know if they could successfully rush out of the Endless Labyrinth. They only knew that if they stayed, they would definitely die!
In order to survive, they could only gamble on that one in ten thousand chance!
But in the next moment, the three Fenglan Clan members fell into despair. The giant black snake that was hiding at the back of the tunnel suddenly swept over and swallowed the three of them with its bloody mouth.
There were no screams or bloody scenes. This was just a scene, but it exuded an indescribable fear.
At the same time, three spots of bloody light landed on the three Sanqing Stones in Pingyi Heaven. That area was the territory of the Fenglan Clan members. The Fenglan Clan members who were waiting outside immediately roared in anger.
This time, all the big clans had suffered heavy losses. Only the Fenglan Clan had lost one person. The Fenglan Clan members felt a sense of relief. Who would have thought that three of them would die at the same time?
The hearts of the Nirvana Realm creatures in the tunnel were filled with fear. There was a blockade in front and a pursuer behind. This was a dead end …
But under such circumstances, they did not have time to think.
The tunnel was not long. The giant snake, Xie Qi, would soon come up.
Even if the three Fenglan Clan members were swallowed by Xie Qi, they had no choice but to continue rushing up!
After the Fenglan people, another four Nirvana Realm creatures rushed out. These four Nirvana Realm creatures were luckier than the others. Perhaps the giant snake Xie Qi had just swallowed three Fenglan people and didn't have the time to turn back.
…
Seeing that the four creatures of the Nirvana Realm who rushed out were fine, the other creatures of the Nirvana Realm, including Luo Zheng, rushed out immediately!
In the silent scrolls, the creatures that had turned into paintings were still running wildly on the scrolls!
The end of the Unbroken Labyrinth was a series of intersecting passageways that looked like a magnified nine-square grid. After the Nirvana Realm creatures crossed the intersecting passageway, Luo Zheng immediately turned into the next parallel passageway!
Just as Luo Zheng and the others turned in, the Xie Qi at the bottom had already rushed up.
Another two Nirvana Realm beings couldn't dodge in time and were swallowed by Xie Qi.
"So miserable …"
The two Nirvana Realm creatures that were swallowed were extremely close to him. Kuai Jian's face turned pale as he watched them being swallowed. From his perspective, Kuai Jian could also see his own face that was as white as a sheet of paper …
In contrast, Luo Zheng and Fei Yu were much calmer.
Needless to say, Luo Zheng had experienced many life and death crises that these people couldn't compare to.
Fei Yu even relied on his own ability to take charge in the Thirteen Levels of Heaven. Those experiences in danger weren't in vain.
"The way Xie Qi moves is very strange," Luo Zheng analyzed while sticking close to the passageway.
"Yes, they seem to only be good at moving in a straight line," another creature of the Nirvana Realm said.
This creature of the Nirvana Realm was also sticking close to the wall. She was small and had a pair of pointy ears on top of her head. She looked like a half-human half-fox. As she was in the form of a painting, the complete details were barely visible.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng subconsciously sized up this' fox '.
In a panic, most people only thought about how scary Xie Qi was and how they should escape. However, this fox seemed to have the same judgment as him.
"That should be the case," Luo Zheng nodded.
The Xie Qi at the bottom made a turn and went into the passageway that everyone was hiding in. However, the Xie Qi's turning speed was very slow!
If the Xie Qi's turning speed was also very fast, everyone including Luo Zheng would have been wiped out long ago.
"If the Xie Qi's turning speed is slow, then it shouldn't be difficult to deal with it, right?"
Fei Yu's mind was also very fast. He felt a glimmer of hope for survival and immediately became excited.
At the end of the Unbroken Labyrinth, this space was a huge nine-square grid. Within the grid, there were many hexagonal star array rooms.
Under such a layout, there were corners in all directions.
The Xie Qi's speed was unimaginably fast. Even the fastest Ziyun Clan members couldn't possibly dodge it. However, if they were slow in turning, the threat to the Nirvana Realm creatures would be greatly reduced.
At this moment, the Xie Qi that was rushing forward turned back again. Its speed was still very fast.
Just as this Xie Qi rushed towards Luo Zheng, its speed suddenly became extremely slow. It slowly turned its head and turned towards the passageway that Luo Zheng and the others were in!
"It, it's turning around!" A Nirvana Realm creature said while trembling.
The Xie Qi's slow turn made it very close to the Nirvana Realm creatures that were hiding at the side.
"We'll follow it," Luo Zheng said calmly.
After that, Luo Zheng, Fei Yu and the rest of the Nirvana Realm creatures turned from the horizontal passageway to the vertical passageway.
The Xie Qi had just turned into the horizontal passageway when it realized that all its prey had turned into the vertical passageway. It was obviously stunned. Then, it could only slowly turn its head and follow them.
However, the Xie Qi's slow speed wasn't much of a threat.
The Nirvana Realm creatures kept turning from the horizontal passageway to the vertical passageway, then from the vertical passageway to the horizontal passageway, slowly moving upwards.
Their speed wasn't fast, but as they moved, step by step, they moved towards the exit of the Unbroken Labyrinth. The situation gradually became clear.
"Looks like this Xie Qi isn't as evil as the legends say …" A Nirvana Realm creature said in disdain.
"It doesn't know how to turn. What's so scary about it, hehe," Another Nirvana Realm creature laughed.
If it was so easy to break through, why did the Xie Qi stumble so many Nirvana Realm creatures?
Luo Zheng didn't say anything. He felt that something wasn't right.
If the Xie Qi was so easy to deal with, it wouldn't be so evil. After all, those who could enter the Immortal Picture Scroll were all people who could reach the Immortal Realm. None of them were fools.

Chapter 3404
These Paramita Lifeforms were fully focused on the first strand of Xie Qi when they were switching between the vertical and horizontal paths. They had neglected the second strand of Xie Qi.
And the second strand of Xie Qi was slowly rotating further away. Just as these Paramita Lifeforms were about to switch to the horizontal path, they coincidentally clashed with the second strand of Xie Qi!
A small portion of the Paramita Lifeforms were stunned!
"Rush over!"
Luo Zheng roared out. Both his hands pushed upwards, causing his entire body to move to the side. This move of his had used all his strength.
Now, there was no path of retreat. Other than charging forward, there was no other choice.
Luo Zheng was not the only one who made such a decision. Those Paramita Lifeforms who reacted swiftly also charged forward. When facing death, the explosive strength of these Paramita Lifeforms could not be underestimated.
And just as they charged out, the second strand of Xie Qi pierced through!
That black snake figure was a silent death charge!
These Paramita Lifeforms were all glaring at the Xie Qi as they charged forward. It was as though they could slow it down by glaring at it.
The dozen or so Paramita Lifeforms at the front charged over. Fei Yu, Kuai Jian and the others who were in the middle barely managed to charge over. Luo Zheng followed closely behind Kuai Jian, though he was slower by half a body's length.
At the next moment, Luo Zheng felt a terrifying force crashing onto his body. He was not swallowed by the Xie Qi. Instead, he was struck by the flying Xie Qi from the side. An enormous and terrifying force was exerted on Luo Zheng's body, causing him to spin like a top.
He spun rapidly in the passageway and only stopped after a long while.
When Luo Zheng stopped, he took a look at his own body and a look of horror appeared on his face.
Strands of intense pain spread out from his limbs and bones. The surface of that painting of his was filled with cracks. Clearly, his injuries were not light!
Since his body was injured, his Chaotic Origin Spirit naturally became fatigued. That also meant that the force of that collision had exceeded 100,000 divine jun …
But compared to the Paramita Lifeforms behind Luo Zheng, he was considered much luckier.
Apart from Luo Zheng, two other creatures from the Nirvana Realm were also hit.
Although the two Nirvana Realm creatures had also cultivated the Body of Eternity, their bodies were directly shattered by the terrifying collision. Broken limbs and blood flowed all over the ground …
However, what was presented in the scroll were all pictures, and it didn't look that bloody.
Luo Zheng and the rest were sent flying. The Nirvana Realm creature behind them was swallowed in a single gulp. Xie Qi's speed was too fast and he didn't even have time to scream.
"Brother, Luo Zheng, are you okay?" Fei Yu, who was not far away, asked.
Luo Zheng suddenly felt that the atmosphere was a little strange, as if all the Nirvana creatures were looking at him.
"I'm a little injured. Fortunately, I'm fine," Luo Zheng replied in a relaxed manner.
'What do you mean by a little injury …'
The Nirvana Realm beings present were all dumbfounded.
The creatures of the Nirvana Realm who could enter the Immortal Picture Scroll must have cultivated the Body of Eternity.
Since everyone had the Body of Eternity, the amount of God Jun Energy they could withstand should be about the same …
Even if I can only withstand 70,000 to 80,000 God Jun Energy, and you, Luo Zheng, are slightly stronger and have more Calamity Bone Inscriptions, allowing you to withstand 100,000 God Jun Energy, the difference shouldn't be so huge …
Of the two Nirvana Realm beings beside Luo Zheng, one was from the Inverse Refinement Race and the other was from the Iron Vein Race. These two Nirvana Realm beings were known for their powerful fleshy bodies. After cultivating the Body of Eternity, their fleshy bodies were much stronger than ordinary Body of Eternity.
But after that collision, how much did it shatter?
They found it inconceivable that Luo Zheng's body had only some cracks …
"It's good that you are fine. Let's continue moving upwards." Fei Yu let out a sigh of relief.
Luo Zheng's fleshy body was strong was his own secret. He naturally wouldn't ask about it.
The other Nirvana Realm beings also understood that Luo Zheng wouldn't answer or ask about it. But they still discussed it in private.
"I've heard that being able to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm in the Primal Chaos World is a unique method to strengthen the fleshy body."
"I've long heard of it. Even if we use a living being from the Sixteenth Heaven Chaotic Origin Sea, even if we cultivate that plaything to the limit, we can only withstand 10,000 to 20,000 God Jun Energy. It isn't worth mentioning."
"Then how did he withstand the collision of Xie Qi?"
"I don't know. If you want to know, you can ask him directly …"
The group moved through the horizontal and vertical passages time after time, slowly moving towards the exit of the Endless Maze.
The space at the end of the Endless Maze wasn't huge. As they got closer to the exit, the group gradually became excited.
Before this, no one had ever passed through the defense of the Great Serpent Xie Qi. But this group of people did it. This was something they could be proud of.
But including Luo Zheng, several Nirvana Realm beings were very cautious. Could it be that they could pass through the Endless Maze so easily?
"Go to the left. The second Xie Qi is on the other side!"
The half-human half-fox Nirvana Realm being at the front of the group directed the group in the direction.
Previously, they didn't pay attention to the second Xie Qi and suffered a huge loss. Now that everyone was paying extra attention, they naturally paid more attention.
"Upper right side!"
"Upper left side!"
"Upper right side …"
Under the reasonable guidance of the fox, the group continued moving along the slots. The two Xie Qi could only slowly turn their heads and could not accelerate.
"Upper right side!"
"Upper right side again!"
"Upper right side again!"
That fox continued to direct the group. No one discovered that the corner of the fox's mouth curled up, revealing a mocking smile.
"Yi?"
At this moment, someone in the group suddenly felt that something was amiss, "If we keep moving towards the upper right side, wouldn't we be getting further away from the exit of the Endless Maze?"
The exit of the Endless Maze was located at the center of the end. And they were moving towards the upper right side in a staircase. They were indeed getting further away from the exit.
"How are you leading the way!"
"Isn't it better to change the direction?"
"…"
Just as everyone was reprimanding that fox, the fox suddenly stopped. Following which, it let out a bell-like laughter, "You guys are really too slow for not noticing it …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng, Fei Yu and the rest frowned.
At the same time, the two Xie Qi blocked them from the left and right sides. These two Xie Qi's body went around a few paths and completely blocked them at the upper right side.
This' fox 'deliberately led them to a dead end.
"You … why did you harm us!"
"Why did you do this!"
"You led us to a dead end. You will die too!"
When the Nirvana Realm beings saw the two Xie Qi blocking their path, their faces turned pale. At the same time, their eyes were filled with deep confusion.
They were all Nirvana Realm beings. Why did they lead them to a dead end?
"Is there a half-human half-fox Nirvana Realm race in Ping Yi Heaven?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
